### [1] [Reconstruct, Practice, Go Real: Guided Self-Improvement for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02204)
*Yen-Jen Wang,Haozhe Jiang,Shuying Deng,Haoru Xue,Weirui Ye,Rocky Duan,Nika Haghtalab,S. Shankar Sastry,Pieter Abbeel,Haozhi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RPG通过仿真练习、失败诊断与技能迭代，在不更新模型权重的情况下持续提升机器人操作能力；其在仿真测试中达到95.0%的成功率，并在30次实体机器人试验中全部成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可靠且适用于多种任务的机器人能力，通常需要大量人工投入来开发和维护技能、设计奖励，并协调感知与控制。

Method: RPG从离线数据集中识别操作能力，并在仿真中生成相关练习任务。练习过程中，系统结合执行反馈、仿真器特权状态和可用视频诊断失败，进而创建可复用的符号技能、改进现有技能并修订系统提示。候选改动及其合并版本均经跨任务评估后才保留。测试时，多模态大语言模型利用更新后的提示和技能库协调感知与控制，全程不更新模型权重。

Result: 在22项操作任务的未见初始化条件下，成功率从第一轮练习后的28.6%提升至第15轮后的95.0%，高于所有评估基线，包括ASPIRE（75.5%）和由GPT-6 Astra Pro驱动的CaP-Agent0（60.0%）。经过统一校准和硬件适配后，冻结系统在实体机器人上的30次试验全部成功，三项任务各进行10次。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于诊断的仿真练习和可复用技能迭代，有望在不调整模型权重的前提下显著提升机器人跨任务执行能力，并迁移至实体机器人。不过，实体试验仅覆盖三项任务、共30次，适用范围仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Building reliable robot capabilities across diverse tasks requires substantial human effort to develop and maintain skills, design rewards, and integrate perception with control. We present Reconstruct, Practice, Go Real (RPG), a framework for autonomous improvement of robot execution systems without updating model weights. RPG identifies manipulation capabilities in an offline dataset and constructs related practice tasks in simulation. During practice, RPG uses execution feedback, privileged simulator state, and available dataset videos to diagnose failures. It develops new reusable symbolic skills, refines existing skills, and revises the system prompt based on these diagnoses. Cross-task evaluation tests individual candidate changes and merged revisions before they are retained for reuse. At test time, a multimodal LLM uses the resulting system prompt and skill library to coordinate perception and robot control. On held-out initializations of 22 manipulation tasks, RPG improves task success from 28.6% after the first practice round to 95.0% after 15 rounds, outperforming all evaluated baselines, including ASPIRE (75.5%) and CaP-Agent0 powered by GPT-6 Astra Pro (60.0%). After a common calibration and hardware-adaptation procedure, the frozen system succeeds in all 30 physical trials, with ten trials on each of three tasks. Project Website: this https URL

</details>


### [2] [Watch, Infer, Coordinate: Inferring Robot Partner Constraints for Zero-Shot Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02170)
*Suyu Ye,Zheyuan Zhang,Vaishnav Tadiparthi,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Ehsan Moradi Pari,Tianmin Shu,Homanga Bharadhwaj,Nakul Agarwal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人如何通过观察团队协作行为，推断伙伴的物理能力限制，并将推断结果用于新任务中的零样本协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件退化或执行器故障会限制机器人的可靠行动，而伙伴可能并不知情。单看受限机器人的行为难以判断其能力，因为演示只呈现了它实际执行的动作，未揭示它本可执行的动作；协作伙伴的补偿行为也会掩盖这些限制。

Method: 提出 Watch, Infer, Coordinate 基准，覆盖三类物理耦合的操作任务；并设计一种约束推断方法，根据观测到的双方联合行为，为候选能力限制评分。其核心思路是，限制会影响整个团队的行为，因此双方动作都能提供有关受限机器人的信息。

Result: 在三类任务中，该方法均显著提升了能力约束推断的准确性和零样本协作表现，结果接近已知真实约束的预言机基线。

Conclusion: 利用团队联合行为推断伙伴的隐含物理限制，有助于机器人在缺少直接能力信息时更有效地协作，并可推广至不同的物理耦合操作场景。

Abstract: Robots operating in the physical world will increasingly need to coordinate with other robots, particularly in manipulation tasks where an object may be too large or heavy for a single robot to carry alone. Physical limitations caused by hardware degradation or actuator faults can restrict the actions a robot can reliably execute, yet these limitations may be unknown to its partner. We study whether a helper can infer a robot partner's physical constraints from observing it coordinate with another robot, then use the inferred capability to coordinate with the same partner on a new task. This is difficult because a demonstration shows what the constrained robot did, but not what it could have done. In physically coupled tasks, the other robot may also compensate for its limitations, making those limitations difficult to identify from the constrained robot's behavior alone. Our key insight is that these constraints shape the joint behavior of the team, making the actions of both robots informative about the constrained partner's capability. We introduce Watch, Infer, Coordinate, a benchmark spanning three physically coupled manipulation settings, together with an inference approach that scores candidate constraints using observed joint behavior. Across all three settings, our method substantially improves constraint inference and zero-shot coordination, approaching an oracle with access to the true constraints.

</details>


### [3] [DuoMind: Enabling Distributed Multi-Robot Coordination with Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02161)
*Hanchu Zhou,Dechen Gao,Hang Wang,Brendan Lynch,Boqi Zhao,Qiyao Ma,Raman Goyal,Junshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DuoMind 是一种面向多机器人协作的分布式分层框架，通过语义通信结合 VLM 高层协调与 VLA 低层执行，并在 RoboPoly 和 RoboTwin 上取得更好的任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型与视觉语言动作模型的进展主要集中于单机器人场景。多机器人系统仍面临长时序行为协调与精细可靠执行的挑战，同时相关评测基准不足。

Method: 每个机器人由 VLM 编排器和 VLA 动作模型组成。编排器依据任务指令、本地观测及其他机器人发送的消息进行推理，生成低层执行指令和供协作机器人使用的语义消息；VLA 动作模型负责低层动作执行。研究还构建了 RoboPoly 基准，涵盖需要分布式闭环协作的长时序操作任务。

Result: 在 RoboPoly 和 RoboTwin 上的实验表明，DuoMind 提升了多机器人任务表现；消融实验验证了分层编排和语义通信的作用。

Conclusion: 将 VLM 的语义推理与协调能力和 VLA 的动作生成能力结合，有助于提升多机器人长时序协作表现；RoboPoly 为此类系统提供了新的评测基准。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) and vision-language-action models (VLAs) have recently driven rapid progress in general-purpose robots, yet most progress has focused on single-robot settings. Extending these capabilities to multi-robot systems remains challenging because robots must coordinate long-horizon behaviors while maintaining reliable, fine-grained execution. We introduce DuoMind, a distributed hierarchical framework for multi-robot coordination through semantic communication. Each robot uses a VLA-based action model for low-level execution and a VLM-based orchestrator for high-level reasoning and inter-agent coordination. At each planning step, the orchestrator at each robot reasons over the task instruction, local observations, and messages received from other robots. It then generates low-level instructions for the action model and semantic messages for peer robots. This architecture exploits the complementary strengths of pretrained models by combining the semantic reasoning capabilities of VLMs with the precise action-generation capabilities of VLAs. To address the scarcity of benchmarks for multi-robot coordination, we further develop RoboPoly, a benchmark comprising long-horizon manipulation tasks that require coordinated, closed-loop execution under distributed control. Experiments on RoboPoly and RoboTwin demonstrate that DuoMind improves multi-robot task performance, while ablation studies confirm the contributions of hierarchical orchestration and semantic communication. More details are available on our project page.

</details>


### [4] [SkeleWAM: Skeleton World-Action Modeling for Efficient Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02120)
*Juyi Sheng,Hua Wang,Mengyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkeleWAM以稀疏三维骨架表示机器人与物体状态，用于动作生成和未来状态预测，在LIBERO-Plus上取得85.9%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型多预测视频或视觉潜变量，交互几何表达隐式，且可能保留与控制无关的外观信息。

Method: 从RGB-D观测和机器人本体感知在线构建由机器人关节、物体中心及交互点组成的三维骨架。模型依据当前骨架和语言指令生成动作，并预测未来骨架以提供几何监督；Medoid Action Consensus（MAC）作为随机动作样本的辅助共识策略。

Result: SkeleWAM使用57.1M个参数，在LIBERO-Plus上总体成功率为85.9%，比Cosmos-Policy高3.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 稀疏三维机器人—物体结构可作为有效的状态表示，支持稳健且参数高效的世界动作学习。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) combine robot action generation with future state prediction. Existing WAMs typically predict videos or learned visual latents, which represent interaction geometry only implicitly and may retain appearance information unrelated to control. We introduce SkeleWAM, a compact WAM that represents a manipulation scene as a sparse 3D skeleton composed of robot joints, object centers, and interaction points. Constructed online from current RGB-D observations and robot proprioception, the skeleton provides a unified geometric state for action generation and future skeleton prediction. Future skeleton prediction provides additional geometric supervision for action learning without requiring visual reconstruction. At inference, SkeleWAM generates actions directly from the current skeleton and language instruction, while Medoid Action Consensus (MAC) serves as an auxiliary consensus strategy for stochastic action samples. On LIBERO-Plus, SkeleWAM achieves an overall success rate of 85.9% with 57.1M parameters, outperforming Cosmos-Policy by 3.7 percentage points. These results demonstrate that sparse 3D robot--object structure provides an effective state space for robust and parameter-efficient world action learning. The project is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [5] [GlassGuard: Verified Glass Plane Mapping for Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02110)
*Hanwen Guo,Zhengzhi Lin,Yusen Xie,Ji Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GlassGuard融合视觉与LiDAR证据重建建筑玻璃平面，在提高玻璃覆盖率的同时减少对可通行空间的误标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR可能穿透玻璃，导致地图缺失碰撞边界；但重建位置不准又会把可通行空间误标为障碍。因此，导航地图需要兼顾玻璃覆盖率与自由空间污染。

Method: 利用基础视觉模型生成玻璃实例掩码，以结构化三维线索提出具有度量尺度的平面假设，并通过无需深度信息的二维投影几何验证平面朝向；随后将通过验证的结果整合到全局地图中。

Result: 在涵盖九个建筑场景、超过一小时及2.1公里真实机器人行驶的数据上，广角版本实现85%的玻璃总覆盖率。同等针孔输入下，GlassGuard覆盖率为82%，高于所测基线最高61%；每帧假阳性体素减少5至17倍。定性结果显示，重建平面可阻止规划路径穿过玻璃，同时保留可通行路线。

Conclusion: GlassGuard将玻璃覆盖与自由空间保护共同纳入重建流程，在多样建筑环境中提升了导航地图的可靠性；现有摘要未报告定量导航成功率。

Abstract: Transparent and specular surfaces pose a serious challenge to LiDAR-based SLAM and navigation because laser returns may pass through glass, leaving collision boundaries absent from the map. Prior work attempts to reconstruct the missing surfaces, but inaccurate obstacle placement can create the opposite failure: contamination of traversable free space. Recognizing this dual requirement, we present GlassGuard, a navigation-oriented framework for reconstructing planar architectural glass from complementary visual and LiDAR evidence. We formulate success in terms of both glass coverage and free-space contamination and apply this principle throughout proposal verification and global map construction. A foundation vision model provides glass-instance masks, structural 3D cues generate metric plane hypotheses, and depth-free 2D projective geometry checks their orientations before they enter a consolidated global map. We evaluate GlassGuard in nine building-scale scenes spanning diverse glass structures, spatial scales, and lighting conditions, with more than one hour and 2.1 km of real-world robot traversal. GlassGuard achieves 85% of total glass coverage for its panoramic version. Under identical pinhole inputs, GlassGuard achieves 82% total coverage, compared with at most 61% for the evaluated baselines, while producing 5-17x fewer false voxels per frame. Qualitative examples with a navigation planner illustrate the reconstructed planes blocking paths through glass while leaving traversable routes open. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [6] [HumanoidToolBench: Benchmarking Humanoid Tool Use from Selection to Mobile Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02089)
*Kyochul Jang,Seohyeon Park,Ohchul Kwon,Sangjun Park,Junhyeok Choi,Seungyeop Yi,Chaeyun Kim,Sangkyu Lee,Idan Szpektor,Avi Caciularu,Jongmin Park,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 HumanoidToolBench 与 ToolBook，用于评估人形机器人选择工具并通过操作及必要时移动完成任务的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未能在同一人形机器人平台上联合评估工具选择、操作与移动，而这些能力对超越机器人自身物理限制至关重要。

Method: 构建包含18项任务的 HumanoidToolBench，覆盖三种场景、三个执行等级和两种工具集模式；另收集包含3.1千条仿真及 Unitree G1 实机演示的 ToolBook。评估了7种仿真策略和3种实机策略，并对 GR00T N1.7 进行专项测试。

Result: 实验显示，选择合适工具与成功完成任务之间存在显著差距。GR00T N1.7 在面对未见工具时选择准确率下降，且可能在收到无关指令后仍继续执行任务。

Conclusion: 该基准与数据集为系统评估人形机器人的工具使用能力提供了资源，也揭示了工具选择泛化和指令遵循方面的不足。

Abstract: As robotic hardware and learning methods advance, humanoids need tools to perform tasks beyond their inherent physical limits. Successful tool use requires selecting a suitable tool and coordinating manipulation and, when needed, locomotion to complete the task. Existing benchmarks do not jointly evaluate these capabilities on a humanoid. We introduce HumanoidToolBench, an 18-task benchmark spanning three scenarios, three execution levels, and two tool-set modes, together with ToolBook, a dataset of 3.1k demonstrations collected in simulation and on a real Unitree G1. Evaluation of seven policies in simulation and three on the real robot reveals substantial gaps between selecting a suitable tool and completing the task. Focused GR00T N1.7 probes show reduced selection accuracy on unseen tools and continued task execution under unrelated instructions. Code and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [7] [UniWAM: Unified World-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02054)
*Jiayi Chen,Wenxuan Song,Jingbo Wang,Shuai Zhou,Xicheng Gong,Zehua Fan,Ziyang Zhou,Junwu E,Haodong Yan,Fuhao Li,Qize Yu,Xu Huang,Pengwei Wang,Wen Chen,Shunbo Zhou,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniWAM 将物理推理、世界生成与动作预测整合于统一架构，并通过人类与机器人数据联合训练提升具身任务能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言动作模型缺乏对世界动态的充分 grounding；世界动作模型则在语义理解和分布偏移下的推理方面受限。研究旨在融合两者优势，统一学习物理语义、视觉生成与动作预测。

Method: 构建包含物理推理器、世界生成器和动作预测器的架构；通过严格的数据清洗与标注处理人类第一视角及机器人数据。将低层动作表示为自然语言，并按模型组件分配视觉问答、人类数据和机器人演示的互补监督。后训练采用未来视觉噪声增强及历史条件流匹配，以减少对精确未来预测的依赖并加速动作生成。

Result: UniWAM 在多项评测中取得先进表现，涵盖同分布性能、鲁棒性、泛化、指令遵循和长时程任务执行。研究还发现人机联合训练呈对数线性 scaling law。

Conclusion: 将语义推理、世界建模与动作生成统一，并扩大人类和机器人数据的联合预训练规模，是提升具身模型综合能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models benefit from the understanding and reasoning capabilities of pretrained vision-language models, but action-only supervision provides limited grounding in world dynamics. Conversely, world-action models inherit spatiotemporal priors from video generation models, yet remain limited in semantic understanding and reasoning under distribution shifts. We introduce UniWAM, a unified architecture that integrates a physical reasoner, a world generator, and an action predictor to jointly learn semantic understanding of the physical world, visual generation, and action prediction. To ensure the quality of the training data, we developed a rigorous data cleaning and annotation pipeline for both human egocentric data and robot data. To adapt the vision-language component to embodied tasks while preserving its inherited language capabilities, we represent low-level actions in natural language and introduce a pre-training recipe that assigns complementary supervision from visual question answering (VQA) data, human egocentric data, and robot demonstrations to the appropriate model components. During post-training, future visual noise augmentation reduces reliance on precise future predictions, while history-conditioned flow matching uses encoded action history to initialize action generation. Together, these designs significantly reduce denoising steps while maintaining performance. UniWAM achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across multiple evaluations, including in-distribution performance, robustness, generalization, instruction following, and long-horizon task execution. Furthermore, we uncover a log-linear scaling law of unified human-robot co-training, demonstrating the effectiveness of large-scale pre-training on a mixture of human and robot data.

</details>


### [8] [H-SPAR: Hydrodynamic-aware Simulation for Particle Transport and Autonomous Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01985)
*Navid Zarrabi,Nariman Yousefi,Sajad Saeedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: H-SPAR 将时变水流、粒子输运、概率采样与 USV 闭环自主控制整合到同一仿真框架中，揭示规划收益和采样效果需结合实际执行及水动力条件评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有海洋机器人仿真通常将水流、自主控制与采样目标分开建模，难以联合评估任务成本和采样表现。

Method: 在 ROS 2/Gazebo USV 仿真中使用共享的预计算时空流场，同时驱动拉格朗日粒子输运与水流对车辆的作用，并开展路径规划和覆盖采样实验。

Result: SVF-RRT* 相较传统 RRT* 在规划阶段降低上游成本 69.4%，但在时变水流下实际执行时降幅为 41.7%。在完整 H-SPAR 配置中，仅改变扫掠方向即可使粒子采样率最多变化 22.2%。

Conclusion: 规划性能不能代表实际任务表现；应在一致的水动力条件下联合评估路径规划、车辆执行、粒子输运与采样效果。

Abstract: Environmental robotic sampling requires considering the dual influence of water currents on robotic motion and particle transport. Existing marine robotics simulators generally model flow, autonomy, and sampling targets separately, limiting joint evaluation of mission cost and sampling performance. H-SPAR integrates spatially and temporally varying velocity fields, Lagrangian particle transport, probabilistic sampling, and ROS 2/Gazebo-based uncrewed surface vehicle (USV) autonomy. In this work, shared precomputed flow fields drive particle advection and current-induced forces during closed-loop vehicle execution. Path-planning experiments show that the existing current-aware planner SVF-RRT* achieves 69.4% lower upstream cost than conventional RRT* at the planning level, but this reduction falls to 41.7% during execution under time-varying currents, reflecting temporal flow variation, vehicle motion constraints, and path deviation omitted during planning. Coverage experiments show that sweep orientation changes the particle-sampling rate by up to 22.2% under the complete H-SPAR configuration. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating planning, vehicle execution, particle transport, and sampling together under consistent hydrodynamic conditions. The project webpage is available at this https URL , and the open-source code is available on GitHub at this https URL .

</details>


### [9] [BLT*: Informed Belief Localization Trees for Uncertainty-Aware Planning on Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01972)
*Elliot Preston-Krebs,Abhishek Goudar,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Informed BLT*，一种面向大型室外数字孪生环境、利用点云观测进行信念空间规划的采样算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信念空间规划在大型环境中处理点云观测时计算开销较高；需要提升初始可行解搜索效率，同时兼顾观测信息与概率碰撞约束。

Method: 将 RRT* 和 Informed RRT* 扩展至信念空间，并采用 2-Wasserstein 距离度量信念状态。在各向同性高斯信念假设下，高效连接采样状态；通过复用已有测量信息，避免反复传播观测。另提出生成语义标注数字孪生的框架。

Result: 在仿真环境和数字孪生中的实验显示，该方法在多数地图上更快找到初始解，且成本收敛表现具有竞争力。

Conclusion: Informed BLT* 将信念空间规划、信息利用与概率避障结合，为基于点云定位的真实环境规划提供了可扩展方案；摘要未提供具体性能数值或适用条件的进一步验证。

Abstract: We present Informed Belief Localization Trees* (Informed BLT*), a sampling-based belief space planning (BSP) algorithm that scales to large outdoor digital twins with point-cloud observations. We adapt RRT* and Informed RRT* to belief space using the $2$-Wasserstein ($W_2$) metric. Assuming isotropic Gaussian beliefs, sampled belief states can be connected efficiently while accounting for available information and probabilistic collision constraints. This enables steering and rewiring without repeatedly propagating observations, and allows previously computed measurement information to be reused. We present a framework to generate semantically labelled digital twins for planning in real-world environments with point-cloud-based localization. Experiments in simulated environments and digital twins show faster initial solution discovery in most maps with competitive cost convergence.

</details>


### [10] [TouchTherm: Building Multimodal Digital Twins of Objects for Tactile and Thermal Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01943)
*Yitao Zhang,Hong Ying,Haoran Guo,Xiaoying Zhou,Guanyu Chen,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TouchTherm 从真实物体构建可用于仿真的视觉、触觉与热学资产，结合微观表面重建和动态温度场估计，支持触觉识别及温度感知虚拟交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维数据集与重建方法侧重物体尺度的几何和视觉外观，较少刻画高保真触觉渲染所需的微观表面结构，也难以呈现交互中的瞬态温度变化。

Method: 视觉与触觉重建结合结构光扫描和基于光度立体的多视角法线图：将法线图配准到扫描几何并转换至切线空间，以恢复局部微观高度场；粗网格用于碰撞检测。热学重建则采集受控加热后自然冷却过程中的同步多视角红外视频，并据此重建受物理规律约束的动态热场。

Result: 在20个物体上的实验表明，微观高度场保留了主要表面结构，并恢复粗略几何之外的高频细节。热场在30秒和45秒时的留出表面温度平均绝对误差分别为0.465°C和0.592°C。生成的触觉资产支持基于触觉观测的合成到真实物体识别；手套式VR系统展示了具有空间和时间变化的热反馈。

Conclusion: TouchTherm 展示了构建多模态仿真资产的可行性，可将微观触觉线索与动态热信息纳入物体表示，为多感官模拟和温度感知虚拟交互提供支持。

Abstract: Robotic simulation and virtual reality increasingly require object assets that capture not only visual geometry but also the physical cues underlying tactile and thermal interaction. Existing 3D datasets and reconstruction methods primarily represent object-scale geometry and visual appearance, overlooking microscale surface structure for high-fidelity haptic rendering and transient temperature dynamics for temperature-aware interaction. We present TouchTherm, a framework for constructing simulation-ready visuo-tactile-thermal object assets from real-world objects. For visual and tactile reconstruction, we combine structured-light scanning with multiview normal maps obtained from photometric stereo. The normal maps are registered to the scanned geometry and transformed into tangent space to recover local micro-height fields for optical tactile rendering, while the coarse mesh handles collision detection. For thermal reconstruction, we capture synchronized multiview infrared videos of natural cooling following controlled heating and reconstruct a physics-regularized dynamic thermal field. Experiments on 20 objects show that the reconstructed micro-height fields preserve dominant surface structures and recover higher-frequency details beyond the coarse geometry, while the thermal fields achieve held-out surface-temperature MAEs of 0.465 degrees C and 0.592 degrees C at 30 s and 45 s, respectively. The resulting tactile assets support synthetic-to-real object recognition from tactile observations, while a glove-based VR system demonstrates spatially and temporally varying thermal feedback. These results highlight the potential of TouchTherm for multimodal sensory simulation and temperature-aware virtual interaction.

</details>


### [11] [Robot Learning on Discrete Surfaces: Theory and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01910)
*Matteo Dalle Vedove,Fares J. Abu-Dakka,Luigi Palopoli,Daniele Fontanelli,Matteo Saveriano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向多面体表面网格的离散黎曼学习框架，并将其应用于轨迹学习、回归与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习和运动生成方法通常将表面仅视为约束，未利用其内在几何；而 CAD 与三维重建常见的多面体网格具有离散结构，传统方法难以直接处理其面、边、顶点间的几何关系。

Method: 基于离散微分几何定义适用于面、边和顶点的对数映射、指数映射、平行移动及环境空间投影，并用于三类方法：改进动态运动基元（DMP）的映射与切锥编码；构造基于测地线且可控制适用性的高斯过程（GP）核；在黎曼流匹配（RFM）中采用网格原生算子。

Result: 仿真评估显示，DMP具有更好的跨表面泛化能力和稳定性，RFM相较谱方法提升生成质量并缩短训练时间；框架还在两个真实机器人场景中得到验证，包括跨表面泛化用户绘制轨迹，以及基于 RGB-D 重建表面规划抛光运动。

Conclusion: 该框架将多面体网格的离散几何直接纳入机器人学习，为不依赖表面光滑性假设的网格上运动建模与生成提供了统一方法。

Abstract: All the objects composing our world are enclosed within surfaces. Yet, most robot learning and motion generation frameworks treat surfaces as constraints ignoring their intrinsic geometry. This gap is acute for polyhedral meshes--the standard output of CAD and 3D reconstruction--whose discrete geometric structure remains unexploited. In this paper, we propose a unified discrete Riemannian framework that enables robot learning directly on polyhedral surface meshes. Using discrete differential geometry, we define logarithmic and exponential maps, parallel transport, and ambient-space projections that remain well-defined across faces, edges, and vertices. We instantiate the framework in three learning paradigms: (i) Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs), an improved exponential-map computation and a fixed-tangent-cone forcing-term encoding with parallel transport yield better cross-surface generalisation and stability over prior mesh-based approaches. (ii) Gaussian Process (GP), a geodesic-based kernel with practical admissibility control, enables regression at arbitrary mesh locations without smoothness assumptions. (iii) Riemannian Flow Matching (RFM), mesh-native operators improve generative quality over spectral baselines while reducing training time. The framework is validated in simulation against state-of-the-art methods and demonstrated on two real-robot scenarios: generalising user-drawn trajectories across different surfaces and planning polishing motions on RGB-D-reconstructed surfaces.

</details>


### [12] [Towards Physical Underwater Robotic Assistance for Scuba Diver Movement in Confined Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01906)
*Demetrious T. Kutzke,Junaed Sattar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Scuba divers are taught to control their depth to avoid rapid ascents and descents, which could result in serious injuries such as gas embolisms and barotrauma. However, many underwater tasks necessitate lateral control, maintaining distance between subsea structures such as coral reefs, submerged drilling instrumentation, or unexploded ordnance. In this work, we discuss a first-of-its-kind wearable robotic solution providing thruster-actuated directional guidance to a diver, as distinct from prior propulsive-assistance exoskeletons. We introduce ``Robotic Assisted Diver Movement in Confined Spaces'' (RADMCS), a wearable robot that assists divers in maintaining a fixed distance from subsea structures by leveraging perception techniques in monocular depth estimation and force-feedback from submersible thrusters to provide haptic feedback. Its small and compact form factor creates a foundational platform that could be expanded to include more sophisticated control and navigation behaviors. We present results from Institutional Review Board (IRB) in-water studies with eight human scuba diver participants on threshold sensitivity tests in both a closed-water swimming facility and ocean environments; distance-maintaining experiments in a closed-water facility; and form, fit, and function testing in the ocean. We demonstrate that relatively low thrust values (10 percent of maximum) allow robotic direction of a human's movement using the physical sensation of the robot's guidance.

</details>


### [13] [ChunkVLA-AM: Parallel Action Chunking for Vision-Language-Action Robot Control in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01856)
*Zhugang Liu,Kaichuang Zhang,Jinman Zhang,Pu Sun,Martha Asare,Jose Hernandez,Maxim Ermolinsky,Efren Saenz,Qi Lu,Jinghao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将 OpenVLA-OFT 部署到 FAIRINO FR3 机械臂的固定增材制造工作单元，并在物体转移任务中取得 92.9% 的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型应用于增材制造时，适配新机械臂本体成本较高，且环境变化可能导致性能下降。

Method: 构建数据处理流程，将单目真实演示转换为兼容 OpenVLA 的 TFDS/RLDS 数据集，以适配 FR3。每次推理生成包含 8 步的 7 维动作块，机械臂开环执行后再采集观测，实现动作块之间的闭环反馈；系统通过 FastAPI 在云端推理服务器与机械臂客户端之间传输观测。

Result: 在红、蓝目标各占一半的 42 次实机 A 到 B 物体转移试验中，成功 39 次，成功率为 92.9%。3 次失败均发生于最终放置阶段，原因是释放高度控制不足，导致物体倾倒。照明测试显示，灰度亮度 85–125 范围内误差较低，亮度 95 时平均空间误差最低。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了 OpenVLA-OFT 在固定增材制造工作单元中适配 FR3 并执行物体转移任务的可行性。提升最终放置时的释放高度控制，是进一步提高可靠性的关键。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models unify visual perception, language understanding, and action generation, offering new opportunities for automation in additive manufacturing (AM). However, deployment in AM remains challenging because adapting these models to unseen robot embodiments is costly, and performance can degrade under environment changes. In this work, we present a framework for deploying OpenVLA-OFT on a FAIRINO FR3 robot in a fixed AM workcell. A data pipeline converts monocular real-world demonstrations into OpenVLA-compatible TFDS/RLDS datasets to support adaptation to the FR3 embodiment. At runtime, each inference request predicts an eight-step chunk of 7-D actions. The FR3 executes each chunk open loop before capturing a new observation, providing closed-loop feedback between chunks. The system uses a cloud-edge architecture in which the FR3 client streams observations to a remote inference server through a FastAPI interface. In 42 physical A-to-B object-transfer trials, evenly split between red and blue targets, the system succeeded in 39 (92.9%). All three failures occurred during final placement, when insufficient release-height control caused the object to topple. An illumination sweep identified a low-error luminance range of 85-125 on a 0-255 scale, with the lowest mean spatial error at 95.

</details>


### [14] [FlashDexRetarget: Accelerating Dexterous Manipulation Data Generation through Multi-Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01849)
*Kyungmin Lee,Sibeen Kim,Dongyoon Hwang,Yoonsang Oh,Donghu Kim,Youngdo Lee,I Made Aswin Nahrendra,Jaegul Choo,Hojoon Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlashDexRetarget是一种基于强化学习的灵巧操作动作重定向框架，在多种手部平台和不同规模动作数据集上实现了较高成功率与较低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人类手—物体演示迁移到不同机器人手上，需要生成物理可行的动作。现有物理仿真方法在重定向成功率或针对动作的训练效率方面仍有不足。

Method: 融合物体点云、手—物体距离特征和未来轨迹编码，并通过物体运动与手—物体关系奖励提供监督。采用左右手分离的演员—评论家网络，并改造离策略算法FlashSAC以进行灵巧动作跟踪。

Result: 在涵盖单物体和双物体交互的50个动作基准上，成功率达到90%，约为所评估采样式基线的2.5倍，训练计算量最高比所评估强化学习基线低100倍。在XHand和Sharpa Wave Hand上均取得一致提升；扩展至200、500和1,000个动作时仍保持稳定，并能更高效地生成成功重定向动作。消融实验分析了各组件的作用，真实采集演示的回放结果展示了实际适用性。

Conclusion: 该方法兼顾重定向成功率与训练效率，并能扩展到更大规模的演示数据和不同机器人手，为利用人类操作演示训练灵巧机器人提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Human hand-object demonstrations offer a reusable source of dexterous robot manipulation data, but transferring them across embodiments requires physically feasible retargeting. Existing physics-based approaches face limitations in retargeting success, motion-specific training efficiency, or both. To address these limitations, we introduce FlashDexRetarget, an RL-based framework for high-success, efficient dexterous motion retargeting. To make the demonstrated interaction easier to learn, we combine object point-cloud observations, hand-object distance features, and future trajectory encodings with complementary rewards that supervise object motion and reference hand-object relationships. To further accelerate learning, we employ separate left- and right-hand actor critic networks and adapt the off-policy algorithm, FlashSAC to dexterous motion tracking. On a benchmark of 50 motions spanning single-object and two-object interactions, FlashDexRetarget achieves a 90% success rate, approximately 2.5x that of the evaluated sampling-based baselines, while requiring up to 100x less training compute than the evaluated RL-based baselines. Evaluations on both XHand and Sharpa Wave Hand show consistent gains, and component-wise ablations examine the contributions of our design choices. Beyond the 50-motion benchmark, experiments with 200, 500, and 1,000 motions demonstrate that our method remains stable at larger scales and produces successful retargeted motions more efficiently as the training set grows. Qualitative replay results using real-world-captured demonstrations further illustrate the applicability of our framework to recorded human manipulation. Videos and code are available at this https URL

</details>


### [15] [Query-Conditioned Articulation Estimation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01726)
*Abdelrhman Werby,Fabio Scaparro,Kai O. Arra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QueryArt 从单张 RGB 图像、一个二维查询点和相机内参估计物体关节参数，并利用查询点的一次深度测量恢复真实尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图方法将部件分割与关节估计耦合，容易受漏检和部件关联错误影响；单视角也无法直接确定物体的绝对尺度，因此难以可靠回归公制三维几何。

Method: QueryArt 以图像、二维查询点和相机内参为输入，将关节几何表示为相对于查询点、并以该点深度为单位的参数，使估计目标可由单张图像确定。再通过查询点的深度测量补足尺度。模型使用精选的合成与真实关节物体数据集训练。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，QueryArt 在大多数关节估计指标上优于近期基线，包括分布外数据。移动操作平台上的真实实验覆盖 57 次操作、16 个物体部件和 5 类视角，成功率为 70.2%。

Conclusion: 以查询点为参照进行尺度归一化，可缓解单视角下绝对尺度不可辨识的问题；结合一次局部深度测量，QueryArt 能将单图关节估计扩展到公制参数，并展现出真实操作应用潜力。

Abstract: Enabling robots to estimate the kinematic parameters of articulated objects unlocks a wide range of capabilities for interaction and manipulation. The estimation has to happen from the information the robot currently observes, often just a single RGB image of an object it has never seen before. Current single-image approaches couple articulation part segmentation with articulation estimation, making their predictions vulnerable to missed detections and incorrect part associations, and they regress metric 3D geometry that a single view fixes only up to scale. We present QueryArt, a model that estimates articulation parameters from a single RGB image, a 2D query point, and camera intrinsics. QueryArt is trained to estimate the 3D articulation geometry relative to the queried point and in units of its depth, which keeps its target identifiable from the image alone. A single depth measurement at the query point then supplies the scale and recovers the metric parameters. We train QueryArt on a curated mixture of synthetic and real-world articulation datasets. We evaluate QueryArt on several benchmarks and compare it against recent baselines. QueryArt outperforms recent baselines on most articulation metrics, including on out-of-distribution data. To demonstrate the model's capabilities in real-world settings, we evaluate QueryArt on a mobile manipulator across 57 manipulation trials spanning 16 object parts and five viewpoint classes, achieving a 70.2% success rate. We provide code and videos at: this https URL

</details>


### [16] [ActiveWAM: Evidence-Aware Active Vision for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01698)
*Renjun Wu,Luzhou Ge,Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActiveWAM 联合学习视角控制与操作，在保留关键视觉证据的同时主动获取新观测；在基准和真实厨房任务中均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动视觉操作需要同时控制相机与末端执行器，但相机转动会改变有限观测窗口内可见的信息。获取新视角可能遮蔽任务关键线索，维持当前视角又可能错过有用信息，因此需要权衡保留与获取观测。

Method: 提出 ActiveWAM 统一世界—动作模型，联合学习观测与操作。训练时通过反演约束冻结的视频先验，使其利用任务相关源证据和可见的时间变化；部署时，策略依据视角感知历史生成双臂及云台动作，包括保持视角和重新获取视角，并用新测得的 RGB 观测更新上下文。未来视频预测仅作为协同训练信号，动作生成无需解码未来视频或使用最优视角标注。另构建包含 50 项任务、支持可执行云台控制及自动生成示范的 RoboTwin-AV 基准。

Result: ActiveWAM 在 TAVIS 分布外成功率上较最强基线最高提升 17.0 个百分点；在 RoboTwin-AV 上比 Fast-WAM 高 20.0 个百分点；在真实厨房操作任务中高 26.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将视角选择与操作策略统一建模，并依据持续更新的视觉证据在保留和获取视角之间作出权衡，有助于提升分布外及真实场景中的主动视觉操作表现。

Abstract: Active vision manipulation requires a policy to control both its camera and its end-effectors, yet camera motion determines which evidence remains visible within finite observation windows. Acquiring a new view can displace task-critical cues, while retaining a view forgoes potentially useful observations. We formulate this as an evidence-aware retain--acquire problem and present ActiveWAM, a unified world--action model that learns observation and manipulation jointly. To this end, we propose training-time inversion which constrains a frozen video prior by task-bearing source evidence and visible temporal changes, eliminating the need for test-time inversion or candidate ranking. At deployment, the policy generates bimanual and pan/tilt actions-including stay and reacquisition behaviors-from view-aware history, and updates context from newly measured RGB observations. Future-video prediction serves as a co-training signal, while action generation requires neither future-video decoding nor optimal viewpoint annotations. We introduce RoboTwin-AV, a 50-task benchmark with executable pan/tilt control and automatically generated demonstrations. ActiveWAM improves TAVIS out-of-distribution success by up to 17.0 percentage points over the strongest baselines, achieves 20.0 additional points over Fast-WAM on RoboTwin-AV, and outperforms it by 26.7 points on real-world physical kitchen tasks.

</details>


### [17] [Learning a Resolution-Consistent Jacobian Field for Bio-Inspired Rigid-Soft Finger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01668)
*Tianyou Liang,Haisen Zeng,Shanjun Chen,YiMing Zhu,Zhongyue Lu,Zirong Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出雅可比流匹配（JFM），学习具有时间分辨率一致性的雅可比场，用于预测和优化腱驱刚柔耦合手指的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于雅可比的离散控制依赖逐点局部线性近似，性能易受传感器采样频率和控制器更新频率影响，难以准确建模强非线性、构型相关的运动响应。

Method: 将条件流匹配用于学习驱动到运动转换的动力学流，构建分辨率一致的雅可比场；既支持单步预测，也可通过常微分方程积分进行连续滚动预测。

Result: 在腱驱刚柔耦合手指实验中，JFM抑制了离群误差；相较离散雅可比学习基线，单步预测全局平均RMSE降低超过53%。稀疏采样（Stride=8）下，ODE积分轨迹的RMSE中位数降低14.43%，误差方差降低24.87%。

Conclusion: 基于流的雅可比场能够跨时间分辨率保持一致推断，可作为刚柔耦合非线性系统离线多步轨迹优化的有效局部模型。

Abstract: Bio-inspired tendon-driven rigid-soft coupled dexterous fingers exhibit strong nonlinearity and configuration-dependent sensitivity, making accurate modeling challenging. In discrete-time control, Jacobian-based kinematic algorithms typically rely on point-wise local linear approximations, which makes their performance sensitive to sensor sampling frequency and controller update frequency. To address this issue, we propose Jacobian Flow Matching (JFM), a structured learning framework based on Conditional Flow Matching (CFM), to learn a resolution-consistent Jacobian field that models actuation-to-motion transitions as a dynamical flow. The proposed framework supports both single-step prediction and continuous rollout via ODE integration, enabling consistent inference across temporal resolutions. Experiments on a tendon-driven rigid-soft finger show that the proposed method suppresses outlier errors and improves single-step prediction accuracy, reducing the global average RMSE by over 53% compared with a baseline discrete Jacobian learning approach. For long-horizon prediction, trajectories recovered via ODE integration achieve higher fidelity under sparse sampling (Stride = 8), reducing the RMSE median by 14.43% and the error variance by 24.87%. These results demonstrate that the learned flow-based Jacobian field provides an effective local model for offline multi-step trajectory optimization in rigid-soft coupled nonlinear systems.

</details>


### [18] [SonarVoxNet: Diver Detection in 3D Bounding Box using 3D Sonar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01644)
*Eugene Park,Jiwon Lee,Seyoung Kan,Trung Dong,Xiaomin Lin,Jane Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向水下潜水员跟踪的三维声呐检测方法 SonarVoxNet，并发布带有完整三维姿态标注的数据集 Diver3D。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前视声呐缺少估计身体朝向所需的高度信息；三维声呐虽保留高度信息，但回波稀疏且噪声较大。现有检测器主要针对稠密激光雷达数据及仅绕偏航轴旋转的目标，无法准确表示潜水员的俯仰和滚转姿态。

Method: SonarVoxNet 将体素编码器与无锚框、基于中心的检测头适配于三维声呐数据，并以连续六维旋转参数化取代仅预测偏航角的表示，从而预测完整的 9 自由度定向边界框。研究还构建了 Diver3D，这是首个提供潜水员完整三维姿态标签的公开三维声呐数据集，采集于自然洞穴潜水环境，并通过受控消融实验比较骨干网络与检测头。

Result: 消融实验表明，从仅偏航旋转改为完整 SO(3) 旋转，是提升三维声呐潜水员检测效果的主要因素；该改动显著提高检测准确率并降低姿态误差。

Conclusion: 研究表明，仅凭三维声呐即可恢复潜水员的完整身体朝向，为后续潜水员姿态估计及人与水下机器人交互研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) assisting human divers must continuously track not only the diver's 3D position but also their full-body orientation. However, vision-based perception is unreliable underwater, and forward-looking sonar -- despite being widely used -- discards the elevation information needed for orientation estimation, posing a fundamental limitation. Recently commercialized 3D sonar preserves elevation but produces sparse, noisy returns, and existing detectors are built for dense LiDAR data and for targets that remain upright and rotate only about the yaw axis (e.g., vehicles, pedestrians), making them unable to represent a freely pitching and rolling diver. To address this gap, we present two contributions. First, SonarVoxNet adapts a voxel-based encoder and an anchor-free center-based detection head to 3D sonar data, replacing the conventional yaw-only rotation representation with a continuous 6D rotation parameterization to predict full 9-DoF oriented bounding boxes -- to our knowledge, the first 3D sonar diver detector to do so. Second, Diver3D is the first public 3D sonar dataset with full 3D orientation labels for divers in diverse, non-upright poses, collected at a natural cave-diving site. Through controlled ablations over the backbone and detection head, we show that the dominant factor behind accurate 3D sonar-based diver detection is the transition from yaw-only rotation to full-SO(3) rotation. This transition substantially improves detection accuracy and reduces orientation error. These results demonstrate that full-body diver orientation is recoverable from 3D sonar alone, laying the groundwork for future work on diver pose estimation and diver-robot interaction.

</details>


### [19] [ReCo: Response-Consistent Locomotion with Policy-Aware MPC for Legged Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01612)
*Kuankuan Sima,Yichao Gao,Chenxi Gu,Kefan Zhao,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReCo 将响应一致性训练与策略感知模型预测控制结合，实现行走中的连续腿式操作，并提升末端跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在持续行走时，机械臂末端跟踪会受底座运动影响；但传统模型预测控制只能补偿可预测的底座运动，而强化学习策略对指令的响应会随步态相位、接触状态和负载变化。

Method: 通过响应塑形训练强化学习策略，使其在随机化动力学条件下对指令产生一致、可重复的响应；再辨识闭环响应模型，供模型预测控制联合规划底座运动指令与机械臂运动。

Result: 仿真基准测试中，相较各指标上的最佳基线，位置和姿态均方根误差分别降低28.7%和27.4%。真实机器人实验展示了机载连续腿式操作及底座与机械臂的协调运动。

Conclusion: 使学习策略的指令响应更可预测，并将其纳入模型预测控制，有助于提升行走状态下腿式操作的跟踪性能；仿真与真实实验均验证了该框架的可行性。

Abstract: Continuous legged manipulation requires accurate end-effector tracking while the base keeps walking. Combining reinforcement learning (RL) with model predictive control (MPC) suits this task: the learned policy provides robust locomotion, while MPC coordinates the base and arm to compensate for tracking errors. However, MPC can compensate only for base motion that it can predict, and a learned policy's command response varies with gait phase, contact, and payload. We present ReCo, a framework that couples response-consistent locomotion with policy-aware MPC for legged manipulation. Response shaping trains the policy to respond to commands consistently and repeatably across randomized dynamics. An identified closed-loop response model then lets MPC jointly plan locomotion commands and arm motion. On the simulation benchmark, ReCo reduces position and orientation root-mean-square error (RMSE) by 28.7% and 27.4% relative to the best baseline for each metric. Real-world experiments demonstrate onboard continuous legged manipulation with coordinated base and arm motion.

</details>


### [20] [LiDARFlow: Real-Time Panel-Based MAV Guidance in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01573)
*João Machado,Zeynep Bilgin,Matthieu Verdoucq,Murat Bronz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向未知、杂乱环境中微型飞行器的轻量级机载避障引导方法，并通过室内飞行实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中，微型飞行器需要仅依靠机载传感器实时感知障碍并安全导航，同时受限于机载计算资源。

Method: 基于源自空气动力学势流理论的面板方法，根据机载激光雷达测量在线构建和更新障碍物表示，生成平滑、无碰撞的引导向量场，并将其与名义引导向量场融合以形成控制输入。

Result: 在航点导航和方向引导两种室内实验场景中，飞行器均成功完成任务，并仅凭机载感知实现实时避障。方法计算开销较低；点云处理是主要的实际限制。

Conclusion: 实验支持该方法用于未知环境机载引导的可行性，表明其适合资源受限的微型飞行器；进一步提升点云处理效率可能改善实际应用表现。

Abstract: This paper presents a guidance algorithm for micro aerial vehicles operating in unknown, cluttered environments using only onboard sensing. The method is based on a panel formulation originally derived from aerodynamic potential-flow theory and generates smooth, collision-free guidance vectors from locally perceived obstacles. The approach is extended to unknown environments by constructing and updating the obstacle representation online from onboard LiDAR measurements. The resulting obstacle-avoidance field is integrated with a nominal guiding vector field to produce the final control input. The system is experimentally validated in indoor flight tests under two scenarios: waypoint navigation and directional guidance. In both cases, the vehicle successfully completes its task while avoiding all obstacles in real time using only onboard perception. The results demonstrate that the method is computationally lightweight and suitable for onboard implementation, with pointcloud processing identified as the main practical limitation. These results support the feasibility of lightweight onboard guidance in unknown environments.

</details>


### [21] [OpenSpace Lab Solution to the IROS 2026 Indoor Exploration Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01505)
*Yuxuan Zhang,Dong Li,Zezhou Sun,Yuxuan Xu,Siyu Teng,Yuchen Li,Jianjian Yang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍 OpenSpace Lab 在单机器人与多机器人信息采集竞赛中的探索方案及成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限运行时间与预算约束下，机器人需要有效探索未知区域，同时兼顾返航要求，并减少多机器人之间的重复搜索。

Method: 单机器人方案利用预训练地图补全预测进行全局规划，并根据剩余时间调整探索决策、纳入返航约束。多机器人方案依据观测收益、移动成本和预算选择目标，并共享地图与行动意图信息以提升协同效率。

Result: 团队在单机器人公开组获第1名，在单机器人和多机器人封闭组均获第3名；对应覆盖率分别为61.04%、39.53%和39.91%。

Conclusion: 该方案结合地图预测、时间约束规划与共享信息驱动的多机器人目标选择，在不同赛道取得了具有竞争力的覆盖表现；扩展论文正在准备投稿，代码计划于论文接收后发布。

Abstract: This report presents the \textbf{OpenSpace Lab}'s solution to the Competition on Intelligent Information Gathering for Single and Multi-Robot Systems Workshops, organized as part of the IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS) 2026. Our team reached 1st place in the Single-Robot Public Track and 3rd place in both the Single- and Multi-Robot Private Tracks. The single-robot framework utilizes pre-trained map completion predictions for global planning to prioritize unexplored areas. To reconcile map coverage with limited operation time, we introduce a remaining-time-based exploration strategy that integrates homing constraints into the decision-making process. For multi-robot exploration, we utilize a utility-driven target selection strategy that balances observation gains, movement costs, and budget constraints, leveraging shared map and intent data to eliminate redundant search and maximize coordination efficiency. Our solution reached a 61.04\% coverage rate in the Single-Robot Public Track, while reaching 39.53\% and 39.91\% coverage in the Single- and Multi-Robot Private Tracks, respectively. An extended full-length paper based on this report is currently being prepared for submission, and the source code will be released upon acceptance of the full manuscript at this https URL .

</details>


### [22] [Continue, Abort, or Fall: Viability-Aware Policy Selection (VAPS) for Safe Humanoid Acrobatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01397)
*Siwei Ju,Lu Liu,Jan Peters,Oleg Arenz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Viability-Aware Policy Selection（VAPS），通过实时评估候选策略的短期可行性，选择仍安全且尽可能保留任务目标的动作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态人形机器人动作在扰动或仿真到现实的偏差下可能偏离参考轨迹；单纯的动作跟踪策略缺乏补救能力，而仅决定何时切换到保护策略，也忽略了切换到哪种策略这一关键问题。

Method: 除保护性跌倒策略外，训练可随时中止动作并以双脚着地的中止策略。控制器每一步利用学习到的预测器，估计名义跟踪策略与中止策略在短时域内是否仍可行，再按牺牲程度从低到高选择可行策略，优先保留更具任务野心的行为。

Result: 在随机扰动仿真中，VAPS 显著降低 Unitree G1 和 LimX Oli 的头部及手部接触；在 LimX Oli 的侧空翻任务中验证了可行性预测器和完整控制器。与训练得到的最强单网络方案相比，VAPS 在任务成功率和头部冲击两项指标上均实现帕累托优势。

Conclusion: 将安全建模为依赖策略的滚动决策，有助于兼顾动作完成与硬件保护；VAPS 也可用于监督尚未充分训练的策略。

Abstract: Dynamic humanoid motions such as flips risk hardware damage due to suboptimal policies, disturbances or sim-to-real gaps. A motion tracking policy offers no way out once the maneuver leaves its reference, and a backup policy needs to take over to protect the hardware for a minimum-damage landing. Which backup to use matters as much as when to switch. We present Viability-Aware Policy Selection (VAPS), which treats safety as a policy-conditioned, receding-horizon decision. Besides a protective fall policy, we also train an abort policy which can abort the motion at any time, landing on its feet. At every control step, learned predictors estimate whether the nominal tracking policy and the abort policy remain viable over a short horizon, and a least-sacrificial hierarchy keeps the most task-ambitious behavior that remains viable. In simulation with randomized disturbances, VAPS sharply reduces head contact and hand contact, which are the dominant sources of hardware damage, with both a Unitree G1 and a LimX Oli; on the LimX Oli, we validate the viability predictors and the full VAPS controller for side-flip motions. VAPS Pareto-dominates the strongest single-network alternatives we could train, including an end-to-end safe-tracking policy and students distilled from VAPS's own oracle-routed decisions, in both task success and head impact. We also show that VAPS is a powerful framework to supervise undertrained policies and protect the hardware.

</details>


### [23] [Is Success All You Need? Investigating the Impact of Input Perturbations on VLA Behaviour in Tabletop Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01351)
*Sophie Higham,Riccardo Andrea Izzo,Matteo Matteucci,Alessandro Suglia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与基准无关的评估框架，用于衡量视觉—语言—动作（VLA）模型在扰动下成功执行任务时的行为稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 鲁棒性评估主要依赖任务成功率（TSR），但相同的成功率可能掩盖成功轨迹在执行方式上的显著差异，难以全面反映模型表现。

Method: 扩展 LIBERO 与 LIBERO-Plus 基准，评估三种先进 VLA 模型在四类 LIBERO 任务套件和七种扰动条件下的表现。除 TSR 外，还分析成功轨迹的典型行为及其变异性，包括运动平滑度、执行效率和夹爪行为。

Result: 扰动会改变成功轨迹的执行行为，这种变化未必能从 TSR 推断出来。在相同扰动条件下，部分模型的 TSR 相近，但成功轨迹的行为特征存在明显差异。

Conclusion: 仅评估任务是否完成不足以全面衡量 VLA 模型的鲁棒性。建议在 TSR 基础上补充行为评估指标，以刻画机器人成功完成任务的方式及其变异性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved high task success rates on robot manipulation task benchmarks. More recently, there has been an emphasis on evaluating the robustness of VLA models to perturbations. However, this robustness is still predominantly measured through Task Success Rate (TSR). In this work, we propose a benchmark-agnostic evaluation framework to measure the behavioural robustness of models by characterising how successful trajectories are executed under perturbation. We implement this methodology by extending the widely-used LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus benchmarks. Across three state-of-the-art VLA models, four LIBERO task suites and seven perturbation conditions, we evaluate changes in both typical successful behaviour and its variability, including metrics of motion smoothness, efficiency and gripper behaviour. We find that perturbations can alter the behaviour of successful trajectories, a phenomenon which cannot necessarily be inferred from TSR alone. Across LIBERO suites, we identify cases where state-of-the-art VLA models achieve comparable TSR under the same perturbation condition, yet behaviour on successful trajectories diverges substantially. Therefore, to have a more robust assessment of task performance, we argue that suitable measures of robustness should capture not only whether a task is completed, but also how the robot behaves while completing it. When evaluating the robustness of VLA models, TSR may be complemented by behavioural evaluation metrics that characterise the nature and variability of successful task execution by robots.

</details>


### [24] [Cross-entropy optimization with prioritized constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01319)
*Francisco Roldan Sanchez,Pau de las Heras Molins,David Fridovich-Keil,Georgios Bakirtzis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TierCEM，在交叉熵优化中直接落实严格约束优先级，无需为各约束设定重要性权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权惩罚可能导致优化器提升加权得分，却违背预期的约束优先顺序；因此需要能明确处理约束冲突的方法。

Method: TierCEM 按优先级从高到低逐步筛选采样候选。若某项约束筛选后候选集为空，则回退到最近的非空集合，并选取对该阻塞约束违反程度最小的精英样本，同时保留更高优先级约束的满足状态。

Result: 在二维导航和接触丰富的推搡任务中，涵盖本体感知与学习型世界模型设置。实验表明，调换约束顺序会改变冲突时被违反的约束；将任务进展置于较高优先级，也能在必要时放宽低优先级约束，以避免其阻碍进展。

Conclusion: TierCEM 为存在冲突的约束优化提供了基于排序的处理方式，使优化结果遵循明确的优先级，而不依赖人工设定的约束权重。

Abstract: When constraints conflict, an optimizer must determine which requirements to preserve and which to relax. On the one hand, a priority ordering specifies which requirements take precedence. On the other hand, penalty-based formulations encode their relative importance through numerical weights. Depending on these weights, a solution can improve its weighted score while violating intended priorities. We introduce TierCEM, a variant of the cross-entropy method that incorporates strict constraint priorities directly into elite selection without requiring per-constraint importance weights. TierCEM works by sequentially filtering sampled candidates, from highest- to lowest-priority constraint. If and when a constraint eliminates all remaining candidates, TierCEM returns to the last nonempty set and selects elites with the smallest violations of that blocking constraint, recursively preserving satisfaction of all higher-priority constraints. We evaluate TierCEM on 2D navigation and contact-rich pushing tasks in proprioceptive and learned world-model settings. Experiments show that reversing the constraint ordering changes which constraints are violated under conflict. Prioritizing progress toward the task objective also enables TierCEM to relax lower-priority constraints when they would otherwise prevent further progress.

</details>


### [25] [Ex vivo breach detection using electrical conductivity during robotic pedicle drilling in the spine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01310)
*Jorge Andrés Pérez Velásquez,François Teyssere,Thibault Chandanson,Quentin Grimal,Brahim Tamadazte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究提出一种结合机器人钻孔与电阻抗感知的椎弓根钻孔方法，可实时识别潜在穿破并在危险发生前停止钻孔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊柱侧弯治疗中的椎弓根螺钉置入精度要求高，受视野有限和解剖差异影响，存在穿破风险；现有机器人方案通常依赖术中电离辐射成像及复杂配准。

Method: 在椎弓根钻孔工具中集成电阻抗传感器，分析钻孔过程中的信号变化，以识别与朝向椎管的潜在穿破相关的电导率突变。该方法仅使用工具自身信号，无需外部传感器或设备。

Result: 离体实验报告称，该方法在全部51例钻孔中预防了穿破。摘要另有“100 of the 51 drilling cases”的矛盾表述，可能为编辑错误；后文一致报告为51例全部成功预防。

Conclusion: 研究初步证明，利用电阻抗感知进行机器人椎弓根钻孔实时穿破预防具有可行性，可能减少对外部感知系统的依赖并提升置钉安全性；其有效性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Purpose: Pedicle screw placement is technically demanding in scoliosis treatment. High precision is required due to limited visibility, anatomical variability, and the risk of complications. Although robotic systems assist CT-based planning and execution, they still rely on ionizing intraoperative imaging and complex registration. This study proposes robotic pedicle drilling with real-time preventive breach detection using electrical bioimpedance sensing. Methods: We developed a robotic approach combined with a pedicle-drilling tool equipped with a proprioceptive electrical bioimpedance sensor developed by SpineGuard. A real-time detection algorithm was designed to analyze the electrical bioimpedance signal during drilling and identify abrupt changes in conductivity associated with potential breaches towards the spinal canal. The method operates without external devices or sensors. Results: The ex vivo experiments showed that the proposed method prevented breaches in 100 of the 51 drilling cases. These findings demonstrate the system's ability to detect potentially hazardous events during drilling and to stop the procedure before. The ex vivo experiments demonstrated that the proposed method prevented breaches in all 51 drilling cases. Conclusions: This work demonstrates the feasibility of robotic pedicle drilling with electrical bioimpedance sensing for real-time breach prevention. Using only the tool signal, the method eliminates the need for external sensing systems and supports safer pedicle screw placement.

</details>


### [26] [Interaction-Stiffness-Guided Basis Allocation in Dynamic Movement Primitives for Efficient Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01288)
*Chan Xu,Silu Chen,Dehao Wang,Xiyu Chen,Dexin Jiang,Chi Zhang,Guilin Yang,Chenguang Yang,Zaojun Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出阶段关键性引导的动态运动基元（SC-DMPs），根据任务阶段的重要程度自适应分配基函数，以提升精度关键技能的轨迹表示与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DMP的基函数布局预先固定，难以按不同阶段的精度需求分配表示能力，因而可能无法充分描述几何复杂或受任务约束的关键区域。

Method: 结合操作者—机器人交互刚度，以及由多次示范在任务空间中的变化程度得到的轨迹一致性线索，构建阶段关键性指标。依据该指标，通过逆累积关键性在归一化时间内重新分布基函数中心，并映射至规范相位域；同时调整基函数带宽，以改变局部表示范围。

Result: 手写轨迹和三项真实机器人任务的实验表明，关键性较高的阶段集中在几何要求较高或任务约束较强的区域。与DMP、ProMP、ProDMP、GP-MP和KMP相比，SC-DMPs在轨迹复现、终点泛化和任务关键区域精度方面表现更好，同时保持模型紧凑。

Conclusion: 自适应基函数分配能够在保留经典DMP稳定结构的同时，将更多表示能力用于关键阶段，为精度敏感的机器人技能学习提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) provide a compact and stable formulation for trajectory representation and generalization in robot skill learning. However, their predefined basis layout limits the allocation of approximation capacity according to stage-dependent precision requirements. To address this issue, this article proposes Stage-Criticality-Guided Dynamic Movement Primitives (SC-DMPs) with adaptive basis allocation for precision-critical skill learning. Operator-robot interaction stiffness and a trajectory-consistency cue derived from cross-demonstration task-space variability are integrated to construct a stage-criticality index. Guided by this index, basis centers are redistributed in normalized time through inverse cumulative criticality and mapped to the canonical phase domain, while their bandwidths are refined to adjust local approximation support. This enables denser and more flexible representation at high-criticality stages while retaining sparser allocation elsewhere. Experiments on handwriting trajectories and three real-robot tasks show that the inferred criticality is concentrated in geometrically demanding and task-constrained regions. Comparisons with DMPs, ProMPs, ProDMP, GP-MP, and KMP demonstrate improved trajectory reproduction, endpoint generalization, and task-critical accuracy while retaining a compact model and the stable structure of classical DMPs.

</details>


### [27] [ColoACT: Multi-Cue Action Chunking for Smooth Autonomous Colon Navigation on a Self-Propelled Endoscopic Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01258)
*Jian Hu,Shujing He,Leixin Chang,Zongze Li,Ding Huang,Chaoyang Shi,Chengzhi Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ColoACT：结合 RGB-D-E 感知与动作分块 Transformer 的自主结肠导航系统，并在离体猪结肠中验证其导航能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠形变、内窥镜图像纹理弱且存在高光，以及器械与组织间复杂接触，使自主导航困难。传统几何方法易受手工特征和切换逻辑限制；学习方法则可能出现几何估计不稳定、控制过度平滑及仿真到现实迁移差距。

Method: 将 ColoACT 部署于紧凑型自推进斜齿轮内窥镜机器人（BGER）。策略输入包含 RGB、估计相对深度和基于梯度构造的伪高程图，以突出皱襞脊线等高频几何线索；通过预测重叠动作块并进行时间集成，实现连续平滑控制。

Result: 在约 60 厘米的不同离体猪结肠中，直线段和弯曲段成功率分别为 85.4% 和 72.5%；90 度转弯成功率为 70%，双弯序列为 60%。系统还在更具挑战性的三弯段展示了可行性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，融合多种视觉几何线索与动作分块控制，可支持紧凑型机器人在离体结肠中的自主导航；但复杂弯道上的成功率仍有限，且摘要未提供与其他方法的定量对比或活体验证。

Abstract: Autonomous colonoscopic navigation can reduce operator burden and the risk of loop formation or tissue trauma, but remains challenging due to deformable anatomy, weak-texture and specular endoscopic visuals, and contact-rich viscoelastic interactions. Existing methods either rely on geometry-driven pipelines, which are efficient and interpretable yet brittle due to manually engineered features and switching logic, or adopt learning-based policies, whose inferred depth/geometry can become temporally inconsistent or overly smooth under weak texture and specular highlights while simulation-trained variants (e.g., deep reinforcement learning) may further suffer from a sim-to-real gap. We propose ColoACT, an autonomous navigation system that integrates an RGB-D-E based Action Chunking Transformer policy (ColoACT policy) for a compact self-propelled Bevel-Gear-Based Endoscopic Robot (BGER). The ColoACT policy augments RGB with estimated relative depth and a gradient-based pseudo-elevation map to enhance fold-ridge saliency and other high-frequency geometric cues, and enables smooth continuous control of the BGER by predicting overlapping action chunks and fusing them via temporal ensembling. In different \textit{ex-vivo} porcine colons (approximately 60 cm), our system achieves success rates of 85.4\% and 72.5\% in straight and curved segments, respectively, and achieves 70\% success in 90-degree turns and 60\% in double-bend sequences, with feasibility further demonstrated in challenging triple-bend segments. The project page is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [28] [EIDA: Execution-Interface Dynamics Adaptation for Real-to-Sim-to-Real Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01219)
*Yiwei Qian,Shanze Wang,Qingyuan Hu,Xinming Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EIDA通过适配机器人执行接口的运动响应与速度反馈，提升了导航策略从仿真到真实机器人的迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 速度指令产生的实际运动和策略接收到的反馈可能与训练仿真模型不一致，导致仿真到真实迁移失败；传统做法可能需要重建复杂的执行器动力学。

Method: 利用目标平台的执行数据分别拟合机体坐标系位姿增量和策略可见的速度反馈，并将短时速度反馈历史加入策略输入。拟合模型嵌入轻量级、GPU并行仿真器，用于策略训练与评估。

Result: 在Jackal和Go2完整验证集上，拟合模型相较预设运动模型降低了位置与偏航角预测误差。在100个独立物理仿真导航环境中，EIDA在有无全局引导时均取得对比学习策略中最高的成功率和导航得分。反馈消融实验表明，匹配策略接收的速度估计是重要因素。真实Go2静态场景测试中，EIDA在20次试验中均无碰撞抵达目标，基线为4次。

Conclusion: 无需精细重建执行器动力学，仅适配执行接口的运动响应与反馈，也能显著改善导航策略的仿真到现实迁移表现。

Abstract: Simulation-to-robot transfer can fail when velocity commands produce motion and feedback that differ from those modeled during policy training. We present execution-interface dynamics adaptation (EIDA), which fits these responses from target-platform execution data without reconstructing actuator dynamics. A model of body-frame pose increments updates simulator geometry, while a separate model predicts the velocity feedback observed by the policy; a short history of velocity feedback is included in the policy input. The fitted models are used within a lightweight GPU-parallel simulator. On the full Jackal and Go2 validation sets, the fitted models reduced position and yaw prediction errors relative to the simulator's predefined motion model. Across 100 benchmark navigation environments evaluated in a separate physics-based simulator, EIDA achieved the highest success rate and navigation score among the compared learned policies, both with and without global guidance. Feedback ablations further supported the need to match policy-facing velocity estimates. On a physical Unitree Go2, EIDA reached the goal without collision in all 20 static-scene trials, compared with 4 of 20 for the baseline. These results show that execution-interface adaptation can improve navigation transfer without detailed actuator simulation.

</details>


### [29] [AFD-CAMLs: Agile Force-Distribution-Aware Planning and Control for Cable-Suspended Aerial Multi-Lifting Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01185)
*Antreas Kourris,Sihao Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种多无人机协同搬运框架，通过规划与控制协同改善缆绳张力分配，同时保持机动性和载荷跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冗余或病态的张力—合力分配，加上几何偏差和底层跟踪误差，可能造成张力分布不均，影响协同搬运的稳定性与可行性。

Method: 全局规划器在预设内力条件下探索分配零空间，生成载荷轨迹与缆绳力参考；集中式局部规划器将这些参考纳入代价函数，规划各无人机轨迹。随后，导纳滤波器比较规划力与机载张力估计，并调整运动学参考，以改善退化或近退化构型下的力跟踪。

Result: 涵盖四至十架无人机的仿真与实验表明，该方法在悬停和高机动动作中均能使张力分配更加均衡，且未降低机动性或载荷跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 将零空间力分配规划与基于张力反馈的导纳调整结合，可缓解协同搬运中的力分配不均，并适用于冗余或近退化构型。

Abstract: Multiple UAVs can cooperatively transport heavy payloads while controlling their position and orientation. Trajectory-based methods offer high agility while satisfying system constraints, but can produce uneven force distributions when the tension-to-wrench allocation is redundant or ill-conditioned, particularly under geometric mismatch and low-level tracking errors. We propose a hybrid planning-and-control framework to address this problem. A global planner generates payload trajectories and cable-force references by exploring the allocation null space under a prescribed internal-force setting. These references augment the cost of a centralized local planner, promoting feasible force distributions while generating trajectories for all UAVs. An admittance filter then compares the planned forces with onboard cable-tension estimates and adjusts the kinematic references to improve force tracking in degenerate or near-degenerate configurations. Simulations and experiments involving four to ten UAVs demonstrate more balanced tension distributions during both hovering and demanding agile maneuvers, without compromising agility or payload-tracking performance.

</details>


### [30] [Recova: Agent-Guided Failure Recovery for Autonomous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01178)
*Isabella Liu,An-Chieh Cheng,Johan Bjorck,Zhiding Yu,Hongxu Yin,Jan Kautz,Linxi Fan,Yuke Zhu,Sifei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Recova 在数字孪生中联合学习任务执行与失败恢复，并通过真实机器人经验持续验证和改进；实验显示其提升成功率并减少人工介入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作失败可能使场景进入任务策略无法自行恢复的状态。构建有效恢复能力，需要可扩展的失败探索，并确保学到的行为能适用于真实物理环境。

Method: Recova 在重建的数字孪生中诊断失败、测试恢复程序，并分别收集任务执行与恢复轨迹以训练相应策略。部署时监测任务进度，调用学习式或程序式恢复，确认场景恢复后继续执行；若缺少合适的恢复方式，则由人工示范解决并纳入学习循环。真实机器人轨迹和示范通过 DAgger 用于相应策略的训练。

Result: 在六个 LIBERO-Pro 设置和四类 MolmoSpaces 场景中，平均成功率分别为 78.8% 和 64.9%，高于最强基线的 71.7% 和 38.0%。四个真实机器人工作站并行采集后，DAgger 微调将平均成功率从 23.8% 提升至 77.5%，加入恢复技能后进一步达到 87.5%。单任务四轮采集中，人工介入率从 87.5% 降至 0%。

Conclusion: 将失败转化为可复用的恢复能力，可提升机器人任务的鲁棒性，并逐步减少对人工介入的依赖。

Abstract: Manipulation failures can leave scenes in states from which a task policy cannot recover. Learning corrective behaviors requires scalable failure exploration and physical grounding. We present Recova, an agent-guided framework that jointly develops task execution and recovery in a reconstructed digital twin, then verifies and refines both through real-world experience. In the twin, the agent diagnoses failures, tests corrective programs, and collects successful task and recovery rollouts for separate policies. During deployment, it monitors progress, invokes a learned or programmatic recovery, verifies scene restoration, and resumes execution. When no suitable recovery is available, a human demonstration resolves the failure and enters the learning loop, allowing the system to expand its recovery capabilities. Physical rollouts and human demonstrations are routed to the corresponding policy for DAgger training. Across six LIBERO-Pro settings and four MolmoSpaces categories, Recova achieves 78.8% and 64.9% mean success, compared with 71.7% and 38.0% for the strongest baselines. With parallel collection across four real-robot workstations, DAgger fine-tuning raises mean success from 23.8% to 77.5%, and recovery skills further raise it to 87.5%. Over four collection rounds on one task, observed human intervention falls from 87.5% to 0%. Together, these results show how agent-guided recovery turns failures into reusable capabilities, improving robustness while progressively reducing human intervention. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [31] [Experience-Based Feasibility-Aware Generative Adversarial Imitation from Observation under Embodiment Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01171)
*Yoshiki Takebayashi,Giovanni Perantoni,Hikaru Sasaki,Matteo Saveriano,Takamitsu Matsubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EF-GAIfO，利用机器人自身经验评估仅含状态轨迹的人类示范是否可行，并据此进行模仿学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类与机器人在身体结构和动力学上存在差异，人类示范的动作轨迹可能不适用于机器人，进而降低策略性能。现有方法可能依赖显式动力学模型或大规模先验探索数据。

Method: EF-GAIfO 从机器人自身经验中估计示范的可行性。随着策略学习推进，机器人积累更多状态转移经验，可行区域随之逐步扩展，从而逐步纳入更多示范；因此，可行性判断会适应当前学习阶段，而非依赖预先设定的标准。

Result: 研究在仿真 locomotion 任务以及真实四足机器人执行物体到达与抓取任务中验证了该方法的有效性。摘要未提供具体定量结果。

Conclusion: 基于自身经验并随学习进程更新可行性评估，可使仅含状态信息的示范更适配机器人的能力，为跨具身模仿学习提供了一种无需显式动力学模型或大规模先验数据的方法。

Abstract: With the increasing use of robot-free demonstration interfaces that provide state trajectories without action labels, imitation from observation has become a promising approach for learning robot behaviors from human demonstrations. However, due to differences in embodiment and dynamics between humans and robots, demonstrated human motions may not be feasible for the robot, potentially degrading policy performance. In this study, we propose Experience-Based Feasibility-Aware Generative Adversarial Imitation from Observation (EF-GAIfO), which estimates the feasibility of state-only demonstrations from the robot's own experience rather than relying on explicit dynamics models or large prior exploration datasets. A key feature of EF-GAIfO is that the notion of feasibility evolves with policy learning: as the policy improves and the robot experiences a broader range of state transitions, the feasible region is progressively expanded, allowing additional demonstrations to be incorporated into learning. This enables feasibility-aware imitation that adapts to the current stage of policy learning, rather than relying on a pre-designed feasibility criterion. We validate the effectiveness of EF-GAIfO on a locomotion task in simulation and on a real quadruped robot performing a object-reaching-and-grasping task.

</details>


### [32] [Extreme Length Generalization in a Compact Recurrent Architecture for One-Shot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01105)
*Izen Thornton,Aaron Shey,William Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FRANK 是一种参数量为 507K 的循环架构，在训练序列长度之外的极长序列任务上表现出较强泛化能力，并完成了从仿真到实体地面车辆的初步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人可能需要在远超训练轨迹长度的任务中持续运行，同时受限于固定的机载计算预算，因此需要兼顾长期记忆、计算效率与稳健性的架构。

Method: FRANK 将 tau 门控循环模块、内容寻址记忆和前馈反射通路结合起来。研究在参数量匹配条件下，将其与循环、状态空间及简化模块化基线进行比较；模型在长度为 5–20 的序列上训练，并在四项算法序列任务中测试，最长测试序列达两百万个 token。此外，研究通过组件消融分析各任务对不同通路的依赖，并测试仿真训练策略在实体地面车辆上的表现。

Result: 在达到最大训练长度 100,000 倍的测试长度时，FRANK 的 10 个随机种子中有 6 个仍保持 100.0% 准确率；50 个基线配置均未达到这一结果，差异具有统计显著性（双侧 Fisher 精确检验，p = 4.2×10⁻⁶）。四项任务的消融结果呈现不同组件依赖模式。另有仿真训练的 FRANK 策略在无需远程操控的情况下，驱动物理地面车辆绕过障碍并前往指定航点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，FRANK 在所测试的算法序列任务中具备显著的超长上下文泛化能力，且其不同组成部分可能按任务需求发挥不同作用；实体车辆演示则提供了初步的现实环境验证。不过，摘要未说明真实部署的覆盖范围或长期可靠性，因此不宜据此推断其已适用于广泛的自主机器人任务。

Abstract: Autonomous robots on one-shot missions run over horizons far longer than the trajectories seen during training, under a fixed onboard compute budget. We present FRANK, a 507K-parameter recurrent architecture that combines tau-gated recurrent modules, content-addressable memory, and a feedforward reflex pathway. We evaluate it against recurrent, state-space, and reduced modular baselines at matched parameter count on four algorithmic sequence tasks, trained at length 5-20 and evaluated out to two million tokens. At 100,000x the maximum training length, 6 of 10 FRANK seeds retain exactly 100.0% accuracy, while none of the 50 baseline configurations does, five architectures at ten seeds each with none left incomplete (Fisher exact, two-sided p = 4.2E-6. Targeted lesions across the four tasks yield four distinct component-reliance profiles, consistent with task-dependent allocation across the recurrent, memory, and reflex pathways. Separately, a FRANK policy trained in simulation drives a physical ground vehicle to commanded waypoints through obstacles without teleoperation.

</details>


### [33] [OrbitTAMP: Grounding Language Models for Task and Motion Planning in Spacecraft Rendezvous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01093)
*Yuji Takubo,Daniele Gammelli,Marco Pavone,Simone D'Amico*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向航天器交会与近距离操作的分层任务与运动规划框架，将大语言模型的语义理解与可复用行为图、领域规划模块及轨迹优化相结合，在保留自然语言交互能力的同时保证任务规划的物理可行性与可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交会与近距离操作规划高度依赖专家，工程师需将高层任务意图手工转换为满足动力学和操作约束的轨迹，难以实现规模化。直接使用大语言模型又可能产生缺乏轨道动力学依据、违反操作约束或不符合可执行机动结构的结果，因此需要建立语义推理与领域约束之间的可靠连接。

Method: 框架采用分层任务与运动规划架构：首先由预训练大语言模型将自然语言指令转换为部分任务规格；随后由基于可复用行为图和领域规划模块的规划器，在允许的操作空间内补全未指定决策；最后通过轨迹优化将完整任务规格转换为动力学可行的航天器轨迹。此外，研究引入验证器引导的修订机制，以及更广泛的行为—计划搜索，以提升小模型的规划质量和候选轨迹选择能力。

Result: 数值实验表明，与直接生成相比，该架构显著提升了任务意图恢复能力。在前沿大语言模型支持下，所有评测数据划分上的部分任务规格精确恢复率达到98%。对于9B规模模型，验证器引导的修订将精确恢复率从75%提升至88%；扩大行为—计划搜索范围则进一步改善了在多个可接受轨迹实现之间的选择能力。

Conclusion: 研究证明，将大语言模型限制并嵌入结构化行为库、领域规划器和轨迹优化流程，可以构建兼具语义灵活性、动力学可行性、可扩展性和可审计性的航天器RPO智能规划系统。该方法的核心贡献在于避免直接依赖LLM生成完整轨迹，而是让LLM负责高层意图解析，将安全与物理有效性留给专用规划和优化模块。

Abstract: Spacecraft rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) are currently planned through an expertise-intensive process in which engineers translate high-level operational intent into safe, dynamically feasible trajectories, creating a bottleneck to scalable operations. Large language model (LLM)-based agents could offer an intuitive interface for this process, although their outputs are not inherently grounded in orbital dynamics, operational constraints, or the structure of admissible spacecraft maneuvers. To exploit their semantic reasoning while ensuring the generated plan's physical validity, this paper presents a hierarchical framework for spacecraft task-and-motion planning (TAMP) that grounds LLM reasoning in a graph of reusable behaviors and domain-specific planning modules. Within this framework, a pretrained LLM maps a natural-language command to a partial mission specification. The associated planners then resolve unspecified decisions within the admissible operational space. Finally, trajectory optimization converts the completed mission specification into a dynamically feasible trajectory. Numerical experiments demonstrate that this architecture substantially improves intent recovery over direct LLM generation, achieving 98% exact recovery of partial mission specifications across all evaluated splits when backed by frontier LLMs. Additional test-time-compute experiments show that, for a compact 9B model, verifier-guided revision increases exact recovery from 75% to 88%, while broader behavior-plan search independently improves selection among admissible trajectory realizations. Overall, these results establish a scalable and auditable foundation for language-driven agentic planning of spacecraft RPO.

</details>


### [34] [WBAG: A Whole-Body and Attached-Geometry Safety Framework for Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01083)
*Samuel Zhen,Siwon Jo,Yanze Zhang,Wenhao Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WBAG 是一种面向视觉—语言—动作策略的机器人安全框架，通过显式建模机器人全身及随抓取变化的物体几何，提升避碰能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时安全方法多采用以末端执行器为中心的简化表示，难以充分覆盖机械臂各部件、抓取物体与环境之间的碰撞风险。

Method: WBAG 根据抓取状态构建动态安全集，并将其转化为可微控制屏障函数（CBF）约束；在尽量保留 VLA 原生六维操作空间动作的同时，对动作进行最小幅度调整，以规避机器人、场景及附着物体之间的碰撞。

Result: 在加入障碍物的 SafeLIBERO 基准上，WBAG 在所评估方法中取得最高的总体安全性与安全任务成功率：聚合场景安全率为 97.38%，安全成功率为 59.38%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，显式纳入机器人全身和抓取相关几何，有助于提升 VLA 策略在复杂场景中的安全部署能力；但安全成功率仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have demonstrated impressive capabilities in generalizable robotic manipulation, but their deployment in the real world remains challenging due to potential collisions involving different parts of the robot, manipulated objects, and the surrounding environment. Existing inference-time VLA safety frameworks typically rely on simplified end-effector-centered representations that do not explicitly model the full articulated robot and attached-object geometry. In this paper, we present WBAG, a safety framework that models the robot's whole-body and grasp-dependent attached geometry. WBAG constructs a grasp-conditioned safe set that adapts the protected geometry as objects are grasped, then converts this evolving geometry into differentiable CBF constraints that minimally modify the VLA's native six-dimensional operational-space action for collision avoidance across robot, scene, and attached geometry. On the SafeLIBERO benchmark, a variant of LIBERO augmented with obstacles for safety evaluation, WBAG achieves the best overall safety and safe task success among the evaluated methods under a scene-level safety evaluator that monitors all eligible non-task objects, reaching 97.38\% aggregate Scene Safety and 59.38\% Safe Success.

</details>


### [35] [quARtet Marker: A 3D-Printable Multi-Tag Fiducial for Robust Near-Frontal Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01072)
*Araki Wakiuchi,Hikaru Sasaki,Takamitsu Matsubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可3D打印的quARtet四标签标记，通过倾斜布置多个AprilTag提升近正视角下的物体位姿估计，同时比较不同布局对抓取性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明或反光实验室器皿难以识别和定位。单个平面标签在近正视角下姿态估计不稳定；非平面结构虽能增强透视线索，却可能占用夹爪接触所需的平坦表面。

Method: 将四个倾斜AprilTag集成于紧凑结构，并将检测到的全部角点用于同一个PnP位姿求解。通过共享配置描述制造几何与检测器模型，测试三种在位姿一致性和可抓取性之间权衡的布局，并开展固定相机估计、机器人搭载相机闭环保持及摆落抓取实验。

Result: 固定相机实验中，三种布局将近正视角平均姿态误差从单平面标签的2.18°降至0.24–0.47°，位置均方根误差从1.50 mm降至0.17–0.20 mm。闭环位姿保持实验验证了该改进。摆落测试中，带平坦接触条的两种布局滑移约2 mm；无平坦接触条的布局滑移约100 mm。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下，布局选择取决于优先目标：若位姿估计一致性最重要，可采用无平坦接触条的布局；若标记面必须便于夹持，应选择保留平坦接触条的布局。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation of labware is difficult when transparent or reflective objects must be identified and localized. Coded planar fiducials are a practical retrofit: easy to print, they leave the marked face flat and graspable. Yet a single planar tag is least reliable in near-frontal views, where perspective cues fade. Non-planar geometries restore those cues but intrude on the flat face that a parallel-jaw gripper must contact. Our idea is to tilt multiple tags within one compact footprint, so that each tag is seen at a non-frontal angle even when the marker faces the camera. We propose the quARtet marker, a 3D-printable fiducial embodying this idea: all detected corners of its four tilted AprilTags enter one Perspective-n-Point solve, and a shared configuration defines the fabricated geometry and the detector model. Because tilting consumes flat area, its three layouts trade pose-estimation consistency against graspability. In robot-referenced, same-setup fixed-camera experiments, all three layouts reduced the mean frontal orientation error from 2.18 degree for a single planar tag to 0.24-0.47 degree and the root-mean-square position error from 1.50 to 0.17-0.20 mm. A robot-mounted-camera pose-hold test confirmed this separation under closed-loop visual feedback. In swing-down trials under identical conditions, the two layouts with flat contact strips retained the object with about 2 mm of in-grasp slip, whereas the layout without flat strips slipped by roughly 100 mm. For the tested conditions, the results support a rule: the layout without flat strips when pose-estimation consistency dominates, a layout with flat strips when the marked face must remain graspable.

</details>


### [36] [Online Planning for Sparse Ground Target Search from a High-Altitude UAV under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01067)
*Ashik E Rasul,Hyung-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究利用无人机云台相机进行主动感知，以较少观测步骤发现高空视角下难以辨识的地面目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高空拍摄时，目标可能虽在视野内，却因成像尺度过小而难以检测；常规检测器受图像降采样和上下文不足影响，而城市空域限制也使无人机难以通过飞行调整视角。

Method: 将云台控制下的序贯探索建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），维护目标位置的信念状态，并采用POMCP进行规划。感知可靠性依据目标尺度建模；确定候选位置后，再使用选择性多模型集成检测进行补充判断。

Result: 在逼真仿真器的多种环境条件和飞行器状态下，方法能够检测不同成像尺度的地面目标，相比基线方法显著减少所需步骤，且对传感器分辨率的依赖较低。

Conclusion: 通过主动调节云台视场、按需增强候选区域检测，可在不依赖无人机大幅移动的情况下提升高空小目标搜索效率。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) searching for ground targets from a high altitude face a unique challenge, particularly when the target is already within the field of view but effectively unobservable because of its small apparent scale. Standard object detectors often underperform in such scenarios because of resolution downscaling and limited context. In contrast, active object search frameworks address this challenge by directing the agent to a suitable pose to gather richer visual information. However, flight regulations in urban airspace often restrict such physical movements for UAVs. As an alternative active sensing approach, the UAV can leverage the pan-tilt-zoom (PTZ) mechanism of the onboard camera to dynamically adjust its field of view and sequentially gather enhanced visual information from specific regions of interest. Once the candidate locations of the target are identified, it can deploy more expensive object detection schemes, such as an ensemble of multiple models, to get better reasoning at a fixed scale. In this work, we formulate the sequential exploration with PTZ operation as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), in which the agent maintains a belief state over the target's true location. To solve the POMDP, we deploy partially observable Monte Carlo planning (POMCP), where we condition the sensing reliability on target object scale and deploy selective ensemble detection as an additional reasoning step. We validate our methodology with experiments in a photorealistic simulator under different environmental conditions and vehicle states, showing detection of ground targets at variable scales with significantly fewer steps and minimal dependence on sensor resolution compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [37] [Divide-and-Remember: Recursive Action-Relevant Memory for Long-Horizon VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00982)
*Xuehui Yu,Eason Yu,Meiyi Wang,Haozhe Du,Stefano V. Albrecht,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Divide-and-Remember（D&R），通过学习保留与当前动作相关、且无法从当前观测中获取的历史信息，提升视觉-语言-动作模型在长时程操作任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史依赖型操作任务中，当前观测不足以决定动作，模型需要利用历史记忆。现有方法通常依据预设规则筛选记忆内容，且不同任务上的效果不稳定。

Method: 作者从部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程下的模仿学习出发，将记忆选择表述为优化问题：最大化给定当前观测时，动作与记忆之间的条件互信息 I(a_t; m_t | o_t)。据此提出递归记忆方法 D&R：将全历史的筛选递归拆分为多个对 2K 个 token 进行 top-K 选择的子问题，并在所有递归块间共享同一个轻量级选择器，以支持长上下文并降低计算开销。

Result: 在包含 16 项长时程操作任务的 RoboMME 基准上，D&R 仅使用 64 个记忆 token，便取得当前最佳平均成功率，并在四个任务套件中均有稳定提升。真实机器人实验也显示出相同的提升趋势。

Conclusion: D&R 将记忆选择建立在动作相关信息保留这一原则上，并通过递归筛选与共享选择器实现高效扩展。实验结果表明，该方法能以较小记忆预算改善长时程操作任务表现。
