Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models struggle on history-dependent manipulation tasks, where the current observation alone does not determine the action, and the policy needs a memory of the history. Existing memory methods decide what to remember by design, for example, keeping frames with large pixel changes, and show inconsistent gains across tasks. We view what to remember as an optimisation problem. From the POMDP formulation of imitation learning, we show that the optimal memory maximises the conditional mutual information $I(a_t; m_t \mid o_t)$ between the action and the memory given the current observation. Intuitively, this means preserving the action-relevant information in the history that is not already contained in the current observation. Based on our analysis, we propose Divide-and-Remember (D&R), a recursive memory method that learns a memory function $m_t = M(h_t)$ and scales to long contexts while staying compute-light. It involves two strategies: (1) the selection over the full history is divided recursively into subproblems of top-$K$ selection over $2K$ tokens, so that fixed-size, lightweight selectors learned end-to-end support an unbounded history; (2) all recursion blocks share one selector, which captures the selection rule common to every block and keeps the method efficient. On RoboMME, a benchmark of 16 long-horizon manipulation tasks that require remembering when, where, what, and how to act, D&R achieves a state-of-the-art average success rate with consistent gains across all four suites under a budget of only 64 tokens; real-robot experiments show the same gain. Code, checkpoints and more results are at this https URL

</details>


### [38] [ShowerFlex: Achieving Pseudo-Static Balancing in a Continuum Shower Hose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00936)
*Zhiyu Ren,Girish Krishnan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于坐姿淋浴辅助的被动式柔性淋浴喷头机构，以低操作力实现可调整并可保持位置的“推后停留”体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有淋浴设备通常需要使用者持续握持手持软管，或难以灵活调整固定喷头，增加操作负担与失衡风险。研究旨在改善老年人在洗浴中的自主性与便利性。

Method: 将由摩擦锁定球窝关节组成的模块化连续体机构，与弹簧卷线器结合以补偿重力；建立递归正运动学模型和静力平衡分析框架，并通过蒙特卡洛数值仿真及实体原型实验评估其适应性、稳定性和操作力。

Result: 该机构具有51个自由度。仿真显示其能适应标准浴室空间中的多种姿态，实验验证了其在重力作用下的静态稳定性，且重新定位所需操作力处于老年人的人体工学能力范围内，无需外接电子电源。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一种低成本、被动且具有内在稳定性的淋浴辅助方案，有望降低洗浴操作负担并提升使用者的自主性；其效果仍需在更广泛的实际用户与长期使用场景中进一步验证。

Abstract: With the global population rapidly aging, maintaining independence in Activities of Daily Living (ADLs), particularly bathing or showering, has become a critical challenge. Older adults who sit while showering currently have limited options, often relying on rigid overhead showerheads or flexible hand-held hoses that require continuous gripping and precarious user maneuvers, increasing the risk of falls. To address this need, this paper introduces a highly articulated, pseudo-static balanced continuum mechanism designed specifically for accessible bathing assistance. The proposed mechanism features a modular continuum architecture composed of friction-locked ball-and-socket joints (loc-line), seamlessly integrated with a retractable spring-loaded reel for effective gravity compensation. This hybrid design achieves an intuitive "Push-and-Stay" interaction logic, maintaining an approximate static equilibrium with ergonomically low actuation force, without any external electronic power, while significantly minimizing the force needed for repositioning. Theoretically, we established a recursive forward kinematics framework to construct the geometric model of the structure, coupled with a comprehensive static equilibrium analysis to evaluate the system's holding capacity across its 51-DOF structure. Numerical simulations utilizing Monte Carlo methods validate the system's morphological adaptability and stability at various extreme positions within a standard bath space. Physical experiments further validate the prototype's real-world performance, confirming its intrinsic static stability against gravity and ensuring that the actuation force remains well within the ergonomic capabilities of older adults. Ultimately, this research provides a low-cost, intrinsically safe showerhead design that reduces physical strain, restoring dignity and autonomy in personal hygiene.

</details>


### [39] [eRLT: Efficient VLA Reinforcement Learning via Action-Relevant Token Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00913)
*Dehao Huang,Jianbang Liu,Jianpan Gao,Chao Tang,Zilang Cen,Zedong Dan,Jiaheng Wang,Tingguang Li,Yue Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: eRLT从冻结的视觉—语言—动作（VLA）模型中动态提取任务相关、动作相关特征，构造用于在线强化学习的状态表示，并在仿真与真实机器人任务中提升学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线强化学习方法依赖状态表示质量，但常用的独立视觉编码器或固定压缩方案，未能明确提取最有助于下游动作优化和价值估计的VLA特征，因而限制了样本效率。

Method: eRLT使用可学习路由令牌跨多个网络层聚合视觉—语言特征，再通过轻量级层路由器将其整合为固定维度的强化学习令牌。路由模块先利用专家演示初始化，以提取能预测专家动作的特征，再根据在线交互中的评论家反馈进行优化，以支持动作价值估计。

Result: 在7项LIBERO和RoboTwin任务中，相较代表性基线，eRLT的平均归一化学习曲线AUC最高提升23.7%。在USB连接器插入和主板排线插入的真实机器人实验中，相较最强基线，AUC分别提升108.9%和46.7%。

Conclusion: 通过针对任务动态路由冻结VLA不同层与令牌中的特征，eRLT改善了在线强化学习的状态表示，并在仿真和真实机器人任务中展现出更高的学习效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide strong behavioral priors for robotic manipulation, yet efficiently adapting them to downstream tasks remains challenging. Recent work addresses this challenge by adapting frozen VLAs through online reinforcement learning (RL), whose sample efficiency depends on the quality of the state representation used by the actor and critic. Existing methods construct such representations either with VLA-independent visual encoders or through fixed compression of internal VLA representations. Neither design explicitly extracts the task-specific action-relevant VLA features most useful for downstream action refinement and action-value estimation, therefore limiting sample efficiency. To address this limitation, we introduce eRLT, which constructs an effective state representation by routing task-specific action-relevant information across both tokens and layers of the frozen VLA. Specifically, learned routing tokens dynamically aggregate visual-language features at multiple depths, while a lightweight layer router combines these summaries into a fixed-dimensional RL token. The routing module is initialized using expert demonstrations to capture features predictive of expert actions and then refined using critic feedback from online interactions for action-value estimation. Across seven LIBERO and RoboTwin tasks, eRLT improves mean normalized learning-curve AUC by up to 23.7% over representative baselines. Real-robot experiments on USB connector insertion and motherboard ribbon-cable insertion further show AUC improvements of 108.9% and 46.7%, respectively, over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [40] [Screw Attention: Rigid-Body Algebra Inside a Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00904)
*Aly Magassouba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Screw Attention 将刚体间的相对位姿与关节螺旋轴融入注意力机制，在多项模拟操作任务中表现出较高成功率，并对坐标系变化保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规学习策略需从数据中重新学习刚体力学已明确描述的空间关系，因此数据需求较高，且面对场景几何变化时容易失效。

Method: 将每个 token 表示为具有位姿的刚体，并以相对位姿及关节螺旋轴描述 token 间关系。消息沿空间变换传递至接收者坐标系，注意力分数则仅依赖坐标系不变的量。该设计使消息对各 token 的独立坐标系变换保持等变，且单层即可表达刚体力学中的速度递推。

Result: 在模拟操作任务中，该方法在参数量相近时达到或超过图网络、Transformer 和扁平网络等基线。在 LIBERO-Spatial 上，仅使用物体位姿的 16,162 参数模型成功率为 97.3%，超过参数量多 27 倍的扁平网络。改变每个连杆的坐标系约定后，其成功率不变，而其他学习网络均降至 3% 以下。作为解析控制器的门控残差模块时，插入任务成功率提高 17.3 个百分点；对最高 10 毫米的位姿噪声及 Franka 机械臂出厂标定范围内的关节偏差也保持稳定。

Conclusion: 研究表明，当任务依赖的跨坐标系几何关系未由系统其他部分提供时，显式纳入几何结构尤为关键。Screw Attention 以较小模型实现了较强的任务表现与坐标系鲁棒性。

Abstract: Learned manipulation policies rediscover from data the spatial relations that rigid-body mechanics supplies in closed form. This costs data, and it leaves the policies fragile to geometric changes in the scene. We present Screw Attention, a transformer layer in which the relation between two bodies is a spatial transform rather than a graph edge. Every token is a body with a pose. Each pair of tokens carries the relative pose and, for robot joints, the joint screw. Messages are transported along this relation into the receiver's frame, while the attention scores see only frame-invariant quantities. By construction, the messages are equivariant to an independent change of frame at every token, and a single layer can express the velocity recursion of rigid-body mechanics. On simulated manipulation tasks, Screw Attention matches or outperforms controls of the same size, including graph, transformer and flat networks on LIBERO-Spatial. With 16,162 parameters it reaches 97.3% on LIBERO-Spatial from object poses (without images or language), above a flat network with 27x more parameters. Under a change of per-link frame convention its success is unchanged, while every other learned network falls below 3%. Placed on an analytic controller as a gated residual, it raises insertion success by 17.3 points. It is unaffected by pose noise up to 10,mm and by joint offsets within the factory calibration of a Franka arm. These results suggest a criterion: geometry is decisive when the task requires relations between frames that no other part of the system supplies. Code and trained policies will be released.

</details>


### [41] [Real-Time Human-Adaptive Task Allocation for Multi-Human Multi-Robot Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00897)
*Seabin Lee,Sujeong Park,Nayoung Kim,Sungjin Park,Haechan Jung,Changjoo Nam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种考虑操作者实时认知状态的多机器人监督任务分配方法，以动态调节监督能力、平衡工作负荷并提升团队表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常假设每位操作者的监督能力固定，忽略工作负荷与疲劳的波动，可能造成部分操作者过载、其他人利用不足。

Method: 根据操作者当前监督能力及不同任务所需工作量分配机器人监督任务；采用带任务优先级的贪心策略，尽量降低估计工作负荷并维持负荷平衡。

Result: 用户研究按预设时间区间进行分析，结果显示，相较于不考虑人为因素的基线方法，该方法持续取得更高表现，且行为层面的疲劳迹象更少。

Conclusion: 动态调整操作者监督能力有助于预防过载，并在持续时间长、需求高的场景中维持操作者表现。

Abstract: We propose a human-factor-aware method of allocating robot supervision tasks to multiple human operators. In scenarios where multiple operators occasionally teleoperate multiple robots to help the robots overcome difficulties, the allocation of the supervisory control tasks to humans needs to consider the real-time cognitive states of individual operators. However, most existing methods assume fixed supervisory capacity per operator and overlook fluctuations in the human factors such as workload and fatigue. As a result, workload distribution can be unbalanced where some operators become overloaded while the others remain underused. Our method dynamically regulates supervisory capacity and allocates tasks in a way that maintains balanced mental workload, prevents overload, and improves overall team performance. The allocation method uses a greedy strategy that minimizes estimated operator workloads with task prioritization. Robots are assigned to operators by reflecting their current supervisory capacity where the required effort depends on the types of tasks. In the user study, the analysis across predefined time intervals shows that the proposed method consistently achieves higher performance and lower behavioral signs of fatigue compared to a baseline method that does not consider human factors. These results highlight adaptive capacity adjustment as an effective preventive mechanism for sustaining operator performance in long-duration, high-demand settings.

</details>


### [42] [Are Frontier VLM Agents Ready to Be Robot Generalists? An Empirical Study with the Embodied Agent Arena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00854)
*Haojian Huang,Pukun Zhao,Zexi Li,Yehang Zhang,Yangkai Wei,Wenqian Li,Han Yang,Kaiwen Zhou,Ying-Cong Chen,Yinchuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出具身智能体评测平台 Embodied Agent Arena，用于分析视觉语言模型（VLM）在机器人任务中的局部能力如何转化为完整任务成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单项场景估计、交互定位与动作能力，并不能直接说明模型能否完成完整机器人任务；评估模型迈向通用机器人能力的准备程度，需要检验局部能力与任务成功之间的差距。

Method: 构建包含1,000个案例的评测集，覆盖几何、空间推理、可供性、任务规划和操作，案例来自32个既有来源及新建的 GeoProbe 几何估计基准。采用尽量保留原始观测与操作方式的统一评测框架，并区分度量精度、功能性定位和原生目标完成；评估七种VLM，同时比较更丰富观测的执行协议与多轮复核。

Result: Astra在精确估计和可用于操作的接触位置定位方面优势最明显。评测也显示，丰富观测与多轮复核是考察模型执行能力的协议维度；摘要未提供具体数值或各模型的完整排名。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在部分几何估计和交互定位任务上已展现优势，但将这些局部能力转化为协调且目标导向的连续动作，仍是实现机器人通用能力的主要障碍。

Abstract: Frontier vision-language models (VLMs) combine scene estimation, interaction grounding, and executable actions. Understanding how these abilities support complete robotic tasks is central to evaluating their readiness as robot generalists. We introduce Embodied Agent Arena to examine where local competence supports, or falls short of, complete task success across Geometry, Spatial Reasoning, Affordance, Task Planning, and Manipulation. The arena contains 1,000 cases drawn from 32 established sources and GeoProbe, our new benchmark for geometric estimation on Blender renders and real-scene images. A minimal harness preserves source observations and operations while separating metric precision, functional grounding, and native goal completion. We evaluate seven VLMs, analyze Astra's task-specific advantages, and compare richer-observation execution protocols and multi-round review. Across the arena, Astra's advantage is strongest in precise estimation and usable-contact localization; completing coordinated, goal-directed actions remains the key gap to robot generalism.

</details>


### [43] [Reactive Humanoid Multi-Contact Using Learned Stability Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00823)
*Stephen McCrory,Beomyeong Park,Nicholas Kitchel,Nehar Poddar,Robert Griffin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用手部支撑接触提升人形机器人稳定性的快速规划与控制方法，并通过仿真和硬件实验验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低稳定性情形下，仅依靠足部接触可能无法避免跌倒；传统优化方法评估候选手部接触点的效率较低，因此需要快速选择有效的支撑位置。

Method: 在机器人可达范围内采样候选接触点，并通过质心动力学预测碰撞前、碰撞及碰撞后的运动。依据碰撞后质心压力中心（CoP）的控制能力为候选点评分。方法利用学习得到的碰撞后CoP区域模型加速评估，并分两阶段规划：先选择支撑区域，再确定区域内的支撑点。

Result: 仿真中，相较于不使用手部接触的恢复方式，抗冲击能力平均提高89%；相较于选择最近可达区域的朴素策略，提高17%。硬件站立推力测试中，稳定时间较朴素手部放置策略平均减少43%；行走测试中，恢复表现较不使用手部接触的基线提高18%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够快速规划手部支撑位置，并在仿真及站立、行走硬件测试中改善机器人受扰后的稳定能力；其核心优势在于利用学习模型高效评估碰撞后的CoP控制能力。

Abstract: We present a planning and control approach to reactively use hand contacts to stabilize a humanoid in low stability scenarios, where only using feet contacts may result in a fall. Candidate contacts are sampled within the robot's reachable workspace, and a preview is computed by rolling out the centroidal dynamics through pre-impact, impact and post-impact phases. Sampled points are scored based on the Center of Pressure (CoP) control authority at the post-impact phase. Central to our approach is a learned model of the robot's CoP region during post-impact, which enables rapid evaluation of candidate contact points compared to traditional optimization-based methods. The presented planner has two stages: the first selects an optimal bracing region and the second computes an optimal bracing point within the region. Our simulation results demonstrate an average increase in impulse resilience of 89% over recovery without hand contacts and 17% over a naive planning strategy (closest reachable region). We validate our framework on hardware, performing push tests while standing and walking. The standing trials show an average 43% reduction in stabilization time compared to naive hand placement and the walking trials demonstrate a 18% reduction compared to baseline recovery (without hand contacts).

</details>


### [44] [TRACE: Privacy-Preserving Next-Best-View Selection over Distributed 3D Gaussian-Splat Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00822)
*Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Athanasios Cosse,Qiyu Sun,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACE 让多机器人在不共享各自 3D Gaussian Splatting 地图的情况下，协同进行下一最佳视角选择；其视角信息增益接近集中式方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式视角选择需要评估其他机器人地图对本机地图的遮挡与辐射贡献，但汇集完整地图会损害隐私，且通信量随地图规模增长。

Method: 作者证明地图间的耦合可由射线上的前向透射率与后向辐射量表示，二者均可按机器人分解。各机器人沿候选视角的射线，在自身地图的深度分箱中计算这两类量及其位姿导数并发送；规划机器人据此计算信息增益及其在 SO(3) 上的梯度。

Result: 理论上，除非同一 splat 后方的深度分箱混合了不同机器人的命中，否则重建精确；混合时作者给出误差界。在 Habitat-Sim 的 100 多次决策中，TRACE 有 83.3% 的选向与集中式结果相差不超过 15 度，所得视角达到集中式信息增益的 97.9%。

Conclusion: TRACE 以固定规模的聚合量替代地图共享，在保护地图隐私、避免通信量随地图增长的同时，实现了接近集中式性能的协同视角规划。

Abstract: Share the light, not the map. We study next-best-view selection for a team of robots, each of which builds its own 3D Gaussian Splatting map and keeps it private. A robot picks the view with the largest expected information gain (EIG) about the splats along its own path. This gain depends on the other maps. Their splats occlude its own and shine behind them, so the gain has to be evaluated against the pooled map. No robot has this map. We show that the coupling passes through only two ray quantities, the transmittance in front of a splat and the radiance behind it, and that both are sums over the hits of the ray. Hence, they decompose across the robots, and each robot sums them over depth bins in its own map, along the rays of a candidate view, and sends the sums with their pose derivatives. The robot planning the view turns them into its EIG and gradient on SO(3). Transmittance and Radiance Aggregates, communicated for the EIG, give the protocol its name: TRACE. No robot shares its splats, and the message size does not grow with a map. We prove that the reconstruction is exact unless a depth bin behind a splat mixes hits of two robots, and we bound the error otherwise. Over 100 next-best-view decisions in Habitat-Sim, TRACE picks a heading within 15 degrees of the centralized one in 83.3% of the cases, and its views reach 97.9% of the centralized EIG.

</details>


### [45] [Getting Out and Getting Back: World and Behavior Grounding in Real2Sim2Real Co-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00821)
*Samuel Liu,Youngsun Kim,Martin Matak,Gilwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究分别考察模拟与真实世界的对齐程度及模拟行为与人类动作的相似度，发现二者结合可显著提升机器人策略表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟数据可弥补真实示范稀缺，但世界保真度和行为相似度各自如何影响策略性能尚不明确。

Method: 构建 real2sim2real 流程，独立调节世界对齐与行为对齐，并在动态灵巧抓取与分类任务中使用生成的模拟数据进行协同训练；通过潜在空间分析研究部署策略的行为来源。

Result: 完全对齐的协同训练将成功率从 52% 提升至 86%。跨配置平均而言，世界对齐带来 18 个百分点的提升，行为对齐带来 10 个百分点的提升。策略在真实数据覆盖的状态中更像真实示范，在覆盖范围之外则更多依赖模拟行为。

Conclusion: 世界对齐帮助策略利用真实数据覆盖范围之外的模拟经验；当世界对齐不充分时，行为对齐尤为重要。即使训练基础模型，对齐良好的模拟数据仍有价值。

Abstract: Simulation can expand scarce real demonstrations for co-training, yet how world fidelity and similarity to human behavior affect policy performance remains unclear. We distinguish world grounding, which aligns simulation with the real system, and behavior grounding, which aligns simulated trajectories with human motion. We build a real2sim2real pipeline that varies these axes independently to generate data for co-training. On a dynamic dexterous pick-and-sort task, fully grounded co-training raises success from 52% to 86%; averaged across configurations, world grounding improves success by 18 percentage points and behavior grounding by 10. Deployed policies behave like a mixture of real-derived and simulation-derived policies, imitating real demonstrations in covered states and relying on simulated behavior elsewhere, which we examine through latent-space analysis. Together, these results suggest complementary roles: world grounding lets policies use simulated experience beyond real-data coverage, while behavior grounding matters mainly when world grounding is imperfect. Grounded simulation remains beneficial when co-training foundation models.

</details>


### [46] [ECoMEM: Explicit Concept Memory for Memory-Dependent Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00801)
*Yize Liu,Ke Wang,Mac Schwager,Yiqing Xu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ECoMEM 为视觉-语言-动作策略提供显式、可复用的概念记忆，在多数基准任务及新真实机器人任务中显著提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略依赖当前观测或较长历史，难以保留后续行动所需的信息；动作监督也未明确教会策略哪些事实应被记住，以及如何随新证据更新。

Method: 将记忆维护与行动学习分离。ECoMEM 使用由证据支撑的概念库记录任务相关历史，由 Writer 选择和更新记录，再由学习得到的 Reader 将其转化为直接条件化视觉-语言-动作策略的记忆令牌。

Result: 在 RoboMME 的 16 项任务中，ECoMEM 在 15 项上优于所评估的机器人策略。在两项新的真实机器人任务中，记忆库可直接迁移，或仅需新增一个概念；成功率达到 86.1%，无记忆策略为 8.6%。

Conclusion: 显式概念记忆为机器人控制提供了可复用、可扩展的记忆接口，有助于支持长时程行为并促进跨任务迁移。

Abstract: A robot may lose sight of an object it must later retrieve, need to recall what a person demonstrated earlier, or track which steps of a task it has already completed. Current vision-language-action (VLA) policies often fail once the information needed for action disappears from the current observation, making memory critical for long-horizon robot behavior. Existing approaches typically provide longer histories or learn implicit memory from observation-action trajectories. But action supervision tells a policy how to act, not what to remember: it does not specify which past facts should persist or how they should change as new evidence arrives. We therefore separate maintaining an evidence-grounded account of the past from learning how to act on it. This insight motivates Explicit Concept Memory (ECoMEM), which represents task-relevant history with a reusable library of grounded concepts. An evidence-based Writer selects and updates these records, while a learned Reader turns them into memory tokens that directly condition the VLA. Across 16 RoboMME tasks, ECoMEM leads the evaluated robot policies on 15 tasks. On two new real-robot tasks, the same memory library either transfers directly or requires only one new concept, achieving 86.1% success versus 8.6% for a no-memory VLA. These results show that explicit concepts provide a reusable and extensible memory interface for robot control. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [47] [DITTO-X: Forward and Reverse Teleoperation for Dexterous Manipulation and Human Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00781)
*Zhanpeng He,Joaquin Palacios,Zhangyu Wang,Chenhao Li,Katelyn Lee,Matei Ciocarlie,C. Karen Liu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DITTO-X 是一种适配多种灵巧手的遥操作接口，通过力觉反馈与反向遥操作提升示范采集和策略干预效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作多依赖视觉反馈，并围绕平行夹爪设计；在视野受遮挡、任务进行中需要接管灵巧手时，操作者难以准确感知接触状态并匹配机器人当前手部姿态。

Method: DITTO-X 利用机器人手部已有传感信息，向操作者呈现关节级力反馈和指尖接触事件，并支持驱动 Sharpa、Wuji 和 Inspire 三种商用灵巧手，无需针对每种手重新设计。其可执行反向遥操作，在控制权转移前由机器人带动操作者手指匹配机器人姿态。

Result: 在常规数据采集以及策略部署期间针对接触丰富型操作任务的人类干预中，DITTO-X 相较商用手部追踪手套提升了示范质量和采集效率。

Conclusion: 将力觉反馈、多手型兼容和姿态匹配纳入遥操作，有望改善灵巧操作数据采集与部署时的人机接管；摘要未提供具体提升幅度或实验细节。

Abstract: Teleoperated demonstrations are a primary source of data for robot manipulation, and teleoperated interventions are a primary mechanism for correcting policies at deployment. Yet most teleoperation systems close the loop through vision alone and are built around parallel-jaw grippers, limiting both what the robot can execute and what the operator can express through it. This is most damaging in shared autonomy, where the operator sees the scene only through occluded cameras and must take over a dexterous hand mid-task, often with an object already grasped. We present DITTO-X, a hand-agnostic dexterous teleoperation interface that renders joint-level force and fingertip contact events from sensing already on the robot hand, and drives three commercial dexterous hands (Sharpa, Wuji, and Inspire) without per-hand redesign. Because the exoskeleton is actuated, DITTO-X also supports reverse teleoperation, in which the robot back-drives the operator's fingers into its own configuration before control is transferred, so the human enters the loop already matched to the state they inherit. Our results show that DITTO-X improves demonstration quality and throughput over a commercial hand-tracking glove, both in regular data collection and in human intervention during policy deployment for contact-rich manipulation tasks. More information can be found from our website: this https URL .

</details>


### [48] [Measuring Asset and Scene Reconstruction Effects in Real-to-Sim Robot Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00731)
*Sanya Verma,Luca Cilio,Velissarios Christodoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究比较两种机器人仿真环境重建方案与真实评测结果的一致性；作者构建的重建方案表现优于默认方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真评测成本低且便于重复，但其价值取决于仿真结果能否反映真实机器人的表现。研究旨在检验更精确的几何、物理参数和场景重建能否缩小仿真与现实之间的差异。

Method: 为同一双臂机器人工作单元构建两种仿真环境：作者方案采用估算的公制尺度物体几何、投影纹理、人工设定的物理参数和自建场景重建；默认方案采用开源流程生成的单图网格、引擎默认物理参数及高斯泼溅场景。两种方案使用相同的物体照片和场景视频。两种策略分别执行五项任务，形成十个任务—策略组合；每个组合在每种环境中运行二十次，并与同一组真实试验评分进行比较。

Result: 作者方案的仿真均值与真实评分的皮尔逊相关系数为0.90，默认方案为0.51。平均评分误差分别为6.97和17.54个百分点，作者方案减少10.56个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，更高质量的环境重建与物理参数设定可提升仿真对真实表现的吻合度。不过，结论基于十个任务—策略组合，适用范围仍需在更多任务和机器人上验证。研究公开了评测工具、逐次评分、运行配置以及两种环境的资产与场景。

Abstract: Simulated evaluation is increasingly used alongside real-world evaluation of robot policies because it is cheaper and easier to repeat; however, its value depends on how closely its outcomes track the real robot's. We test whether our reconstruction pipeline, combining metrically scaled object geometry, authored physical parameters and scene reconstruction, reduces disagreement between simulated and real robot scores relative to a default open-source recipe. We constructed two simulated versions of one bimanual robot cell: an authored reconstruction, using object geometry at estimated metric scale, projected textures, authored physics and our own scene splat; and a baseline, referred to as the default reconstruction, using the open-source recipe of a generative single-image mesh, engine-default physics and a Gaussian-splat scene. Both reconstructions use the same object photographs and scene video. Two policies ran five tasks each, giving ten task-policy pairs, which we call cells; each cell was run twenty times in each reconstruction with all other settings held fixed. Both reconstructions were scored against the same real trials, graded by a third-party evaluator. Pearson correlation between the ten simulated and real cell means is r = 0.90 for the authored reconstruction and 0.51 for the default. Mean score error is 6.97 percentage points for the authored reconstruction and 17.54 for the default, a reduction of 10.56 percentage points. These results show that improving the quality of the environment reconstruction through higher visual fidelity, authored physics and metric scale makes the simulation more faithful to the real world and narrows the sim-to-real gap. We release the harness, the per-trial scores, every reported run's configuration, and the assets and scenes of both reconstructions.

</details>


### [49] [Toward Humanoid Robots in Construction: A Teleoperation Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00718)
*Parastoo Ali Pour,David R. Martin,Chang Min Hur,Bo Zhang,Tommy Zhou,Brandon Thomas Lichter,Shane Stanfield,Pramod Khargonekar,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向建筑任务的人形机器人遥操作系统，结合 XR 上肢控制与脚踏式移动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对建筑行业劳动力短缺、作业环境风险及人形机器人自主能力不足，探索可近期应用的遥操作方案，以减轻工人身体负担并采集高质量示范数据。

Method: 在 Unitree G1 上实现单人遥操作，使操作者能够同步控制机器人移动与操作；选取 O*NET 数据库中的两类代表性建筑任务，并与人工操作基线比较任务成功率和完成时间。

Result: 工具运输任务成功率为 100%，表面涂装任务成功率为 80%；遥操作完成任务所需时间显著长于人工操作。

Conclusion: 该系统展示了遥操作执行建筑任务的可行性，但当前效率低于人工操作，仍需改进控制效率与任务表现。

Abstract: We present a teleoperation system that enables a single operator to perform construction tasks on a Unitree G1 humanoid, combining extended reality (XR) based upper body control with pedal-based locomotion to enable simultaneous manipulation and locomotion. Motivated by persistent labor shortages, hazardous working conditions, and challenges in humanoid autonomy, we investigate teleoperation as a practical near-term approach for reducing physical strain on workers while generating high quality demonstration data. We evaluate the system on two representative construction tasks drawn from O*NET occupational database, and report task success and completion time relative to a manual baseline. The system achieved 100% success on tool transport and 80% success on surface painting, with teleoperation requiring substantially more time compared to manual execution.

</details>


### [50] [TacDyn-WAM: Learning Implicit Tactile Dynamics in a Heterogeneous Visuo-Tactile World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00638)
*Enyi Wang,Mingxin Wang,Quan Shi,Hetian Guo,Hongyu Wang,Xi Wang,Bin Qian,Yupeng Zheng,Wenxuan Song,Houde Liu,Yong Xu,Cheng Chi,Wenchao Ding,Yilun Chen,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacDyn-WAM通过预测隐式触觉动力学而非重建未来触觉图像，提升了机器人操作在接触变化和部署漂移下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉世界动作模型多沿用视频生成流程，以迭代去噪重建未来触觉观测；接触位置或力度的微小变化可能显著改变触觉像素，使预测在部署漂移下不可靠。

Method: 提出异构视触觉世界动作模型TacDyn-WAM。其TacRep通过触觉片段的时空掩码预测学习，并以关系结构蒸馏正则化。视觉专家与隐式触觉动力学专家在各自的目标空间预测未来表征，并通过联合注意力交互；触觉专家单次前向预测多个时间跨度的未来表征及其变化，当前触觉状态则由只读触觉记忆提供。

Result: 在UniVTAC上，仅使用给定演示数据的平均成功率为81.5%，达到当前先进水平，并与使用大规模视触觉轨迹预训练的模型表现相近。五项真实机器人任务的平均成功率为71.0%；采用中等规模预训练后提升至85.0%。消融实验支持两条触觉路径及TacRep的有效性。

Conclusion: TacDyn-WAM以对动力学更稳健的隐式触觉表征替代像素级未来重建，在基准测试和真实机器人操作中取得有竞争力的表现；结果表明，预测触觉演化而非精确复现触觉图像具有实际价值。

Abstract: World action models improve robotic manipulation by conditioning actions on predicted futures, yet existing tactile variants largely inherit video-generation pipelines that reconstruct future tactile observations through iterative denoising. Such prediction can become unreliable under deployment drift: small changes in contact position or force may substantially alter tactile pixels even when the underlying contact evolution remains predictable. We introduce TacDyn-WAM, a heterogeneous visuo-tactile world action model that predicts implicit tactile dynamics rather than reconstructing future tactile observations. It learns TacRep, a dynamics-aware tactile target space trained through masked spatio-temporal prediction on tactile clips and regularized by relational structure distillation. A visual expert and an Implicit Tactile Dynamics Expert predict future visual and tactile representations in separate target spaces while interacting through joint attention; the tactile expert predicts future representations and their changes at multiple horizons in a single forward pass, and a read-only tactile memory supplies the current tactile state. On UniVTAC, TacDyn-WAM achieves an average success rate of 81.5% using only the provided demonstrations, reaching state-of-the-art-level performance and remaining competitive with models pretrained on large-scale visuo-tactile trajectories. Ablations confirm the benefits of both tactile pathways and TacRep over pixel-reconstruction and static alternatives. On five real-robot tasks, TacDyn-WAM reaches 71.0% average success, and modest-scale pretraining raises it to 85.0%, further validating our method.

</details>


### [51] [When Reasoning Helps Action: Monitoring and Steering Chain-of-Thought in Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00601)
*Sathwik Karnik,Joseph JR. Lee,Aryaman Gupta,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究提出 TRUST，通过监测并引导视觉—语言—动作（VLA）模型的思维链推理，评估推理修正能否提升推理正确性及实际行为表现。结果显示，推理准确率提升并不必然带来任务表现改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型的思维链可能成为运行时安全干预接口，但推理是否可被有效修正，以及修正是否能转化为预期行为，尚需分别评估。

Method: 提出两个评估维度：可纠正性衡量能否在生成过程中发现并改善不可靠推理；可行动性衡量推理修正能否引发方向一致且有意义的行为变化。设计离线训练的 TRUST 价值模型，根据部分推理前缀预测最终推理正确性，并用于监测和选择性引导冻结的 VLA 策略；在驾驶与操作任务上进行评估。

Result: 在 Alpamayo 1.5 上，TRUST 的推理正确性监测准确率为 88.9%，并将推理正确率从 75.9% 提升至 90.0%；在 AlpaSim 的基线定义困难子集上，相比未引导策略，碰撞率降低 30.4%，最大轨迹误差降低 11.5%，且优于计算量匹配的 Best-of-4 基线。在 DeepThinkVLA 上，抓取状态判断正确率从 69.3% 升至 90.2%，动作选择判断正确率从 68.8% 升至 85.9%，但 LIBERO-Plus 闭环任务表现基本未变。

Conclusion: TRUST 能提升不同 VLA 模型的推理正确性，但其行为收益取决于推理修正是否能传导至行动：驾驶实验呈现与意图一致的行为改善，操作实验则较为有限。因此，将思维链用于运行时安全时，应同时评估可纠正性与可行动性，不能仅凭推理准确率判断实际效益。

Abstract: Reasoning-enabled VLA policies expose chain-of-thought (CoT) traces that appear to explain and guide their actions, creating a potential interface for runtime safety through reasoning monitoring and correction. In this work, we define and operationalize two evaluation axes for assessing when this interface can improve embodied behavior: correctability, which measures whether unreliable reasoning can be detected and improved during generation, and actionability, which measures whether reasoning corrections produce behaviorally meaningful changes in the intended direction. To enable correctability, we introduce Token-level Reward for Utility-Steered Chain-of-Thought (TRUST), an offline-trained value model that predicts eventual reasoning correctness from partial prefixes and uses these estimates to monitor and selectively steer reasoning generation in frozen VLA policies. On the Alpamayo 1.5 driving VLA, TRUST monitors correctness with 88.9% accuracy and improves reasoning correctness from 75.9% to 90.0%. On a baseline-defined challenging subset in AlpaSim, TRUST reduces collision rate by 30.4% and maximum trajectory error by 11.5% relative to the unsteered policy, outperforming a compute-matched Best-of-4 baseline. On the DeepThinkVLA manipulation VLA, TRUST improves the correctness of grasp-state claims from 69.3% to 90.2% and action-choice claims from 68.8% to 85.9%, yet closed-loop task performance on LIBERO-Plus remains largely unchanged. Empirical analysis reveals intent-consistent behavioral effects in Alpamayo 1.5 but limited effects in DeepThinkVLA, helping interpret these different task-level outcomes. Together, our results show that gains in reasoning correctness do not automatically imply gains in embodied performance, motivating evaluation of correctability and actionability when using CoT as a runtime safety interface.

</details>


### [52] [Token-World: World Modeling in Vision-Language Model Token Space for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00575)
*Chuyao Fu,Xiaowei Chi,Yuhan Rui,Yu-kai Wang,Zezhong Qian,Xiaojie Zhang,Yunfan Lou,Kevin Zhang,Kuangzhi Ge,Chak Wing Mak,Zhiyang Chen,Athena Zhuoming Zhong,Hongyang Chen,Haoran Li,Yike Guo,Sirui Han,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Token-World 将 VLM 视觉特征压缩为紧凑状态，在该状态空间中预测动作条件下的未来动态，再还原为策略可用表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 世界模型通常先预测未来 RGB 图像，再重新编码供策略使用，形成间接接口；而直接建模高维 VLM 视觉 token 又面临效率和准确性挑战。

Method: 提出 Token-World：压缩 VLM 特征以构建紧凑 token 状态，在压缩空间中学习动作条件动态，并将预测状态映射回原始的策略输入表示。

Result: 在操作基准上，Token-World 提升了开环特征保真度和策略动作一致性，且长时序滚动中的性能退化更慢。闭环评估中，其模拟策略表现与参考策略表现的相关性高于 Ctrl-World（r=0.794 对比 0.583），同时模拟延迟更低。消融实验显示，紧凑表示的设计与维度会显著影响未来状态预测。

Conclusion: 在紧凑、面向策略的特征空间中建模世界动态，可提升模拟质量、闭环评估相关性与推理效率；状态表示设计是影响预测效果的重要因素。

Abstract: A common approach to world-model simulation for vision-language-action (VLA) systems is to predict future RGB observations and then re-encode them into policy inputs, introducing an indirect interface between simulation and downstream policy execution. We instead investigate whether world dynamics can be modeled in a compact, policy-oriented state derived from VLM visual tokens. A key challenge is that raw VLM visual tokens are high-dimensional, making efficient and accurate autoregressive dynamics modeling challenging. To address this, we introduce Token-World, an action-conditioned world model that compresses VLM features into a compact token state, learns future dynamics in this reduced space, and maps predicted states back to the original policy-facing representation for downstream use. Across manipulation benchmarks, Token-World improves open-loop feature fidelity and policy-action consistency over recent world-model simulators, with slower degradation over long rollout horizons. In closed-loop evaluation, its simulated policy performance correlates more strongly with reference policy performance than Ctrl-World ($r=0.794$ vs.\ $0.583$), while requiring lower simulation latency. Ablations further show that compact-representation design and dimensionality substantially affect future-state prediction. Code will be available at this https URL .

</details>


### [53] [Does Continual Imitation Learning Remain Grounded? A Language-Perturbed Benchmark for Robotic Task Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00542)
*Siddeshwar Raghavan,Ziqin Yuan,Fengqing Zhu,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一套评估协议，用于检验机器人在持续模仿学习中保留技能的同时，是否仍能依据语言指令正确行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习指标关注任务表现与遗忘程度，却无法判断策略是否真正受语言指令引导；策略可能依赖场景线索、物体关联或记忆的任务结构。

Method: 为 LIBERO 的 Goal、Spatial、Object 和 Long 套件构造保持语义或改变语义的指令变体；在策略实验中，重点评估 LIBERO-Goal，并在持续学习各阶段测试原始指令与释义指令。比较具有代表性的持续模仿学习方法，同时区分任务能力与语言敏感性，并评估语义鲁棒性、目标适应能力和语言敏感性。

Result: 实验显示，持续学习表现出色并不必然意味着语言 grounding 可靠；保留已学技能的策略仍可能无法正确响应其指令。

Conclusion: 该协议补充了标准学习与遗忘指标，可用于判断持续学习后的技能是否仍受指令正确引导，并揭示仅凭任务表现评估语言条件策略的局限。

Abstract: Continual imitation learning evaluates whether a robot can learn new knowledge without forgetting previously learned skills. However, retaining task performance does not ensure the behavior remains grounded in language because policies may rely on scene cues, object associations, or memorized task structure. We introduce a benchmark protocol to study how language-guided behavior changes as robotic policies learn successive tasks. We construct meaning-preserving and meaning-changing instruction variants for the Goal, Spatial, Object, and Long suites of LIBERO. Policy experiments focus on LIBERO-Goal, evaluating Original and Paraphrase instructions after each continual-learning stage. We compare representative continual imitation learning methods under their original assumptions while separating task competence from language sensitivity. The proposed diagnostics complement standard learning and forgetting metrics by measuring semantic robustness, goal adaptation, and language sensitivity. Results show that strong continual-learning performance does not always translate to reliable language grounding, and our diagnostics help determine whether retained skills remain correctly guided by their instructions. Additional materials are available at this https URL

</details>


### [54] [Towards a General Humanoid Loco-Manipulation Model via Egocentric Whole-Body Human Data Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00438)
*Chongyang Xu,Zhao Wu,Jin Chen,Yiming Jiang,Jinhui Ye,Yuming Jiang,Shifeng Zhang,Ziliang Feng,Mu Xu,Yilun Chen,Li Lu,Steven C.H. Hoi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 HumanVerse-500 数据集和 λ₀ 全身人形机器人视觉-语言-动作策略，利用人类日常活动数据提升机器人全身移动与操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一人称视频监督对全身动作及其与手部物体交互的协同覆盖不足；通过人形机器人遥操作采集此类数据又成本高、难以规模化。

Method: 构建包含 500 小时开放环境人类移动操作行为的 HumanVerse-500，以轻量可穿戴系统同步采集第一人称视频、身体动作和手部动作。基于该数据训练 λ₀：先从多样化第一人称数据学习交互，再用 HumanVerse-500 学习身体与手部协同，最后针对下游任务和机器人本体进行适配；通过共享表示空间迁移人类经验，并用专用接口处理人机状态与动作差异。

Result: λ₀ 在 SIMPLE 基准和 4 项真实世界移动操作任务上取得当前最佳表现。论文还分析了其扩展性、泛化能力以及不同训练阶段的贡献。

Conclusion: 大规模、覆盖全身动作的人类经验数据有望支持可扩展的人形机器人全身控制学习；作者计划公开数据集、代码和模型。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body manipulation has advanced rapidly, enabling policies to coordinate locomotion, posture, bimanual interaction, and dexterous hand movements. Meanwhile, egocentric human videos provide diverse examples of everyday interactions across objects and scenes, offering scalable supervision without robot operation. However, existing supervision from these videos provides limited coverage of whole-body movement and coordination with hand-object interaction, while obtaining such supervision through humanoid teleoperation is also costly and difficult to scale. We therefore explore how human experience can support scalable learning of humanoid loco-manipulation. To support this study, we introduce HumanVerse-500, a 500-hour dataset of diverse human loco-manipulation behaviors in open-world environments, collected with a lightweight wearable system that synchronizes egocentric video with body and hand motion. Building on this dataset, we develop $\lambda_0$, a whole-body humanoid vision-language-action policy, through three-stage training that first learns interaction from diverse egocentric datasets, then coordinates body and hand motion using HumanVerse-500, and finally adapts the policy to downstream tasks and robot embodiments. Across these stages, $\lambda_0$ learns a shared representation space for human experience transfer, while domain-specific interfaces handle differences between human and robot states and actions. We evaluate $\lambda_0$ on SIMPLE and 4 real-world loco-manipulation tasks, achieving state-of-the-art performance, and further analyze its scaling behavior, generalization, and training-stage contributions to understand how human data support downstream whole-body humanoid control. We will release our code, models, and data to support further research.

</details>


### [55] [Whole-Body Aerial Grasping and Lifting via Partial Visual Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00404)
*Jiaye Jin,Rui Jin,Xinhang Xu,Haotian Jin,Ruiyang Liu,Yi Wang,Jiayan Zhao,Kun Cao,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种循环式教师—学生框架，在仿真中用单一策略协同控制飞行、机械臂与夹爪，完成空中接近、抓取和短距离提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中抓取需要在目标观测不完整且可见性变化时协调全身动作；接近阶段失败会减少训练中抓取与提升状态的暴露机会，执行时也增加对齐和夹爪闭合时机控制的难度。

Method: 强化学习教师使用特权目标状态，并通过关键状态课程先学习抓取和提升，再衔接正常接近轨迹。随后将教师策略蒸馏为循环视觉学生，学生依据双视角点云、本体感觉及历史观测闭环控制；另设专门目标监督基于持续就绪序列的闭合时机。训练与主要评估采用带条件触发锁定、虚拟附着和基于力/力矩的载荷模拟模型。

Result: 在该仿真模型下，学生完成8,996个回合；名义、物理与控制随机化、以及额外相机随机化条件下的全任务成功率分别为99.97%、97.14%和95.84%。名义条件下，按锁定次数加权的各随机种子抓取对齐误差第90百分位均值为8.12毫米。

Conclusion: 结果显示，该框架在所测试的仿真条件下能实现高成功率，并对物理、控制及相机扰动具有一定鲁棒性。结论受仿真抓取与载荷模型及短距离提升评估范围限制，摘要未报告真实平台验证。

Abstract: Aerial grasp-and-lift tasks require whole-body coordination across approach, acquisition, and lifting under partial target observations. Early approach failures can limit exposure to later task stages during training, while changing visibility complicates alignment and closure timing during execution. We present a recurrent teacher-student framework that learns a single policy in simulation to jointly command flight, arm motion, and gripper closure without an explicit task-phase input. A privileged teacher learns through reinforcement learning with a critical-state curriculum that exposes acquisition and lifting states before connecting them to normal approach trajectories. Its behavior is distilled into a recurrent visual student that replaces privileged target states with dual-view point clouds and proprioception, integrating observation history for closed-loop control. A dedicated closure objective supervises closure timing from sustained model-defined readiness sequences. Training and primary evaluation use a simulated acquisition-and-payload model with condition-triggered latching, virtual attachment, and wrench-based payload loading for short-distance lifting. Across 8,996 completed simulation episodes under this model, the frozen student achieves full-task success rates of 99.97%, 97.14%, and 95.84% under nominal, physics/control-randomized, and additional camera-randomized conditions, respectively. The nominal latch-count-weighted mean of per-seed 90th-percentile alignment errors at acquisition is 8.12 mm.

</details>


### [56] [DeepJEPA: Scaling World Models from Within](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00368)
*Zijian Jin,Yunbei Zhang,Yuanzhe Liu,Ming Liu,Baian Chen,Weirui Ye,Shilong Liu,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeepJEPA通过在决策关键的预测转移处增加内部更新，而非让所有转移一律加深，提升或保持规划性能，同时减少平均计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见世界模型规划器通过延长滚动、采样更多轨迹或增加优化轮次来扩展计算，却对每个想象转移分配相同计算。统一加深可能浪费资源，甚至损害规划；真正有价值的精化可能集中在少数会影响决策的事件上。

Method: 提出DeepJEPA：一种权重共享的联合嵌入预测世界模型，将转移深度视为测试时的内部扩展维度，并学习针对每个候选方案和滚动步骤，判断是否值得再进行一次循环更新。

Result: 在五种视觉控制设置中，DeepJEPA的表现优于或持平于最强固定深度规划器，而每个转移平均仅需1.00至1.26次更新。额外计算主要集中在接触开始和持续物体交互阶段，这些阶段的潜在状态修正可能改变候选方案是否进入精英集以及最终动作选择。表示探测还显示，规划改善并不要求物体状态的可解码性普遍提升。

Conclusion: 世界模型的扩展应关注计算分配，而不只是增加计算总量：在可能改变规划决策的关键转移处深入推演，比让每条轨迹一律更深或更长更有效。

Abstract: World-model planners typically scale outward by rolling farther, sampling more trajectories, or optimizing longer, while assigning the same computation to every imagined transition. We show that making every transition uniformly deeper wastes computation and can degrade planning because useful refinement is concentrated at a small set of decision-critical events. We introduce DeepJEPA, a weight-tied joint-embedding predictive world model that treats transition depth as an inner test-time scaling axis and learns when another recurrent update is worth computing for each candidate and rollout step. Across five visual-control settings, DeepJEPA improves or matches the strongest fixed-depth planner while averaging only 1.00-1.26 updates per transition. Its additional computation concentrates at contact onset and sustained object interaction, where latent corrections can change which candidates enter the planner's elite set and which action is selected. Representation probes further show that improved planning does not require uniformly better object-state decodability. DeepJEPA therefore reframes world-model scaling as a problem of allocating internal computation where it can change the planner's decision: think deeper at decision-critical transitions instead of making every rollout uniformly deeper or longer.

</details>


### [57] [IndoorBEV: A Lightweight Real-Time LiDAR BEV Perception System for Indoor Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00355)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IndoorBEV 是一种轻量级室内 LiDAR 感知框架，通过保留高度几何信息的 BEV 表示，在有限计算资源下联合完成语义预测与定向目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云与体素方法通常计算开销较大；传统 BEV 虽然高效，却会损失垂直几何信息。室内移动机器人需要在严格的延迟和内存限制下实现可靠感知。

Method: 将每个 BEV 网格内的垂直点分布编码为统计高度特征和多频高度特征，并使用二维卷积进行高效处理。轻量编码器融合互补几何特征、多尺度局部表示及紧凑的全局场景上下文；解耦的密集预测头联合输出语义 BEV 图和定向目标框。

Result: 模型包含 0.6M 个参数，存储占用为 2.3 MB。在 NVIDIA AGX Orin 上，单次推理使用 21.52 MB GPU 内存，平均延迟为 169.6 ms；在 200 ms 感知时限下，超时率为 1.8%。在模拟场景、真实机器人扫描和开放室内点云数据集上的评估显示，其准确率、延迟与内存消耗之间具有较好的综合权衡。

Conclusion: 在紧凑 BEV 表示中显式编码垂直几何信息，是提升室内 LiDAR 感知资源效率的一种有效方案。

Abstract: Efficient indoor LiDAR perception is challenging because mobile robots must understand cluttered three-dimensional environments under strict latency and memory constraints. Existing point-based and voxel-based methods often incur substantial computational overhead, whereas conventional bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations improve efficiency at the cost of discarding vertical geometric information. We present IndoorBEV, a lightweight LiDAR perception framework that mitigates this tradeoff through a height-aware BEV representation and geometry-conditioned feature fusion. IndoorBEV summarizes the vertical point distribution in each BEV cell using statistical height features and multi-frequency height encoding, allowing informative three-dimensional cues to be processed efficiently by two-dimensional convolutions. A lightweight encoder then integrates complementary geometric features with multi-scale local representations and compact global scene context. Decoupled dense prediction heads jointly produce semantic BEV maps and oriented object bounding boxes. IndoorBEV contains only 0.6M parameters and requires 2.3 MB of model storage. On an NVIDIA AGX Orin, it uses 21.52 MB of GPU memory per inference and achieves a mean latency of 169.6 ms under a 200 ms perception deadline, with a deadline miss ratio of 1.8\%. Evaluations on simulated scenes, real-world robot scans, and an open-source indoor point-cloud dataset demonstrate a favorable tradeoff among perception accuracy, latency, and memory consumption. These results indicate that explicitly encoding vertical geometry within a compact BEV representation provides an effective approach to resource-efficient indoor LiDAR perception.

</details>


### [58] [Real-Time Whole-Body Safe Motion Generation for Multi-Segment Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00220)
*Fangju Yang,Siyi Ma,Tonghao Guan,Tingcong Liu,Hang Yang,Zhengqiang Zhang,Jian S. Dai,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向平面多段腱驱连续机器人的实时安全运动生成框架，可处理非均匀弯曲并实现全身避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续机器人的腱间距与弯曲刚度可能沿本体变化，准确建模及在移动障碍物环境中进行全身碰撞规避，对实时控制构成挑战。

Method: 构建基于能量的变曲率模型，描述空间变化的腱间距和弯曲刚度，并推导解析雅可比矩阵，用于微分逆运动学与安全监测。采用多点控制屏障函数二次规划（CBF-QP）约束本体与障碍物的间距及驱动速度；优化决策维度仅取决于独立驱动的节段数。

Result: 模型与GVS参考结果吻合，最大曲率误差为5.223×10⁻² m⁻¹。MuJoCo的100次试验中，静态和动态场景的无碰撞成功率分别为96%和100%；不使用CBF约束时约为60%和80%。受限环境中的孔洞穿越测试也验证了安全性。监测600个本体点时，平均控制步耗时为6.66 ms。

Conclusion: 该框架兼顾建模精度、全身避障效果与实时计算性能，可支持腱驱连续机器人在静态、动态及受限环境中的安全运动生成。

Abstract: Real-time motion generation for tendon-driven continuum robots requires accurate modeling of nonuniform bending and whole-body collision avoidance. This paper presents a unified actuation-space framework for planar multi-segment tendon-driven continuum robots. An energy-based variable-curvature model captures spatially varying tendon spacing and bending stiffness and provides analytical Jacobians for differential inverse kinematics and safety monitoring. A multipoint CBF-QP enforces backbone clearance under obstacle motion and actuation-velocity bounds, while its decision dimension depends only on the number of independently actuated segments. The model closely agrees with GVS references, with a maximum curvature error of $5.223 \times 10^{-2}\,\mathrm{m}^{-1}$. Over 100 MuJoCo trials, the proposed method achieves collision-free success rates of 96% and 100% in static and dynamic scenarios, respectively, compared with approximately 60% and 80% without CBF constraints. Hole-traversal tests further demonstrate safe motion in constrained environments. With 600 backbone monitoring points, the mean control-step time is 6.66 ms, demonstrating real-time whole-body safe motion generation.

</details>


### [59] [HumanoidTTT: Test-Time Capability Reuse for Efficient Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00198)
*Jingtai Yang,Yining Wu,Yanjun Li,Zeyu Zhang,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HumanoidTTT 让人形机器人在持续部署中安全复用已验证动作，并动态管理有限存储中的动作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复生成已有动作会浪费计算资源；但机器人状态可能因中间动作而改变，盲目重放可能不安全。同时，部署中积累的能力数量不断增加，有限存储需要筛选保留内容。

Method: 提出选择性全动作复用，仅当当前入口状态属于已认证适用状态时，才直接复用已验证的完整动作，从而跳过重新生成；并提出测试时能力整合，根据后续部署中的复用反馈，动态调整有限全动作存储中保留的合格能力。

Result: 实验实现零次不安全接受，相比重新生成取得端到端速度提升 16.4 倍。在线能力整合相比固定存储方案，在每 200 次请求中额外避免 13.2 次生成器调用。

Conclusion: HumanoidTTT 能够在持续部署中可靠、高效地复用已验证动作，并根据使用情况自适应保留有价值的动作能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in motion generation and whole-body tracking have enabled humanoid robots to execute increasingly diverse motions, yet the same motion capabilities may be requested repeatedly during continual deployment. Reliable reuse is challenging because intervening motions can change the robot's entry state, making previously successful motions unsafe to replay blindly. Meanwhile, validated capabilities accumulate during deployment, while bounded storage requires deciding which ones are worth retaining. To address these challenges, we present HumanoidTTT, a framework for test-time capability reuse in continual humanoid control. Specifically, we introduce Selective Full-Motion Reuse, which authorizes direct reuse of validated complete motions only from certified applicable entry states, allowing accepted reuse to bypass fresh generation. We further introduce Test-Time Capability Consolidation, which adapts which qualified capabilities persist in a bounded Full-Motion Store using subsequent deployment reuse as feedback. Experiments demonstrate zero unsafe accepts and a 16.4$\times$ end-to-end speedup over fresh generation, while online consolidation improves avoided generator calls by 13.2 per 200 requests over its frozen counterpart. Overall, HumanoidTTT enables reliable and efficient reuse of validated motion capabilities while adaptively retaining useful capabilities throughout continual deployment. Code: this https URL . Website: this https URL .

</details>


### [60] [Probabilistic Plan Legibility with Off-the-shelf Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00065)
*Michele Persiani,Thomas Hellström*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种适用于任意 PDDL 领域的可读规划方法，使计划能从观察者视角更清晰地表明目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有可读规划研究难以直接扩展到经典规划领域，且往往需要为特定领域构造专用规划器。

Method: 将可读性纳入经典规划，并通过二阶心智理论连接规划者与观察者的任务空间，以估计观察者视角；使用正则化因子平衡计划可读性与效率。

Result: 多个 PDDL 基准领域的实验表明，可读性通常与计划效率存在权衡；不同领域提升可读性的效果不一，平衡两者需要正则化因子。

Conclusion: 该方法无需专用规划器即可应用于任意 PDDL 领域，并可用于机器人通过计划隐式传达目标；其效果取决于领域特性及可读性与效率之间的权衡。

Abstract: Legible planning is the creation of plans that best disambiguate their goals from a set of other candidates from an observer's perspective. In this paper we propose a method for legible planning for arbitrary PDDL domains, by extending previous research on legibility to classical planning without requiring to construct ad-hoc planners. We also discuss how the observer perspective may be estimated through a second order theory of mind that connects the planner's and the observer's task spaces. Our solution can for example be deployed in human-robot teaming scenarios, where an autonomous robot in a team can implicitly communicate its goal by producing legible plans. We present benchmark results on several PDDL planning domains. Our results generally show that plan legibility is a trade-off with plan efficiency, however, not all planning domains allows to increase legibility in the same way and a regularizing factor to balance legibility and efficiency was proved necessary.

</details>


### [61] [Multi-Reference Path Tracking Control for an Agricultural Tractor with Nonlinear Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00057)
*Marcel Moll,Timo Oksanen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究提出一种用于拖拉机沿预设路径行驶的非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）方法，并通过田间试验验证了其实时性与跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确跟踪预设路径是精准农业的重要环节；控制器需要有效处理由多个直线段构成的分段线性参考路径，并从完整路径中选取可行的参考路段。

Method: 将分段线性参考路径的多个路段直接纳入NMPC目标函数，并提出从完整路径中筛选可行参考路段的方法。系统通过拖拉机的拖拉机机具管理（TIM）转向接口进行控制和田间测试。

Result: NMPC求解器平均在3.45毫秒内收敛；对曲线路径的平均绝对横向偏差为6.1厘米。

Conclusion: 该方法在田间测试中实现了快速求解和较高的路径跟踪精度，显示出用于拖拉机自动路径跟踪的应用潜力。

Abstract: Guiding a tractor along a predefined reference path is a key component of precision agriculture. This study develops a path tracking controller based on Nonlinear Model Predictive Control, which incorporates multiple segments of a piecewise-linear reference path directly into the objective function. In addition, methods for selecting viable reference segments from the full path are presented. The control system is evaluated during a field test with a tractor controlled via the Tractor Implement Management steering interface. The NMPC solver converged on average after 3.45 ms and tracked the curved reference path with a mean absolute cross-track error of 6.1 cm.

</details>


### [62] [InterEvolve: Test-Time Evolution of Reward Programs for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02196)
*Zhuo Lin,Sirui Xu,Liuyu Bian,Yu-Xiong Wang,Liang-Yan Gui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InterEvolve 通过迭代演化任务奖励程序，使人形机器人控制器在不重新训练的情况下，调用并组合已有运动能力，完成未训练过的复杂操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有控制器蕴含广泛的运动与操作能力，但人工设计的奖励难以充分激发这些能力。研究旨在建立一种连接规划与控制的接口，使任务表达足够灵活，且执行结果可测量、可用于持续改进。

Method: 方法包含两部分：一是物体感知的前向—后向（FB）行为基础模型，在冻结的身体先验上加入物体残差，使测试时的新奖励能够引导运动与操作；二是将任务表示为含阶段、完成条件及可调参数的奖励程序。LLM 智能体依据执行反馈和已验证程序库修改程序结构，数值优化器调整参数，并通过并行仿真验证候选方案。

Result: 实验表明，InterEvolve 演化出的奖励程序能够释放人工奖励未能利用的 FB 模型能力，有时还产生新颖策略。该方法在仿真中支持多样任务、复杂场景及长时序任务组合；演化出的技能还能基于机载第一人称感知，在 Unitree G1 实体机器人上自主运行。

Conclusion: InterEvolve 展示了无需重新训练、依靠执行反馈持续改进机器人行为的测试时演化框架。其核心价值在于通过可迭代的奖励程序，将已有控制能力重新组合并迁移到新任务中。

Abstract: We study test-time evolution for humanoid loco-manipulation: solving tasks that a controller was never trained for by repurposing its existing skills, improving from its own attempts, and retaining what it learns, without retraining. Our key insight is that a broad controller already holds much of the competence a new task needs, and that this competence becomes accessible through an interface between planning and control that is expressive enough to specify contact-rich, multi-stage interactions, yet executable and measurable enough that execution feedback can guide planning from experience. InterEvolve realizes this interface with two components. First, we develop an object-aware forward-backward (FB) behavioral foundation model, whose object residuals on a frozen body prior turn a new reward about the body or objects into loco-manipulation behavior at test time. Second, we specify tasks as reward programs: staged rewards with completion conditions and tunable constants. A large language model (LLM) agent revises the program structure in context, drawing on execution feedback and a skill library of verified programs, while a numerical optimizer tunes its constants. With every candidate verified across parallel simulation scenarios, the program explores new ways to induce, repurpose, and compose the controller's existing motor competence for the task at hand, and thus improves over iterations. Experiments show that human-designed rewards leave much of the FB model's loco-manipulation competence untapped, whereas the programs InterEvolve evolves release it, sometimes through novel strategies. It further produces behaviors for diverse tasks, complex scenes, and long-horizon compositions in simulation, and evolved skills run autonomously on a physical Unitree G1 from egocentric onboard perception.

</details>


### [63] [World Motion Models: Flexible Sequence Modeling of SE(3) Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01742)
*Jiahui Lei,Qianqian Wang,Trevor Darrell,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出世界运动模型（WMM），以稀疏 SE(3) 位姿轨迹统一表示动态三维场景中的物体、人体、相机及机器人状态，并通过同一模型支持多种视觉与机器人任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间智能需要对动态三维世界建立综合生成先验，但现有表示难以在统一框架中描述不同实体及其随时间的变化，也难以灵活适配多样任务。

Method: 将场景元素近似为刚性 SE(3) 轨迹，并把多实体、多时间步的联合分布建模为序列生成问题。采用带逐 token 噪声水平的流匹配，并引入上下文 token 以支持非序列条件输入；通过改变掩码实现不同条件生成与推断任务。

Result: 在涵盖 3D 视觉与机器人学的六类应用实验中，WMM 展现出较强的通用性与灵活性，并取得良好表现。摘要未提供具体指标或基线对比数据。

Conclusion: WMM 将多种动态实体和任务统一到稀疏 SE(3) 轨迹表示及共享生成模型中，为未来预测、运动补全、模型预测控制、逆运动学、跨具身迁移和策略学习提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Equipping artificial agents with spatial intelligence requires a comprehensive generative prior over the dynamic 3D world. We propose World Motion Models (WMMs) that capture "what was, is, and will be where across time" via sparse SE(3) pose trajectories. WMMs are built on the observation that elements of dynamic scenes can be well approximated by a set of rigid SE(3) trajectories, a minimal yet expressive primitive for 4D modeling. This representation unifies articulated objects, human bodies, hand-object interactions, piecewise-rigid scene dynamics, camera motion, and even robot states and actions into a single shared space. Given this representation, we cast the joint distribution of these entities as a flexible sequence modeling problem, utilizing flow-matching with per-token noise levels. Coupled with a context token mechanism for non-sequential conditioning, this formulation supports any-to-any marginal conditioning across an arbitrary number of entities and time steps. Tasks such as future prediction, motion infilling, model-predictive control, inverse kinematics, cross-embodiment retargeting, and policy learning all reduce to the application of different masks over the same network. Experiments on 6 diverse applications of 3D vision and robotics demonstrate the versatility and flexibility of WMMs with strong performance.

</details>


### [64] [Beyond Leaderboard Scores: A Deployment-Focused Protocol for Interpretable Tracking Evaluation in Pedestrian-Centric Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01682)
*Dominik Wojcikiewicz,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向部署的行人跟踪器评估方案，以共享检测输入分离跟踪器表现，并分析跟踪能力与运行时开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 总体跟踪指标难以揭示轨迹何时、如何失效；检测器输入差异也会混淆跟踪器与检测器的影响。移动机器人应用还要求跟踪及时、连续、身份稳定且满足嵌入式算力限制。

Method: 在 JRDB 上使用统一检测结果评估六种开源跟踪器和轻量级 PedRefTrack，并加入 GT 辅助版本估计理想关联与运动条件下的跟踪器侧性能。评估初始化、检测缺失期间的轨迹延续、身份恢复、近邻关联及负载相关的单步运行时间，并以 HOTA 作为补充指标。

Result: 非 GT 跟踪器的 HOTA 为 24.26%–29.67%，但各项能力差异明显。检测器支持中断 1.0 秒后，没有任何非 GT 跟踪器能在超过一半的合格案例中同时维持空间位置正确且身份一致的输出，表明缺失观测下的延续是主要短板。近邻关联错误较少，主要在间距最小时增加。Jetson Orin 上的单步运行时间呈长尾且受负载影响，多个跟踪器在拥挤帧中无法达到 10 Hz。

Conclusion: 该协议可复现地刻画行人跟踪器的行为与部署适用性；实验显示，检测缺失时的轨迹延续能力和拥挤场景下的运行时负载是关键限制。

Abstract: Mobile robots operating among pedestrians need trajectories that become available quickly, remain spatially credible through missed observations, preserve identity, and fit within an embedded computing budget. Aggregate tracking scores provide limited insight into when and how trajectories fail, while varying detector inputs can confound tracker and detector quality. We present a deployment-focused, tracker-only evaluation protocol that uses shared detections to isolate tracker behavior and directly evaluates initialization, detector-gap continuation, identity recovery, close-neighbor association, and load-dependent tracker-step runtime, while Higher Order Tracking Accuracy (HOTA) is retained as a complementary aggregate measure. We apply the protocol to the JackRabbot Dataset and Benchmark (JRDB) using six open-source trackers and our lightweight Pedestrian Reference Tracker (PedRefTrack), together with a GT-assisted variant that estimates the remaining tracker-side gap under idealized association and motion. Under fixed detections, the non-GT trackers span only 24.26%-29.67% HOTA yet exhibit markedly different capability profiles. After 1.0 s without detector support, no tracker without GT assistance maintains spatially correct, same-identity output in more than half of eligible cases, making missing-observation continuation the dominant limitation among the tested properties. Close-neighbor failures are smaller and increase mainly at the shortest separations. Tracker-step runtime on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin is heavy-tailed and load-sensitive, causing several trackers to fall below the 10 Hz real-time target in crowded frames. The protocol provides a reproducible way to characterize tracker behavior and deployment suitability in pedestrian-centric environments. Code and evaluation scripts are released at this https URL .

</details>


### [65] [ALFRED: Requirement-driven development of an open-source mobile manipulator for long-term plant monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01477)
*Ciarán Miceal Johnson,Christopher Quail,Garry Ellard,Alistair McConnell,Steve Tonneau,Fernando Auat Cheein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ALFRED 是一款采用商用组件、可重构且开源的户外移动操作机器人，完成了为期一年的月度森林调查，并通过多轮设计改进提升机械臂可用工作空间和激光雷达视野。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期作物与森林观测需要反复采集同一地点的数据，但相关机器人设计文件往往未公开；此外，机器人结构对机械臂可达范围和传感器遮挡的影响缺少跨版本比较。

Method: 基于六项户外部署需求，经过四轮迭代设计 ALFRED：采用 Ackermann 转向底盘、六自由度机械臂、多种传感器、可重构铝型材框架及容器化 ROS 软件。通过模型分析比较后三个版本的机械臂可用姿态比例，并用射线投射评估激光雷达视野；同时开展长期森林调查部署。

Result: 后三个版本的机械臂可用姿态比例分别为 34.0%、60.0% 和 66.1%；只有最终版本能保持车架安装的激光雷达前后水平方向视野无遮挡。机器人完成 528 次森林巡查，全年未错过计划采集，即使经历电池老化和配置调整亦然；版本切换约需六小时。

Conclusion: ALFRED 展示了基于商用组件构建可复现户外移动操作机器人的可行性。结构模块化有助于适应不同研究任务，但其可靠性也取决于机器人描述文件能否及时、准确地反映硬件配置。

Abstract: Tracking seasonal change in crops and forests requires observing the same plants repeatedly. Ground robots can do this at close range, and a manipulator gives their sensors more viewpoints. Yet the robots behind long-term field datasets are rarely released with their design files, and how a robot's own structure limits arm reach and occludes its sensors is seldom compared between builds. We present ALFRED, an open-source mobile manipulator built from commercially available components. It carries a six-degree-of-freedom arm, LiDAR, RGB-D cameras, RTK GNSS and an IMU on an Ackermann-steered base, all mounted on a reconfigurable aluminium strut frame, and runs containerised ROS software. It was developed through four builds against six requirements for repeated outdoor deployment: durability, modularity, repairability, sensing reach, endurance and reproducibility. Model-based analysis of the last three builds shows the usable share of the arm's reachable poses rising from 34.0% to 60.0% and then 66.1%, and ray casting shows that only the final build keeps the frame-mounted LiDAR's horizontal view clear both forwards and backwards. ALFRED completed a year of monthly forest surveys (528 traversals) without missing a scheduled collection. This was despite battery degradation, reconfiguration for another researcher's study, and the parallel development of ALFRED 2.0 for autonomous crop-row operation, with each switch between builds taking about six hours. The deployment also showed that mechanical modularity is only as dependable as the robot description that tracks it.

</details>


### [66] [NarrativeFlow: Flow-Based Vision-Language-Action Model Using Robot Velocity Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00981)
*Shota Kobayashi,Koki Seno,Daichi Yashima,Komei Sugiura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NarrativeFlow 将语言条件下的机器人运动表示为连续速度场，并通过流匹配生成适用于操作任务的机器人流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言条件操作对实用机器人系统至关重要，但机器人基础模型的扩展受限于按机器人本体分别采集数据的高成本。现有方法要么用稀疏关键点位移粗略近似机器人流，要么无法处理语言条件操作。

Method: 提出 NarrativeFlow，以语言为条件，采用流匹配框架对机器人流进行连续速度场建模，旨在生成符合真实物理规律的操作运动。

Result: 在标准语言条件操作数据集上，NarrativeFlow 的标准评估指标优于代表性基线；多项真实世界操作任务实验中，其成功率也高于基线方法。

Conclusion: NarrativeFlow 为跨机器人平台利用运动数据提供了连续、以运动为中心的表示，并在基准测试和真实操作中展现出优于基线方法的性能。

Abstract: We focus on language-conditioned flow-based manipulation, where robot flows (robot velocity fields) serve as embodiment-agnostic, motion-centric representations for leveraging data collected from multiple robot platforms. This task is crucial because language-conditioned manipulation is essential for practical robotic systems, yet scaling robot foundation models remains limited by the labor-intensive collection of embodiment-specific data. Existing methods either coarsely approximate robot flows with sparse keypoint displacements, or cannot handle language-conditioned manipulation. To address this limitation, we propose NarrativeFlow, which models robot flows as continuous velocity fields using a flow-matching formulation conditioned on language. Accordingly, NarrativeFlow generates robot flows that are physically consistent with real-world manipulation. To validate NarrativeFlow, we have conducted experiments on standard datasets for language-conditioned manipulation. The experimental results show that NarrativeFlow outperforms representative baseline methods on standard evaluation metrics. Furthermore, through real-world experiments, we show that NarrativeFlow achieves higher success rates than baseline methods across multiple manipulation tasks. The project page is available at this https URL

</details>


### [67] [UniTrackPLA: Unified Panorama-Language-Action Model for Instruction-Guided Navigation and Dynamic Person Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00878)
*Pengfei Qi,Haoran Lin,Sizhuang Chen,Kai Luo,Sirui Zhang,Xinqi Liu,Fei Cheng,Wenrui Chen,Liming Yin,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniTrackPLA 将全景感知、语言指令与闭环动作控制结合，在统一框架中实现指令导航和动态人员跟踪，并在模拟及真实机器人实验中取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖前向视角，且导航与人员跟踪采用独立策略，难以充分利用全向观测并进行统一的闭环控制。

Method: 提出全景感知编码（PAE），保留全景图像各透视视图的时间与方位结构，使预训练视觉编码器能够处理全向观测；通过共享视觉语言骨干，将指令与全景上下文对齐并预测连续的机器人坐标系航点序列。世界—动作一致性模块（WAC）预测动作条件下的未来视觉状态，并在线验证航点前缀，以便复用可靠动作或在不一致时重新规划。另构建包含模拟跟踪轨迹、模拟视觉语言导航路线和真实路线的数据集。

Result: UniTrackPLA 将总体跟踪成功率从23.50%提升至35.00%，将全景视觉语言导航的成功率/路径效率从13.00%/12.77%提升至19.75%/19.29%。加入76条真实路线后，留出测试集的EP@0.2米从42.92%提升至92.08%。Go2-W机器人闭环实验展示了室内外统一的全景跟踪与导航能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，保留全景时空结构、共享语言动作策略并进行在线动作验证，有助于统一处理导航与动态跟踪；真实路线数据也显著改善了相应的留出测试表现。

Abstract: General-purpose embodied robots should support both navigation toward language-specified destinations and dynamic person tracking under arbitrary initial target azimuths. However, existing methods typically rely on forward-facing observations and address these tasks with separate policies, limiting omnidirectional perception and unified closed-loop control. We present UniTrackPLA, a unified panorama-language-action model for instruction-guided navigation and dynamic person tracking. Its Panoramic-Aware Encoding (PAE) preserves the temporal and azimuthal structure of perspective views projected from each panorama, enabling perspective-pretrained visual encoders to process omnidirectional observations. A shared vision-language backbone grounds instructions in the panoramic context and predicts continuous robot-centric waypoint chunks for both tasks. World-Action Consistency (WAC) further predicts action-conditioned future visual states and verifies waypoint prefixes online, allowing reliable actions to be reused while triggering replanning upon inconsistency. We also introduce OmniTrackNav-Bench, comprising 5,000 simulated tracking trajectories, 10,000 simulated VLN routes, and 96 verified real-world routes, providing 919,978 waypoint-supervision instances. UniTrackPLA improves overall tracking SR from 23.50% to 35.00% and Omni-VLN SR/SPL from 13.00%/12.77% to 19.75%/19.29%. Incorporating 76 real-world routes further improves held-out EP@0.2m from 42.92% to 92.08%. Closed-loop experiments on a Go2-W robot demonstrate unified panoramic tracking and navigation across indoor and outdoor environments. The project page is at this https URL .

</details>


### [68] [Kinematic MeanFlow: One-Step Action Generation Policy for Robotic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00864)
*Jiawei Fan,Sifeng Wang,Yuqing Hou,Anbang Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Kinematic MeanFlow（K-MF），使机器人基础模型能够以单步方式生成动作，并显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步流匹配推理延迟较高。MeanFlow 虽有望实现单步生成，但直接用于机器人基础模型时会出现性能崩溃，原因与速度场在去噪后期加速度骤增、样本间幅值差异扩大有关。

Method: 基于运动学恒等式，将 MeanFlow 中的时间导数项拆分为由中间点隔开的两个子区间项，分别刻画去噪早期和后期动态，以减轻误差累积与放大。

Result: K-MF 支持从头训练和微调两种范式下的单步动作生成，在多种任务的大多数设置中优于多步流匹配。对于 GR00T-N1.6，动作头延迟降低 67.5%–74.4%，端到端延迟降低 30.3%–54.9%。

Conclusion: 通过针对机器人速度场动态调整 MeanFlow 的形式，K-MF 在保持或提升任务表现的同时，实现了更高效的单步动作生成。

Abstract: In this paper, we study how to achieve one-step action generation in Robotic Foundation Models (RFMs), aiming to overcome the high inference latency of multi-step flow matching. MeanFlow provides a promising framework for this goal, yet its direct application leads to performance collapse. We discover that this stems from two distinctive dynamics exhibited in the RFM velocity field: (1) the ``local acceleration" exhibits stability early on, but surges sharply towards the end of the denoising process, and (2) the spread of its magnitudes across samples widens as denoising progresses. To address these issues, we introduce Kinematic MeanFlow (K-MF), a novel one-step action policy tailored for RFMs. Specifically, grounded in a kinematic identity, K-MF decouples the time derivative term in the MeanFlow formulation into two sub-interval terms separated by an intermediate point. This decoupled formulation enables the two terms to capture early-stage and late-stage denoising dynamics, respectively, while mitigating the error amplification across the process. As a result, our K-MF empowers RFMs to achieve one-step action generation in both training from scratch and fine-tuning paradigms across diverse tasks, while outperforming multi-step flow matching in most settings. In terms of inference efficiency, K-MF reduces action-head latency of GR00T-N1.6 by 67.5%~74.4% across L40 and Jetson Orin in eager and compiled modes, yielding end-to-end latency reductions of 30.3%~54.9%. Code will be available at this https URL .

</details>


### [69] [DexPolicy: Scheduled Exploration for Trajectory-Guided Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00360)
*Haoyu Wang,Siyuan Qian,Yanjun Li,Zeyu Zhang,Yandong Guo,Boxin Shi,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexPolicy 将探索噪声设为随训练进程逐步收窄的显式策略，在多种优化方法和真实机器人实验中提升了目标成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作需要先探索手指与物体的接触，再实现精确控制；持续较大的动作噪声可能妨碍后者。基线 PPO 训练后期仍接近初始噪声水平，因此需要显式调节探索尺度。

Method: 在损失函数、网络结构、奖励和优化器设置不变的条件下，按训练步数将探索从宽逐渐收窄。比较 PPO、无 critic 的 GRPO 延续训练和流参数化 PPO（FPO），在五种 YCB 物体、三个训练随机种子上评估，并在真实机械臂上进行 360 次试验。

Result: 模拟实验中，FPO、GRPO、PPO 的平均确定性 Target 成功率分别由 49.4% 升至 68.1%、由 14.1% 升至 45.4%、由 32.0% 升至 35.7%。真实机器人实验中，相应成功率由 25.0% 升至 85.0%、由 10.0% 升至 63.3%、由 8.3% 升至 43.3%。PPO 组件筛选显示，控制噪声优于所测试的优化器收缩方案；在三个测试物体上，选定的 PPO 调度优于端点相同的线性衰减。

Conclusion: 探索尺度调度能提升多种策略优化方法的终端任务表现，但训练回报、确定性目标成功率和抗执行噪声能力并不等价。调度方案应针对具体任务和优化方法，并依据预期执行条件下的终端成功率评估。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for dexterous manipulation must discover finger-object contacts and then control the object precisely; the action noise that serves the first goal can interfere with the second. In trajectory-guided settings such as ViViDex, where RL refine hand-object trajectories from human video, our baseline PPO runs end near their initial action noise after 5M steps, motivating explicit control of exploration scale. DexPolicy makes that scale an explicit function of training steps, annealing from broad to narrow exploration while holding loss, architecture, reward, and optimizer settings fixed. We study three policy-optimization settings: PPO, critic-free GRPO continuation, and a flow-parameterized PPO variant (FPO). Across five YCB objects and three training seeds, mean deterministic Target success rises from 49.4% to 68.1% (FPO), 14.1% to 45.4% (GRPO), and 32.0% to 35.7% (PPO). On a RealMan RM75 arm with an Inspire/RH56 hand, 360 trials over three objects raise mean Target success from 25.0% to 85.0% (FPO), 10.0% to 63.3% (GRPO), and 8.3% to 43.3% (PPO), with one trained model per object-method condition. PPO component screening favors noise control over the tested optimizer contraction; the selected PPO schedule yields higher mean Target success than linear decay with the same endpoints on three tested objects. Training return, deterministic Target success, and tolerance to execution noise dissociate; schedules should therefore be judged by terminal task success under the intended execution conditions, per task and policy-optimization setting. Code: this https URL . Website: this https URL .

</details>


### [70] [Retrospective Open-Vocabulary Memory for Long-Term Object Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00330)
*Jiaming Wang,Zhiwei Xue,Chen Jizhuo,Peng Shiqi,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ECROM利用观测机会校正检测与未检测证据，推断物体长期出现概率，并将其用于主动搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期搜索需要从不均匀、受环境变化影响的重复观测中学习物体常见位置。若不考虑机器人是否有机会观察某处，检测或未检测都可能造成偏差；此外，系统还需支持仅在查询时指定的开放词汇概念。

Method: 将回顾式开放词汇记忆建模为对删失观测的概率推断，按各位置的观测机会加权证据。ECROM据此估计概念的长期出现率，并直接将该信念转化为主动搜索先验。研究还构建了覆盖10个HM3D住宅环境的受控长期基准，分别改变物体摆放与观测机会。

Result: 在留出查询上，ECROM相较各项指标中最强的竞争记忆方法，支持级AP提升4.5个百分点，搜索SPL提升4.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 按观测机会校正证据，有助于从不完整且不均匀的长期观察中形成更可靠的物体位置先验，并提升后续搜索表现。基准、数据集与代码计划开放发布。

Abstract: Long-term object search requires learning where objects usually appear from repeated but uneven observations of a changing environment. We formulate retrospective open-vocabulary memory as probabilistic inference from censored observations, where the key idea is to reason with evidence per opportunity: a detection or non-detection should influence belief only in proportion to the robot's opportunity to observe the corresponding location. We introduce ECROM, which uses this principle to estimate long-term prevalence for concepts specified only at query time and converts the resulting belief directly into an active-search prior. To evaluate this problem, we introduce a controlled long-term benchmark in ten HM3D homes that independently varies object placement and observation opportunity across repeated traversals. ECROM improves support-level AP on held-out queries by 4.5 points and search SPL by 4.2 points over the strongest competing memory in each metric. The benchmark, dataset, and code will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [71] [Training-Free Diffusion Planning with Analytical Local Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01959)
*Michael Y. Fatemi,Jinhao Liang,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的扩散式运动规划器，通过解析局部评分函数进行轨迹去噪，在复杂环境和多机器人场景中生成平滑、可行且无碰撞的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式规划器依赖大量可行轨迹进行训练，通常具有地图特定性，在缺少高质量示范数据时难以部署。本文旨在保留扩散模型迭代优化和多模态建模能力，同时降低对训练数据的依赖。

Method: 将学习得到的全局轨迹评分替换为解析局部评分，评分项包括障碍物约束、轨迹平滑性、速度限制和机器人间可行性约束。基于轨迹评分可由相邻路点与邻近约束重构这一观察，设计分解式去噪过程，将扩散迭代与经典轨迹优化结构结合。

Result: 在大量复杂环境和大规模多机器人任务上的实验表明，该方法能够以较低计算成本生成平滑且可行的轨迹。在包含100多个障碍物的环境中，可在GPU上6秒内为300多个机器人生成可行路径，并优于具有竞争力的学习方法和优化基线。

Conclusion: 解析局部评分使扩散式运动规划摆脱了对大规模示范数据和特定地图训练的依赖，同时保留迭代细化能力与经典优化方法的结构优势，适合复杂环境下的高效多机器人规划。

Abstract: Path finding and multi-robot motion planning require trajectories that are smooth, goal-directed, and collision-free in environments with complex geometric constraints. Recent diffusion-based planners have shown that trajectory generation can be cast as iterative denoising which has opened the doors to learning-based approaches that can handle multi-modal trajectory distributions and refine entire trajectories. However, a key limitation is that diffusion planners require training on large collections of feasible trajectories, rendering them map-specific, and difficult to deploy when high-quality demonstrations are unavailable. This paper introduces a training-free diffusion-based motion planner that replaces learned global trajectory scores with analytical local scores derived from obstacle, smoothness, velocity, and inter-agent feasibility terms. The proposed idea relies on a key observation: the score of a trajectory can be reconstructed by considering only local interactions between neighboring waypoints and nearby constraints. This structure exploitation yields a decomposed denoising procedure that retains the optimization structure of classical trajectory methods while inheriting the iterative refinement behavior of diffusion models. Experiments on a large collection of complex environments and large multi-agent planning tasks show that the proposed analytical score produces smooth and feasible trajectories within limited computational costs, for example in generating feasible paths for 300+ agents in environments containing 100+ obstacles in under 6 seconds on a GPU, outperforming strong learning-based and optimization baselines, while avoiding the data requirements of learned diffusion planners.

</details>


### [72] [End-to-End Learning vs. Modular Architectures: Comparative Insights into Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01746)
*Kartik B. Kapse*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文比较端到端学习与模块化自动驾驶架构的优缺点，并提出基于四个维度、十二项二元标准的架构选择框架；混合架构被视为有前景的发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统存在多种设计范式，但不同范式在可解释性、可扩展性、鲁棒性及实际部署方面各有取舍，因而需要系统比较并为架构选择提供依据。

Method: 综合分析端到端学习与模块化架构的特点，并提出涵盖安全性、运行环境、数据与计算资源、部署背景的四维架构选择框架。该框架包含十二项二元标准，并使用十种已发表的自动驾驶系统进行验证。

Result: 端到端学习结构简洁、适应动态环境，但透明度较低且依赖大量数据；模块化架构可解释性较强、便于针对任务优化，但集成复杂且面临扩展挑战。选择框架对十种系统中的七种给出了与其实际部署架构相符的推荐。

Conclusion: 两类架构各有适用场景，混合方案有望结合其优势、缓解各自局限。所提出的选择框架可辅助架构决策，但其验证结果也表明，推荐并非对所有案例都准确。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems have become a central focus of intelligent transportation research, with End-to-End Learning and Modular Architectures offering two prominent design paradigms for their implementation. E2E Learning uses deep learning algorithms to map raw sensory inputs directly to driving actuators, providing a streamlined and adaptable solution. while Modular Architectures employ a pipeline-based approach, dividing the system into distinct subsystems for perception, cognition, planning, and control. This paper presents a comprehensive comparative analysis of these paradigms, focusing on their strengths, limitations, and trade-offs to provide insights into their suitability for various autonomous driving applications. The study evaluates key factors such as interpretability, scalability, robustness, and real-world applicability. While End-to-End Learning emphasizes simplicity and adaptability in dynamic environments, it lacks transparency and is highly dependent on large datasets. Conversely, Modular Architectures offer superior interpretability and task-specific optimization, but face challenges related to integration complexity and scalability. To address these limitations, hybrid approaches that combine the strengths of both paradigms have emerged, offering a promising direction for overcoming these challenges. Beyond this comparative synthesis, following work proposes a Four-Dimensional Architecture Selection Framework, comprising twelve binary criteria across safety, operating environment, data/computational resources, and deployment context, and validate it against ten published autonomous driving systems, correctly recommending 7/10 deployed architectures.
