### [91] [FERPO: Forward Entropy-Regularized Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02198)
*Sebastian Sanokowski,Alireza Sarmadi,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FERPO 是一种无需对 critic 关于动作求导的在线最大熵强化学习算法，通过拟合由 critic 值构造的目标动作分布来改进策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于动作梯度的策略优化依赖 critic 对动作导数的准确性，但 critic 通常只针对回报预测进行训练；准确的价值预测并不保证可靠的动作梯度，可能导致不稳定的策略更新。

Method: FERPO 从带熵正则和 KL 正则的策略改进目标中推导目标动作分布，再通过最小化前向 KL 将 actor 拟合到该分布。目标函数使用 rollout 策略采样的动作，并以自归一化重要性采样估计。KL 正则限制目标分布偏离 rollout 策略的程度，以控制重要性权重；前向 KL 还鼓励覆盖多个高价值模式。

Result: 在 MuJoCo Playground 和 ManiSkill 上的实验与消融结果表明，FERPO 达到有竞争力的性能，并在样本效率方面有所提升。计算基准显示，其 actor 更新速度快于 REPPO。

Conclusion: FERPO 避开了对 critic 动作梯度的依赖，并结合前向 KL、熵正则与重要性采样实现策略改进；实验结果支持其性能、样本效率和计算效率优势。

Abstract: Several state-of-the-art methods for online reinforcement learning in continuous control improve policies using action gradients of a learned critic. However, critics are typically trained to predict returns, and accurate value predictions do not necessarily yield accurate action derivatives, potentially leading to unreliable policy updates. We propose Forward Entropy-Regularized Policy Optimization (FERPO), an on-policy maximum entropy reinforcement learning algorithm that performs policy improvement using critic values without differentiating the critic with respect to actions. FERPO derives an optimal target action distribution from a policy-improvement objective regularized by entropy and Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence. We then fit the actor to this target by minimizing a forward-KL objective, estimated using self-normalized importance sampling (SNIS) with actions drawn from the rollout policy. By limiting the target distribution's deviation from the rollout policy, the KL regularization helps keep these importance weights well behaved. In contrast to reverse-KL objectives, which can favor a subset of the target distribution's modes, the forward-KL objective encourages coverage of multiple high-value modes and thereby promotes exploration. Experiments and ablations on MuJoCo Playground and ManiSkill show competitive performance and sample-efficiency gains. Computational benchmarks also demonstrate faster actor updates than Relative Entropy Pathwise Policy Optimization (REPPO).

</details>


### [92] [Bellman Meets Lyapunov: Unsupervised Reinforcement Learning via Mastering Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02012)
*Tristan Shah,Wooyoung Chung,Volodomyr Makarenko,Juan Wachs,Stas Tiomkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Forward CIP（F-CIP），一种仅依赖环境动力学、无需人工选择信息变量的内在动机目标，用于无监督强化学习，并能发现有助于复杂运动控制的基础行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习依赖人工设计任务奖励；现有无监督强化学习的内在动机方法通常需要预先选择信息变量，仍然引入领域知识。本文旨在消除这一依赖，使奖励目标直接由系统动力学产生。

Method: 将Controllable Information Production（CIP）目标改写为适用于强化学习的Forward CIP形式，并结合现有强化学习算法进行训练。该目标依据智能体与环境交互中的系统动力学定义内在奖励，无需人工指定信息变量。

Result: 理论上证明F-CIP与强化学习兼容。实验表明，智能体能够无监督地学习保持平衡、维持可控性等基础行为；在加入简单的前向速度奖励后，还能学习跳跃、奔跑等协调步态，而这些行为通常需要精心设计奖励才能获得。

Conclusion: F-CIP为无监督强化学习提供了一种更少依赖人工领域知识的内在动机机制，能够从动力学中自动诱导对机器人控制有用的基础技能，并支持更复杂的运动行为学习。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is a powerful paradigm for training agents, yet its success rests on domain expertise of human engineers who design informative reward signals for every new task. Unsupervised RL aims to reduce this engineering with intrinsic motivation (IM): reward signals that emerge from the agent environment interaction itself. Existing IM objectives, however, involve the selection of information variables, which re-introduces domain expertise the field has sought to eliminate. We introduce Forward CIP (F-CIP), an RL-native formulation of the Controllable Information Production (CIP) objective, which is defined by the system's dynamics alone and requires no such selection. We prove that F-CIP is compatible with RL and demonstrate its effectiveness with existing algorithms. Training agents with F-CIP results in unsupervised discovery of primitive behaviors such as balancing and maintaining controllability, which are essential for more complex robot behaviors. Paired with a simple forward-velocity reward, our method produces coordinated gaits such as hopping and running which otherwise require reward engineering to learn.

</details>


### [93] [Flowing Faster to Coordinate: One-Step Online Multi-Agent Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01882)
*Zhuoran Li,Yunzhan Li,Xun Wang,Yihan Du,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 OMAF，一种基于单步流模型的在线多智能体强化学习框架，兼顾策略表达能力与训练、执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式生成策略能够表示复杂、多模态的协同行为，但反复迭代采样带来较高计算开销，限制其在在线多智能体场景中的扩展。

Method: 采用 Transformer 流策略建模协同行为，并以近似路径分数代理实现同步流策略优化；结合 softmax Q 值估计与联合流策略目标进行联合优化，以支持稳定、样本高效的学习。单步动作生成避免了迭代采样。

Result: 在 MPE 和 MAMuJoCo 的 10 项标准任务上，OMAF 整体优于基线方法，最高实现 3.4 倍回报提升和 10.5 倍样本效率提升。

Conclusion: 实验表明，OMAF 能在保留生成策略表达能力的同时显著降低计算开销，是一种高效的在线多智能体强化学习方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) provides a powerful framework for learning coordinated behaviors through interactions with the environment. Developing MARL policies requires balancing expressive modeling of complex and multimodal action distributions with efficient training and execution. Generative policies, particularly diffusionbased policies, can faithfully capture complex and multimodal behaviors, but costly iterative sampling hinders their scalability in online multi-agent settings. We propose an Online MARL framework via one-step Flow model (OMAF) that combines expressive generative policies with efficient one-step action generation. OMAF employs a Transformer-based flow policy to capture complex coordination behaviors, while its approximate path score surrogate provides a principled route to synchronized flow policy optimization. To enable stable and sampleefficient learning, we further develop a joint optimization scheme coupling softmax Q-value estimation with a joint flow policy objective for coordinated policy learning. By eliminating iterative sampling, OMAF dramatically reduces training overhead without sacrificing policy expressiveness. Extensive experiments across 10 standard tasks from MPE and MAMuJoCo show that OMAF consistently achieves superior performance, with up to 3.4x higher returns and 10.5x sample efficiency improvement compared with baseline methods. These results validate the effectiveness of OMAF as an expressive and computationally efficient one-step flow policy paradigm for online MARL.

</details>


### [94] [Iterative Policy Refinement through Semantic Rollout Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01652)
*Feiyu Gavin Zhu,Qi Xu,Zhifei Deng,Zhigang Hua,Luke Simon,Jean Oh,Reid Simmons*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种利用大语言模型分析策略执行轨迹、迭代优化模仿学习结构化策略的闭环框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构生成方法依赖大量人工输入或静态领域知识，生成的策略结构可能与专家示范不一致。

Method: 将策略执行轨迹记录为具有语义的表格数据，并提示大语言模型生成诊断分析代码，以识别结构缺陷并自动迭代修正，无需人工指导。

Result: 在赛车和开门任务中，相比零样本大语言模型生成的结构，模仿学习性能最高提升15%；达到相同强化学习性能所需的计算量减少75%。

Conclusion: 表格化轨迹分析可作为有效反馈，帮助大语言模型生成并自动优化与专家示范对齐的策略结构。

Abstract: Structured policies improve efficiency, robustness, and interpretability in imitation learning by introducing task-specific inductive bias, but existing structure generation methods rely either on extensive human input or on static domain knowledge encoded in LLMs, which may be inconsistent with the expert demonstrations. We propose a closed-loop framework that iteratively refines structured policies using LLM-guided analysis of policy rollouts. By logging rollouts as semantically meaningful tabular data and prompting the LLM to generate diagnostic analysis code, our method identifies suboptimalities in the policy structure and iteratively corrects them without requiring human instruction. Experiments on car racing and door opening tasks show that our approach improves imitation learning performance by up to 15% over zero-shot LLM-generated structures and requires 75% less compute to achieve the same reinforcement learning performance. These results demonstrate that tabular rollout analysis provides an effective feedback signal to align LLM-generated policy structures with expert demonstrations, and we can utilize it to generate good policy structures automatically.

</details>


### [95] [Supervise What Decides Success: Criterion-Aligned Auxiliary Losses for Latent World-Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01224)
*Takumi Hara,Kanata Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种辅助训练损失，使潜在世界模型更准确地保留判断任务成功所需的物理量，并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型通过潜在空间距离评估候选动作序列，但任务成败取决于实际物理量。研究发现，四种模型对末端执行器位置的编码误差均超过成功判据容许范围，因而难以区分成功与失败的候选方案。

Method: 训练时，在编码器和预测器的输出上接入线性回归头，以成功判据所涉及的物理量为目标，并将回归误差加入训练损失。训练完成后移除该回归头，因此不改变模型结构、推理成本或测试时输入。

Result: 仅加入该辅助损失，PushT 和 cube 任务的成功率分别绝对提升 3.5% 和 3.4%，且两项提升均具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 成功判据能够明确指出世界模型的潜在状态必须保留哪些信息，并可直接作为训练目标，改善模型对任务成败的区分能力。

Abstract: Latent world models plan by scoring candidate action sequences with distances in latent space. However, task success is judged by physical quantities, which we call the success-criterion quantities. In all four latent world models we examine, the end-effector position is encoded in the latent state with an error larger than the success criterion allows. Such a latent state cannot separate successful candidates from failing ones. We propose an auxiliary loss that uses success-criterion quantities as training targets, whereas existing latent world models take them only as inputs. During training, a linear head on the encoder and predictor outputs regresses the success-criterion quantities, and the regression error is added to the training loss. The head is discarded after training, so the model, its cost, and its inputs at test time are unchanged. This loss alone improves the success rate on PushT and cube by 3.5% and 3.4% (absolute), respectively, and both improvements are statistically significant. A success criterion thus specifies what a world model must retain in its latent state, and we show that it can serve directly as a training target.

</details>


### [96] [Reliability-aware short-term roll prediction for unmanned surface vehicles via multi-task learning and adaptive centralization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00996)
*Kaizhen Li,Xi Zhou,Zihao Wang,Dan Zhang,Jianjian Liu,Xiaowei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向无人水面艇短期横摇预测的可靠性感知方法，同时输出预测值与置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注预测精度，对预测结果可靠性的量化关注不足；而可靠性信息对安全相关的下游决策十分重要。

Method: 采用多任务学习架构，共享特征提取骨干网络，并设置横摇回归头和置信度量化头。另提出适用于短期实时预测的自适应中心化策略，以提升模型在不同运行条件下的泛化能力。

Result: 真实海况数据实验表明，该方法能够有效量化预测可靠性，并在运行条件变化时保持较好的泛化表现。

Conclusion: 该方法将精确预测与置信度评估结合，为无人水面艇的可靠横摇预测提供了可用于实际工程的方案。

Abstract: Reliable roll prediction of unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) is essential for ensuring navi?gational safety and enhancing autonomous decision-making. While existing studies primarily focus on improving prediction accuracy, the quantification of prediction reliability remains insufficiently addressed. To bridge this gap, this paper proposes a reliability-aware prediction paradigm that integrates confidence assessment into the predictive pipeline. The architecture utilizes a multi-task learning structure where a shared feature extraction backbone feeds into dual heads: a regression head for precise roll prediction and a quantification head for confidence scoring. This configuration provides accurate prediction and corresponding confidence for risk?sensitive downstream tasks. In addition, an adaptive centralization strategy tailored for short?term real-time roll prediction is introduced to improve model generalization under varying operational conditions. Experiments conducted on a real-sea dataset demonstrate that the proposed method effectively quantifies the reliability of prediction results and maintains superior generalization under varying conditions, offering significant potential for practical engineering applications.

</details>


### [97] [Variational Streaming Flow: Probabilistic Forecasting in Physical Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00976)
*Hans Hao-Hsun Hsu,Minseon Gwak,Soon Hoe Lim,Pan Li,N. Benjamin Erichson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 变分流（VSF）在保留流式流（SF）物理时间建模效率的同时引入条件潜变量分布，实现多种可能未来的概率预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SF通过学习连续速度场高效建模时间演化，但速度场是确定性的，因此给定初始状态和观测历史只能生成单一未来轨迹，难以表达随机性及分岔动力学。

Method: VSF学习由目标系统动力学条件化的潜变量分布，并在物理时间中生成预测，从而兼顾概率建模能力与SF的计算效率；它还可作为即插即用预测器集成到基于JEPA的世界模型中。

Result: 在确定性和随机动力系统上，VSF取得更高的预测准确性与分布保真度；在超过1,000步的长时域预测和分岔动力学场景中均展现优势。集成到JEPA世界模型后，还能改善导航、运动规划和操作任务中的时间动力学建模与目标导向成功率。

Conclusion: VSF扩展了SF，使其能够高效表示多模态未来，并适用于长时域预测及基于世界模型的目标导向任务。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting is important for predicting complex dynamical systems because intrinsic randomness and incomplete observations can cause the same observed state to evolve into multiple plausible futures. While flow matching is a flexible approach for probabilistic forecasting, it is computationally expensive. Streaming flow (SF) reformulates this approach to model temporal evolution efficiently by learning a continuous velocity field directly in physical time. However, SF learns a deterministic velocity field. Thus, it provides only a single future trajectory for a given fixed initial state and observation history. To overcome this limitation, we introduce Variational Streaming Flow (VSF). Our approach learns a latent distribution that is conditioned on the dynamics of interest. In turn, this enables probabilistic forecasting. Importantly, we retain the computational efficiency of SF by generating in physical time. Across deterministic and stochastic dynamical systems, VSF demonstrates superior predictive accuracy and distributional fidelity. We demonstrate the advantage for both long-horizon rollouts exceeding 1,000 steps, and settings with bifurcating dynamics. Moreover, VSF can be integrated into existing Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA)-based world models as a plug-and-play predictor to improve temporal dynamics and goal-directed success rate in navigation, motion planning, and manipulation.

</details>


### [98] [In CEM, a World Model Is Also a Proposal Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00921)
*Oliver Obst,Frieder Stolzenburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，CEM中的预测模型评分误差会影响当前动作选择及后续搜索分布；在所测任务中，用环境评分替换一次模型评分更新可降低最终选中序列的实际成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CEM依据世界模型评分筛选动作序列，并用筛选结果更新后续采样分布，因此评分误差可能同时影响当前决策和之后的候选搜索。研究旨在区分并评估这两种作用。

Method: 使用四类预测模型生成CEM搜索轨迹，并让每个模型对所有已保存的候选池重新评分。将候选序列在环境中的实际执行结果作为参照，确定精英集合及分布更新。分析Walker和Cheetah上的12个独立训练任务—种子单元，并在预先选定的Random nonlinear模型上进行一次更新替换干预；另用6个未参与选择的单元检验结果。

Result: 所有12个单元中，预先指定的提议分布距离均从首轮到最终轮下降；分布宽度收缩，拟合均值相对于剩余搜索宽度出现分离。Walker上的成对排序一致性接近随机水平，Cheetah上则下降；精英集合一致性未见改善。Cheetah中评分器之间的差异大于候选池来源之间的差异；Walker中两类差异及其交互均有体现。一次性以环境评分更新替换模型评分更新后，选择序列的最终实际成本在用于模型选择的6个单元及额外6个留出单元中均降低。

Conclusion: 结果表明，CEM搜索分布虽随迭代收缩，但模型评分与环境评价之间的一致性有限；评分误差可能影响搜索过程。针对所测试的模型与任务，一次环境评分更新带来成本改善，并在留出单元中复现，但结论的适用范围仍受实验设置限制。

Abstract: The cross-entropy method (CEM) uses world-model scores to select action sequences and fit the distribution sampled in its next iteration. A scoring error can therefore change both the present decision and the candidates considered later. We evaluate these two roles separately. Four types of predictive model generate CEM traces, and every model rescores every saved candidate pool. Executing the same candidates in the environment provides a reference elite set and proposal update. Across twelve independently trained task-seed units on Walker and Cheetah, the pre-specified proposal distance falls from the first to the final CEM iteration in every unit. Proposal widths contract and fitted means separate relative to the remaining search width. Pairwise ranking agreement stays near chance on Walker and declines on Cheetah; elite-set agreement does not improve. This comparison shows greater variation between scorers than between pool sources on Cheetah; Walker has variation in both and in their pairings. We use the original six units to select Random nonlinear for a one-update intervention, without inspecting intervention outcomes. Replacing its first model-ranked update with an environment-ranked update lowers final realised selected-sequence cost in those six units and in six further units held out from the selection.

</details>


### [99] [SHARPO: Segment-Level Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00838)
*Xinchen Du,Zhengze Zhou,Wenhui Zhu,Han Yu,Sen Na,Rohit Jain,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHARPO 在环境交互片段层面调整 GRPO 优势信号，改善长程智能体强化学习中的信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程、多步骤任务中，局部决策错误可能导致整体失败；仅使用轨迹级奖励难以判断各个决策的贡献。

Method: SHARPO 将交互轨迹划分为面向环境的片段，计算每个片段内教师与学生的对数概率差，并据此生成有界乘数，重加权 GRPO 优势。同一片段内所有 token 共用该乘数，使不同片段获得不同的信用权重。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 上，SHARPO 在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 基准上的表现优于 GRPO、SDAR、RLSD 和 StepOPSD 等基线。

Conclusion: 片段级的事后优势重加权为长程智能体 RL 提供了更细粒度的信用分配方式，并在所测基准上取得优于现有基线的结果。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) trains a large language model (LLM) to act over long, multi-step interactions. However, a single localized error can cause task failure, while trajectory-level rewards provide limited guidance for assigning credit to individual decisions. To address this limitation, we introduce Segment-level Hindsight Advantage Reweighting for Policy Optimization (SHARPO), a credit-assignment mechanism that refines Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) at the level of environment-facing segments. Inspired by the existing on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) method, SHARPO computes teacher-student log-probability gaps within each segment and uses the resulting signal to compute a bounded multiplier on the GRPO advantage. This multiplier is shared by all tokens within the segment, allowing credit to vary across different segments. With Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, SHARPO outperforms existing baselines on the ALFWorld and WebShop benchmarks, including GRPO, SDAR, RLSD, and StepOPSD.

</details>


### [100] [Learning Goal-Reaching Quasimetric Geometry From Finite-Time Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00778)
*Daisuke Yamada,Travis Pence,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReQRL通过有限时域可达性约束价值梯度，提升离线目标条件强化学习中的目标代价建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拟度量学习的局部约束未能充分体现有限时域内控制组合的方向性影响及环境可行性。

Method: 借鉴带状态约束的最优控制，将动力学可达性与边界几何分离，并从数据中分别估计；据此约束评论器的价值梯度。

Result: 在OGBench上，ReQRL的表现优于或媲美现有拟度量方法及其他离线目标条件强化学习方法。

Conclusion: 将有限时域可达性纳入价值梯度约束，为更准确地刻画目标到达代价提供了一种有效方法。

Abstract: In goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), quasimetric learning models goal-reaching costs as quasimetric distances, connecting local constraints to global value geometry. Its local constraints, however, should reflect the direction- dependent effects of control composition over a finite horizon together with environmental feasibility. We propose ReQRL, which constrains the critic's value gradients through finite-horizon reachability. Drawing on state-constrained optimal control, we decouple dynamical reachability from boundary geometry, estimating both from data. On OGBench, our method outperforms or rivals existing quasimetric approaches and other offline GCRL methods.

</details>


### [101] [Reward as Observation: Learning Reward-Based Policies for Rapid Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00729)
*Morgan Byrd,Maks Sorokin,Robert Wright,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种仅以奖励和动作作为条件的策略，实现观测空间完全不同的环境之间零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规深度策略依赖观测输入，面对观测形式不同的新环境时适应困难，通常需要大量目标环境样本。

Method: 研究奖励条件策略的可行性与训练挑战，并设计实用训练算法；该策略根据奖励与动作进行决策，不依赖特定观测空间。

Result: 在 Pointmass、Cartpole 和二维赛车环境中训练的策略，可零样本迁移至颜色方案或渲染方式不同的环境，以及 Habitat-Sim 中的 Stretch 机器人导航任务。该策略还可引导目标环境中的观测条件策略训练。

Conclusion: 奖励条件策略为跨观测空间迁移提供了新路径，并可兼作目标环境策略学习的引导机制。

Abstract: This paper explores a reward-based policy to achieve zero-shot transfer between source and target environments with completely different observation spaces. While humans can demonstrate impressive adaptation capabilities, deep neural network policies often struggle to adapt to a new environment and require a considerable amount of samples for successful transfer. Instead, we propose a novel reward-based policy only conditioned on rewards and actions, enabling zero-shot adaptation to new environments with completely different observations. We discuss the challenges and feasibility of a reward-based policy and then propose a practical algorithm for training. We demonstrate that a reward policy can be trained within three different environments, Pointmass, Cartpole, and 2D Car Racing, and transferred to completely different observations, such as different color palettes or 3D rendering, or Stretch robot navigation in Habitat-Sim, in a zero-shot manner. We also demonstrate that a reward-based policy can further guide the training of an observation-based policy in the target environment.

</details>


### [102] [JEPA-TTT: Persistent Test-Time Training of Latent World Models for Planning under Dynamics Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00722)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Suyu Ye,Nakul Agarwal,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Ehsan Moradi Pari,Daniel Khashabi,Tianmin Shu,Vaishnav Tadiparthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JEPA-TTT在测试阶段持续自监督更新预训练世界模型的潜在动力学预测器，使其适应训练中未见的环境动力学变化，并提升规划表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境动力学在测试时可能偏离训练分布，导致世界模型的未来状态预测不可靠，进而损害基于模型的规划。

Method: 仅更新预训练动作条件JEPA世界模型的潜在动力学预测器；视觉编码器与奖励头保持固定。方法不需要目标图像或在线环境奖励，通过密集回放构造各时间偏移的预测窗口，将其存入持续增长的缓冲区，并据此采样小批量进行自监督更新，且跨回合累积更新。

Result: 在4个连续控制环境的8种动力学变化下，JEPA-TTT在每种变化上均改善了规划效果。经过500个测试回合，潜在自回归预测误差平均降低83%，相较于冻结的JEPA世界模型，规划表现提升153%。

Conclusion: 持续的测试时自监督训练能够在动力学变化下适配预训练潜在世界模型，并显著改善预测与规划；实验结果支持该方法在所测环境和变化设置中的有效性。

Abstract: World models enable agents to plan by predicting future states of the environment, but their predictions can become unreliable when test-time dynamics differ from those seen during training. We present JEPA-TTT, which adapts the latent dynamics predictor of a pretrained action-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture world model throughout test time. Self-supervised updates accumulate across episodes, while the visual encoder and reward head remain fixed, preserving the pretrained representation and task objective. Planning requires neither a goal image nor online environment reward. JEPA-TTT uses dense replay, which forms prediction windows at every temporal offset, retains them in a growing buffer, and samples minibatches from that buffer for predictor updates. Across eight dynamics shifts in four continuous-control environments, JEPA-TTT improves planning on every shift. After 500 test-time episodes, it reduces autoregressive latent prediction error by 83% on average and improves planning performance by 153% over the frozen JEPA world model. These results show that persistent self-supervised test-time training can adapt a pretrained latent world model under changed dynamics.

</details>


### [103] [Learning Transferable Skills using Goal-Conditioned Bisimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00676)
*Mohammad Amin Abbasfar,Farbod Azimmohseni,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合功能等变时序表示与双模拟的无监督技能发现方法，使技能能够迁移到未见过的环境布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督技能发现方法往往依赖专家数据，或难以泛化到未见布局。核心挑战是在保留环境局部时序结构的同时，学习对布局变化稳健的表示。

Method: 设计动作感知的时序表示学习目标，使表示满足功能等变性并保留局部时序结构；在此基础上，引入基于双模拟的无监督技能发现方法，使技能行为仅依赖直接影响其执行的状态特征，从而减少对布局差异的敏感性。

Result: 实验表明，在某一环境中学习的技能能够用于解决多种环境布局中的下游任务，并表现出较强的分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法通过学习布局不变、动作相关的时序表示与技能，提升了无监督技能向不同环境配置迁移的能力。

Abstract: Unsupervised skill discovery has emerged as a promising approach for leveraging reward-free datasets to pretrain general-purpose policies. However, current skill discovery methods either require access to expert data or exhibit limited generalization, failing to transfer effectively to previously unseen layouts. A key challenge is to learn representations that capture the temporal structure of the environment while remaining robust to variations across layouts. To address this issue, we present an objective for learning action-aware temporal representations that satisfy the functional equivariance property while preserving the local temporal structure of the environment. Building upon this embedding, we further propose unsupervised skill discovery using bisimulation, which learns transferable skills by conditioning the behavior of skills exclusively on the subset of state features that directly affect their execution. This enforces invariant behavior across different layouts, enabling skills to transfer effectively to other configurations. Finally, through comprehensive empirical evaluations, we show that skills learned in a given environment can be effectively applied to solve downstream tasks in various environment layouts, demonstrating strong out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [104] [MIKASA-Robo-VLA: Benchmarking Memory in VLA Models for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00604)
*Egor Cherepanov,Nikita Kachaev,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIKASA-Robo-VLA 是一个评测视觉—语言—动作策略记忆能力的基准，包含 90 个语言条件操作任务，其中 80 个需要记住已消失的线索，另 10 个为线索持续可见的反应式对照任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略通常只接收最近少量帧，难以评估其能否利用任务过程中不再可见的信息。该基准旨在系统检验策略对这类历史线索的保持与使用能力。

Method: 基准重建了 MIKASA-Robo 的 32 项任务，并为每项任务提供指令；记忆依赖任务会隐藏关键线索，对照任务则保持线索可见。70 项任务给出环境阶段时间，用于标定信息缺失区间；其中 28 项的区间超过所调查固定上下文 VLA 中最长的 16 帧窗口。作者还发布了覆盖 10 类记忆的 22,500 条 oracle 轨迹，提供 RLDS 与 LeRobotDataset v3 格式。

Result: 使用当前图像和本体感觉、但不含观测历史或显式记忆模块的 π₀.₅ 基线，在 14 项任务上微调后，平均任务成功率为 0.211 ± 0.044。其在 Long-split 任务上的较低表现，受到开环动作分块及该子集中记忆类型分布的混杂影响，不能单独归因于记忆能力不足。

Conclusion: 该基准通过明确隐藏任务线索，提供了针对策略记忆能力的系统评测资源。时间标注的缺失区间只是线索可证实缺失的时段，并非策略需要保留线索的完整时长；因此，所有记忆依赖任务按构造均需要记忆，即使测得的缺失区间较短。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies often see only one or a few recent frames, which makes it difficult to evaluate how they use information that disappears during a task. We introduce MIKASA-Robo-VLA, a benchmark of 90 language-conditioned manipulation tasks. All but 10 hide the cue an action depends on. Those 10 are reactive controls. MIKASA-Robo, the suite it rebuilds, has 32 tasks and uses language only in a representative VLA subset. Here every task provides an instruction, while memory-dependent tasks hide a task-relevant cue and reactive controls keep it available. For 70 tasks, environment phase timings specify an information gap, and for 28 of them the gap exceeds the 16-frame window of the widest fixed-context VLA we survey. The gap counts only the interval the cue is provably absent, not the full duration a policy must retain it, so every memory-dependent task still requires memory by construction, including the ones whose measured gap is short. We release 22,500 oracle trajectories across 10 memory types in RLDS and LeRobotDataset v3. A reference $\pi_{0.5}$ baseline with current images and proprioception, but no observation history or explicit memory module, is fine-tuned on 14 tasks and achieves 0.211 $\pm$ 0.044 mean task success. Its lower success on the evaluated Long-split tasks is confounded by open-loop chunking and the memory types represented in that subset. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [105] [ALER: Adaptive Learnable Experience Rewriting for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00592)
*Oleg Shchendrigin,Egor Cherepanov,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 ALER 与 Rune-Mazes，研究部分可观测强化学习中记忆内容的改写和融合，并报告其在多种基准任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆架构与基准主要考察信息保留，却较少评估后续观测如何使旧信息失效，或按特定规则改变旧信息的含义。论文将这两种能力分别形式化为改写与经验融合，并关注完成相关任务所需的记忆状态数量。

Method: 提出 ALER（Adaptive Learnable Experience Rewriting），结合 LSTM 与槽式记忆：通过独立寻址的 Gumbel-Softmax 写入，将权重集中于单个槽以覆盖其内容；再用学习门控融合检索内容与循环状态，供策略头和价值头使用。论文还提出 Rune-Mazes，包含三类环境；符文观测会对隐藏线索执行反转、抵消、重置或重复更新，并支持向量与像素观测。

Result: 在 16 种 Endless T-Maze 配置中，ALER 的成功率均不低于 0.82；在 5 种 Rune T-Maze 组合中均不低于 0.99。在带反转符文的四分支 Rune Multi-Corridor 中，ALER 的平均成功率最高；在像素版 Rune MiniGrid Memory 的 10 种配置中，有 8 种高于 PPO-LSTM。论文还发现，若干基线在需要更多记忆状态的任务组合上成功率最低。

Conclusion: 研究表明，部分可观测强化学习中的记忆评估应涵盖保留以外的改写与融合能力。ALER 在多种此类任务上取得较强表现，但结果仅覆盖论文所述基准与配置。

Abstract: In partially observable reinforcement learning (RL), a later observation can make stored information obsolete or change what it implies for the next decision. Memory architectures and benchmarks for RL mostly test retention, the ability to keep information unchanged until it is needed. We formalize two further requirements. Rewriting sets the decision-relevant content to a value independent of the old one, and experience fusion transforms the old content by a rule that a later observation specifies. For tasks built from such updates, we count the memory states that a solution needs, and several baselines reach their lowest success rates on compositions that need more states. We introduce ALER (Adaptive Learnable Experience Rewriting), an agent that pairs an LSTM with a slot memory. An independently addressed Gumbel-Softmax write that concentrates its weight on one slot overwrites that slot, and a learned gate fuses the retrieved content with the recurrent state before the policy and value heads. We also introduce Rune-Mazes, three environments in which rune observations invert, cancel, reset, or repeat updates of a hidden cue under vector and pixel observations. Against seven baselines, ALER reaches a success rate of at least $0.82$ in all sixteen Endless T-Maze configurations and at least $0.99$ on all five Rune T-Maze compositions, and it has the highest mean success rate on four-branch Rune Multi-Corridor with an Invert rune. On pixel-based Rune MiniGrid Memory, it has a higher mean success rate than PPO-LSTM in eight of ten configurations. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [106] [T2SPO: Trajectory-to-Step Policy Optimization for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00388)
*Bo-Wen Zhang,Junwei He,Maoqi Liu,Feiran Li,Song-Lin Lv,Wentao Ma,Rongyi Lin,Shuhan Zhong,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: T2SPO 利用成功交互轨迹估计各状态到成功的剩余距离，并将距离变化转化为逐步反馈；在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多交互任务只在最终结果处提供奖励，难以指明哪些中间决策推动了任务进展。成功轨迹中的中间状态可为后续策略学习提供更细粒度的监督。

Method: 从成功轨迹提取状态表示及其剩余距离目标，结合新轨迹中的状态，由预训练 TabPFN 回归器估计各状态的剩余距离。相邻状态的估计值变化形成辅助步骤奖励，并与任务级监督共同训练策略；新完成的轨迹用于更新回归器的上下文，而不更新其参数。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上，使用 1.5B 与 7B 语言模型的实验表明，T2SPO 均较 GRPO 提高了整体任务成功率。

Conclusion: 利用历史成功轨迹构造逐步进度反馈，是缓解稀疏终局奖励的一种有效方法；T2SPO 无需更新辅助回归器参数即可持续纳入新经验。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning enables large language model (LLM) agents to learn multi-step behaviors through interaction with their environments. However, rewards in many interactive tasks reflect only the final outcome, providing limited guidance on which intermediate decisions advance the task. Successful training trajectories contain intermediate states that can provide supervision for subsequent interactions. We introduce Trajectory-to-Step Policy Optimization (T2SPO), a method that uses past interaction trajectories to provide step-level feedback for policy learning. T2SPO derives remaining-distance targets from successful trajectories and pairs them with representations of the states visited along the way. Conditioned on these examples, a pretrained TabPFN regressor estimates the remaining distance to success at each state of a new rollout. Changes in this distance estimate across consecutive states yield auxiliary credit for agent steps alongside task-level supervision. As training proceeds, newly completed trajectories refresh the estimator's context, incorporating new experience without updating its parameters. Experiments with 1.5B and 7B language models on ALFWorld and WebShop show that T2SPO consistently improves overall task success over GRPO.

</details>


### [107] [Nous: Learning and Certifying Memory Decisions Before Source Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00094)
*Pranav Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在证据噪声、复制或过时的情况下，智能体记忆是否必须先校准信息源才能改进决策。结果表明，学习并验证有用决策所需的数据量可能显著低于精确估计信息源可靠性所需的数据量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统通常依赖对信息源可靠性的校准，但源校准可能成本高昂，且现实中缺少纯净的类别锚点。本文旨在刻画决策学习、改进认证与源校准之间的样本复杂度差异，并建立适用于不完美报告渠道的有限样本认证方法。

Method: 在四模型隐马尔可夫族中分别分析未知贝叶斯决策的学习、相对已有决策的改进认证及源估计的样本复杂度；进一步研究未知的恒等加背景报告渠道，推导策略改进的精确识别区间，并利用可观测见证区域构造有限样本证书。方法还扩展到有界历史依赖错配和条件复制情形，采用拆分训练的见证区域，并与Nous Dimensions集成，在MiniGrid记忆环境中进行测试。

Result: 在持久性参数趋近于零时，学习未知贝叶斯决策和认证其相对信息性基线的改进均需Theta(l^-2)量级记录，而固定精度源估计需Theta(l^-4)，前两者在样本量上可呈二次优势。实验使用45,000条留出可变状态历史和9,000个回合：新证书在MiniGrid中接受了9/9个相对常数基线的改进，以及4/9个相对最后写入优先策略的改进；早期证书均未接受。强基线推断方法仍具有竞争力，部分表现更好。

Conclusion: 记忆系统可以在不完全恢复信息源可靠性的情况下学习并统计认证有用的决策。本文提供的是关于何时能够学习和证明决策改进的统计理论与工具，而不是宣称一种普遍优于现有方法的记忆算法。

Abstract: Belief-based agent memory needs reliable decisions about current state, yet its evidence may be noisy, copied, or stale. Must a memory calibrate its sources before it can improve its decisions? We separate learning, calibration, and revision certification. On one four-model hidden Markov family, learning an unknown Bayes decision requires Theta(l^-2) records and certifying its improvement over an informative incumbent takes O(l^-2) fresh records from the same observation law, while fixed-precision source estimation requires Theta(l^-4) as persistence l vanishes. Thus learning and certifying useful decisions can require quadratically fewer records than source calibration. A broader model class retains the decision rate and source lower bound. Under an unknown identity-plus-background report channel, we characterize the sharp identified interval for policy improvement and derive a finite-sample certificate using observable witness regions, without pure-class anchors. A robustness extension tolerates bounded history-dependent misspecification and conditional copying; split-trained witnesses apply to arbitrary history spaces with explicit power conditions. We integrate policy-bound receipts with Nous Dimensions and test 45,000 held-out mutable-state histories and 9,000 episodes in three external MiniGrid memory environments with an introduced noisy-report interface. The new certificate accepts 9/9 improvements over a constant incumbent and 4/9 over last-write-wins, versus none for the earlier certificate in MiniGrid. Strong established inference baselines remain competitive or better. The result is a statistical account of when memory decisions can be learned and justified without recovering source reliability, not a universally superior memory algorithm.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Fewer Tokens, Better Action: GPT-6 Astra Robot Agents with 14% Higher Success Rate but 65% Fewer Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01939)
*Ruiyang Si,Jianxin Bi,Shunyu Yang,Rui Ni,Wenbo Huang,Qiang Wang,Shulong Jiang,Duomin Wang,Xiuyu Li,Haiwen Feng,Zhen Dong,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PyRUA-Lean是一种面向机器人控制的交互式代码执行框架，通过反馈驱动的动作组合、条件检查、本地重试和选择性观测，减少视觉语言模型调用及冗余视觉输入。在700个仿真任务中，相较同等模型与基础工具调用方法，任务成功率由63.1%提升至71.7%，共同成功任务的模型调用次数减少49%，输入Token减少65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型机器人代理通常需要频繁调用模型并重复获取观测，导致较高的推理延迟和Token成本；同时，单步工具调用难以支持复杂动作流程中的条件判断与局部恢复。该研究旨在降低模型交互开销，并提升机器人任务执行的可靠性与效率。

Method: PyRUA-Lean采用交互式Python代码执行机制，使代理能够将经典机器人动作原语与视觉语言动作策略组合成代码单元。代码单元支持条件检查、局部重试和反馈驱动的流程控制；代理仅请求重新规划所需的图像和状态信息，从而实现选择性观测。实验在LIBERO-PRO、RoboTwin 2.0和RoboCasa365的700个仿真任务上进行，并与使用相同GPT-6 Astra规划器及底层机器人原语的工具调用基线比较。

Result: 在相同大语言模型调用预算下，PyRUA-Lean将总体任务成功率从63.1%提高至71.7%，绝对提升8.6个百分点。在两个方法均成功完成的任务中，PyRUA-Lean减少49%的大语言模型调用次数，并减少65%的输入Token，表明其在任务可靠性和推理成本方面均有改进。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将动作组合、局部恢复和选择性观测整合到可执行代码框架中，可以有效减少机器人代理对大语言模型的依赖，并提升任务成功率。该方法的主要贡献在于优化交互策略和信息传递效率，但摘要未说明真实机器人环境中的表现、额外代码执行开销及不同任务类型下的细粒度收益。

Abstract: Vision language model (VLM) agents can control robots through visual feedback and action primitives, but repeated model invocations and redundant observations incur substantial token overhead. We introduce PyRUA-Lean, an interactive code-execution framework that couples feedback-driven primitive composition with selective observation: the agent composes classical robot primitives and learned vision-language-action (VLA) policies into Python cells that perform conditional checks and local retries, returning only explicitly requested images and state feedback for replanning. Across 700 simulated task instances from LIBERO-PRO, RoboTwin 2.0, and RoboCasa365, we compare PyRUA-Lean with a tool-calling baseline using the same GPT-6 Astra planner and underlying robot primitives. Under equal LLM-call budgets, PyRUA-Lean increases overall success from 63.1% to 71.7%. On instances solved by both agents, it uses 49% fewer LLM calls and 65% fewer input tokens.

</details>


### [109] [MapLightning: Online Vectorized HD Map Construction with 1D Map Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01905)
*Shen Zheng,Anurag Ghosh,Mani Ramanagopal,Srinivasa Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MapLightning 以紧凑的可学习地图 token 替代密集 BEV 网格，实现更高效、准确且对相机外参扰动更稳健的在线矢量地图构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常依赖密集 BEV 中间表示，带来较高的计算与内存开销，并且依赖相机投影参数。研究旨在提升地图推理的精度、实时性和鲁棒性。

Method: 从图像特征构建地图 token，并将地图 token 与图像 token 拼接后输入 Transformer，通过完整自注意力进行联合交互与上下文聚合；随后保留更新后的地图 token 进行解码。轻量表示还支持解码器使用完整交叉注意力，而非可变形交叉注意力。

Result: 在 nuScenes 和 Argoverse 2 上达到先进的精度与效率，最多比密集 BEV 方法减少 16.7 倍中间 token。轻量版本相较 MapTRv2 在两个数据集上分别提升 10.1 和 16.2 mAP，推理速度提升 1.73 倍（超过 40 FPS），内存使用减少 53%。此外，方法改善了不确定性感知地图构建和下游轨迹预测表现。

Conclusion: 紧凑地图 token 与联合自注意力为在线矢量地图构建提供了高效方案，在降低资源消耗的同时提升精度、推理速度及对相机外参变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Online vectorized HD map construction is essential for scaling safe autonomous driving and requires accurate, real-time inference. Prior methods typically rely on dense bird's-eye-view (BEV) grids as the intermediate representation. We propose \textit{MapLightning}, which replaces the dense BEV grid with a compact set of 1D learnable map tokens. To construct map tokens from image features, we choose self-attention over vanilla cross-attention because it enables joint interactions and contextual aggregation among image and map tokens. Our transformer-based mapper concatenates map and image tokens, applies full self-attention, discards the image tokens, and retains the updated map tokens for decoding. This design offers three advantages. First, our representation is efficient, using fewer tokens, consuming less memory, and running faster. Second, the lightweight design allows the map decoder to use full rather than deformable cross-attention for better global context. Third, unlike BEV-based methods, our network does not use camera projection parameters, making it robust to camera-extrinsic perturbations. MapLightning uses up to 16.7$\times$ fewer intermediate tokens than dense BEV-based methods and achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and efficiency on nuScenes and Argoverse~2. Its lightweight variant surpasses MapTRv2 by +10.1 mAP on nuScenes and +16.2 mAP on Argoverse~2, while delivering 1.73$\times$ faster inference (40+ FPS) with 53\% less memory. We further show improvements on uncertainty-aware map construction and downstream trajectory prediction. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [110] [LiteReality-Agent: An Agentic System for Interactable 3D Indoor Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01863)
*Zhening Huang,Yueyan Li,Johnathan Chiu,Xiaoyang Lyu,Matt Zhou,Yuxin Yao,Joan Lasenby,Shangzhe Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiteReality-Agent 将室内 RGB-D 重建建模为代码编写任务，通过专用工具与迭代验证生成可用于仿真和具身 AI 的 3D 场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境的数字孪生需要兼顾几何精度、视觉真实性与仿真兼容性；现有前沿模型在重建质量和可靠性方面仍有不足。

Method: 系统采用“观察—编辑—验证”流程：智能体收集证据、进行测量并优化布局，再迭代编辑可执行的 Python 脚本以生成 3D 场景，同时通过结构化工作流和质量控制机制检查结果。

Result: 论文称，LiteReality-Agent 相较于 Astra、Fable 等近期前沿模型，生成的重建在几何准确性、视觉真实性和仿真兼容性方面更优。

Conclusion: LiteReality-Agent 为稳健的真实到仿真系统提供了实用的编排框架，并可随智能体能力提升而扩展；其源代码和数据采集应用已公开。

Abstract: We present LiteReality-Agent, an agentic system for reconstructing real indoor environments as realistic, articulated, and simulation-ready 3D scenes from RGB-D scans. At its core, LiteReality-Agent formulates 3D reconstruction as a coding problem, in which a coding agent gathers evidence using specialised tools and iteratively edits a Python script, this http URL , which can be executed to produce a 3D digital twin of the room. With this formulation, we develop a robust observe-edit-verify harness that supports evidence gathering, measurement, verification, layout optimisation, simulation readiness, and quality control throughout the reconstruction process. LiteReality-Agent produces high-quality reconstructions suitable for simulation and downstream embodied AI tasks. Furthermore, as agent capabilities continue to improve rapidly, the system introduced by LiteReality-Agent remains a strong orchestration framework for future agents: it equips them with specialised tools, structured workflows, and robust verification mechanisms that substantially improve reconstruction quality and reliability. We demonstrate that LiteReality-Agent produces reconstructions that are more geometrically accurate, visually realistic, and simulation-compatible than those generated by recent frontier models, such as Astra and Fable. We therefore view LiteReality-Agent as a practical and important building block for robust real-to-sim systems. Both the source code and the data-capture application are publicly available. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [111] [Continuous Conditioning of VLAs with Augmenting EMG and Visual Task Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01794)
*Edward W. Staley,Connor O. Pyles,Rahul Hingorani,Frank Camargo,Griffin Milsap,Jared Markowitz,Matthew S. Fifer,Michael Wolmetz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究表明，VLA模型除语言外还可利用肌电信号或视觉分割标注进行任务条件化，并在杂乱、分布外场景中显著提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型较依赖语言描述任务；在场景杂乱或含糊时，其他模态可能提供有价值的补充任务信息。

Method: 提出并测试两种调优模型：EC-VLA将8通道肌电包络作为连续条件输入，与本体感觉向量拼接；VA-VLA将视觉分割标注加入图像输入。两者均在三名参与者的立方体选择任务中，与语言提示基线比较。

Result: 在清晰的分布内场景中，EC-VLA表现与基线相当，VA-VLA略有提升；在杂乱的分布外场景中，两种模型均显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 实验支持将语言以外的模态用于VLA任务条件化，尤其有望改善模型在杂乱、分布外场景中的表现。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models rely strongly on language for describing task information, despite having multimodal inputs. We hypothesize that other modalities in the state space may present opportunities for supplemental task conditioning, which may be particularly relevant in cluttered or otherwise ambiguous scenes. We introduce two tuned models to test this hypothesis: (1) an electrophysiology-conditioned VLA (EC-VLA) that incorporates 8-channel electromyography envelopes as continuous conditioning input concatenated to the proprioceptive vector, and (2) a visually-annotated VLA (VA-VLA) that incorporates visual segmentation annotations to the image inputs. On a cube-selection task evaluated across three participants, EC-VLA matches a language-prompted baseline in uncluttered, in-distribution conditions and substantially outperforms it in cluttered, out-of-distribution scenes. Similarly, VA-VLA shows modest improvements over a language-prompted baseline in in-distribution scenes with substantial improvement in cluttered, out-of-distribution trials. Together, these results provide strong evidence for the potential benefit of task-conditioning beyond language.

</details>


### [112] [GenCOPE: Syn2Real Generalized Category-Level Object Pose Estimation for Robotic Picking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01758)
*Jian Liu,Wei Sun,Zhenqi Dai,Hui Yang,Jian Xiao,Nicu Sebe,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅使用合成数据训练、可直接迁移至真实场景的类别级物体位姿估计方法，兼顾泛化能力与轻量高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类别级物体位姿估计方法面对新类别时，仍依赖耗时的真实数据采集与标注；合成数据训练虽可降低成本，却受到合成与真实图像间显著域差异的影响，尤其是纹理差异。

Method: 通过二维和三维语义一致性约束，促使特征编码器关注同类别物体的语义共性、降低对域特定特征的敏感性；构建端到端位姿回归框架，利用稠密跨模态融合进行二维—三维交叉一致性学习以优化位姿估计。模型仅使用全局特征，以保持轻量高效。

Result: 在REAL275、Wild6D基准以及真实机器人操作场景中的实验表明，该方法取得了较强的合成到真实泛化性能。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了仅依靠合成数据实现类别级物体位姿估计真实场景部署的可行性，并通过语义一致性学习与轻量化设计提升了泛化能力和实用性。

Abstract: Category-level object pose estimation (COPE), capable of generalizing to intra-class unknown objects, has become a core technique for robotic 3D scene understanding. However, existing COPE methods still require labor-intensive recollection of real-world training data for novel object categories, which limits their scalability in practical applications. This paper aims to achieve synthetic-to-real (Syn2Real) generalized COPE, where a model is trained solely on rendered synthetic data and directly generalized to real-world deployments. The central challenge lies in the significant domain gap between synthetic and real-world data, particularly in texture appearance. To address this, we aim to enhance domain generalization by learning domain-invariant representations that capture semantic commonalities among objects within the same category. We introduce 2D and 3D semantic consistency constraints to reduce the sensitivity of feature encoders to domain-specific features. In addition, we propose an end-to-end pose regression framework that performs 2D-3D cross consistency learning, leveraging dense cross-modality fusion to further refine pose estimation. Since simplicity and effectiveness are essential for real-world robotic deployment, our model operates exclusively on global features, yielding a highly lightweight and efficient architecture. Extensive experiments on the REAL275 and Wild6D benchmarks, as well as real-world robotic manipulation scenes, show superior Syn2Real generalization performance of our paradigm. Code and demos are released at this https URL .

</details>


### [113] [FFBL-Coop: Association-Decoupled Cooperative 3D Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01750)
*Haoxin Wu,Xiaokai Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FFBL-Coop提出“先融合、后绑定”的协同三维跟踪框架，分离特征融合与身份管理，在两个数据集上取得较强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同跟踪需整合跨智能体、跨时间的互补观测并保持身份一致；若特征融合和身份继承共用匹配决策，跨视角外观差异与空间错位可能同时损害融合效果和轨迹连续性。

Method: 框架先通过置信度排序的槽位分配（CSA），依据置信度和空间邻近性将协同查询分配至自车槽位；再以统一表示聚合（URA）利用协同语义特征和对齐锚点检索自车特征，并在共享 Transformer 解码器中细化增强查询；最后由协同优先身份锚定（CPIA）结合学习式关联与持久映射，确定跨帧身份。共享码本用于压缩传输数据。

Result: FFBL-Coop在V2X-Seq上的AMOTA/AP为0.611/0.548，在Griffin-25M上为0.688/0.653。共享码本压缩传输负载后，性能仍接近未压缩版本。

Conclusion: 将实例接纳、特征融合与身份绑定分阶段处理，有助于降低错误匹配对融合和轨迹连续性的连带影响；该方法在两个数据集上展现出较强效果，并兼顾传输效率。

Abstract: Cooperative 3D tracking must integrate complementary observations across agents and time while maintaining consistent identities. When evidence integration and identity inheritance share a matching decision, errors arising from cross-view appearance differences and spatial misalignment can compromise both feature fusion and track continuity. We propose FFBL-Coop, a fuse first, bind later framework that separates instance admission from identity management. Confidence-ranked Slot Admission (CSA) allocates cooperative queries to available ego slots using confidence and spatial proximity. Unified Representation Aggregation (URA) uses cooperative semantic features and aligned anchors to guide ego-feature retrieval, refining the augmented query bank within a shared transformer decoder. After refinement, Cooperative-Priority Identity Anchoring (CPIA) combines learned association with persistent mappings to establish accepted identity assignments across frames. A shared codebook reduces transmitted payload while retaining AP and AMOTA close to the uncompressed variant. FFBL-Coop achieves AMOTA/AP of 0.611/0.548 on V2X-Seq and 0.688/0.653 on Griffin-25M. Code will be released.

</details>


### [114] [3DROID: A Renderable 3D Gaussian Dataset with Measured Per-Scene Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01744)
*Wonguen Cho,Junhoo Lee,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究如何将 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）场景与机器人工作空间的真实度量尺度对齐，并构建带场景级可靠性信息的可渲染数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作模型通常依据二维观测进行推理，但实际任务发生在三维空间。尽管深度、点云和三维轨迹等几何先验可弥合这一差距，3DGS 主要针对光度保真度设计，且在相机外参不可靠时可能无法保留真实度量尺度。

Method: 研究外参可靠性与位姿条件对前馈式 3DGS 的影响，并提出具备标定感知能力的处理流程，将重建场景锚定到机器人的度量工作空间；据此构建包含场景级可靠性信息的数据集。

Result: 实验表明，位姿条件可提升新视角保真度；其几何方面的收益则取决于注入外参的可靠性。

Conclusion: 将 3DGS 场景锚定到机器人度量工作空间，并显式记录外参可靠性，可为机器人操作研究提供兼顾可渲染性与度量信息的数据资源。

Abstract: Robot manipulation models primarily reason from 2D observations while acting in the 3D physical world. To bridge this gap, recent work has augmented robot data with geometric priors such as depth, point clouds, and 3D trajectories, while renderable 3D Gaussian representations provide another promising form of 3D supervision. However, 3DGS representation is designed mainly for photometric fidelity and may not preserve real-world metric scale, particularly when the supplied camera extrinsics are unreliable. We study the effect of extrinsic reliability and pose conditioning on feed-forward 3DGS, and propose a calibration-aware pipeline that anchors reconstructed scenes to the robot's metric workspace. Our experiments show that pose conditioning improves novel-view fidelity, while its geometric benefit depends on the reliability of the injected extrinsics. Using this pipeline, we present a renderable, metric-pose-anchored dataset with scene-level reliability information for robot manipulation research. Our dataset is available at this https URL

</details>


### [115] [ATI-VLA: Action-Centric Predictive Vision-Language-Action Models via Actionable Alignment Then Adaptive Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01741)
*Yijie Zhu,Rui Shao,Jie He,Wei Li,Bo Zhao,Yelin Wang,Xiaochen Yuan,Tao Tan,Miao Zhang,Xiaojiang Peng,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ATI-VLA，通过对齐预测观测与动作表征，并将预测潜变量自适应注入动作解码，提升机器人操作性能与收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测式视觉—语言—动作模型常不及直接动作预测模型，原因在于观测与动作模态不对齐，以及联合优化偏离以动作为中心的目标。

Method: ATI-VLA 分两步实现：首先用统一共享码本将预测观测和动作映射至同一离散潜在空间；随后通过轻量级自适应旁路，将预测观测潜变量作为显式先验注入动作解码，并以单一动作中心目标进行优化。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人任务上的实验显示，ATI-VLA 达到当前先进性能，并收敛更快。

Conclusion: 将预测表征与动作表征对齐，再以自适应方式利用预测潜变量，可缓解模态错位和优化冲突，增强预测信息对动作生成的实际作用。

Abstract: Predictive Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models aim to improve robotic manipulation via future observation or world dynamics forecasting. However, existing approaches often fail to realize this potential and underperform direct action prediction models. We argue that these limitations stem from modality misalignment between observations and actions, together with joint optimization conflicts that drive learning away from an action-centric objective. To this end, we introduce ATI-VLA, an Action-Centric Predictive Vision-Language-Action framework via Actionable Alignment Then Adaptive Injection. Specifically, it follows a two-step design: 1) Actionable Representation Alignment via a Shared Codebook. It aligns predictive observation and action representations by mapping both modalities into a shared discrete latent space via a unified codebook, making predictive observation latents readily usable for action generation and mitigating modality misalignment. 2) Action-Centric Adaptive Injection of Predictive Latents. Building upon this, it then injects predictive observation latents into action decoding as explicit predictive priors via a lightweight adaptive side-path, enabling adaptive predictive guidance under a single action-centric objective. Extensive experiments on both simulation and real-world robotic tasks demonstrate that ATI-VLA achieves state-of-the-art performance with faster convergence.

</details>


### [116] [FedCKA: Representation-Guided Layer Personalization for Federated 3D Perception Across Driving Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01510)
*Jolle Verhoog,Ali Burak Ünal,Holger Caesar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FedCKA通过按层评估本地模型与全局模型的特征相似性，动态决定哪些模型层参与共享，提升了跨环境联邦3D目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能车辆的3D目标检测会受到时间、地点和天气变化造成的域偏移影响；部分环境数据稀缺，难以独立训练可靠模型。传统联邦学习依赖单一全局模型，难以适应客户端数据异质性，而固定的个性化层划分也限制了灵活适配。

Method: 提出FedCKA策略，在训练过程中使用中心核对齐（CKA）计算各客户端本地模型与全局模型的逐层特征相似度，并据此生成客户端专属的聚合掩码，只共享表征一致的层，以动态平衡个性化与全局协作。

Result: 在基于nuScenes构建的统一多域基准上，FedCKA优于FedBN、FedRep和FedSelect等联邦学习基线；相较于表现最强的基线，平均NDS提升7个百分点。

Conclusion: FedCKA为应对地点、天气和光照变化下的联邦3D感知提供了更灵活的个性化方案，实验结果显示其具有提升跨域检测鲁棒性的潜力。

Abstract: Robust perception in intelligent vehicles demands 3D object detectors that remain dependable under domain shifts, such as changes in time of day, location, or weather. However, due to costly annotation and rare shifts, some environments lack sufficient data to train a standalone detector. Federated learning offers a privacy-preserving framework for collaborative model training, enabling clients to benefit from shared learning across diverse environments. Yet, this framework traditionally relies on a single global consensus model, which struggles to perform across heterogeneous local data distributions. Local conditions are better captured by adapting a subset of the model, but many personalization approaches rely on predefined layer partitions or fixed personalization ratios, thereby limiting adaptation to client-specific divergence. To reduce this rigidity, we propose FedCKA, a Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA)-based strategy that dynamically handles the personalization-globalization trade-off. Specifically, FedCKA computes layer-wise feature similarities between local client models and the global consensus model during training. By converting layer-wise similarity scores into client-specific aggregation masks, FedCKA selectively shares representation-consistent layers. Evaluation on a unified multi-domain benchmark based on nuScenes shows that FedCKA outperforms established federated baselines, including FedBN, FedRep, and FedSelect, improving average NDS by 7 percentage points over the strongest baseline. The findings offer both a comparative benchmark and a promising direction for robust federated 3D perception across shifts in location, weather, and illumination. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [117] [Resolving Mixed Single-Photon LiDAR Returns for Foreground-View and Hidden Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01206)
*Ziting Wen,Runrong Deng,Zili Zhang,Haitao Zheng,Yuecong Xu,Xiaoqiang Ren,Guodong Shi,Kemi Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向部分透射遮挡场景的单光子 LiDAR 分层重建方法，可同时恢复前景遮挡物与其后的隐藏场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遮挡物会使 LiDAR 收到前景与后方场景的混合回波。传统峰值方法容易丢弃较弱的隐藏回波；而仅拟合波形的方法可能把重叠回波误解为偏移表面或厚密度分布，因此波形拟合准确不一定意味着几何恢复正确。

Method: 逐射线估计局部回波证据，并将射线划分为无可靠表面证据、单回波或双回波状态。利用状态为双头神经场分配监督：所有射线用于波形重建，可靠锚点用于几何定位。此外，构建包含固定姿态下遮挡与无遮挡采集数据的真实配对单光子 LiDAR 数据集。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在隐藏场景深度和点云精度方面优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于回波状态区分与几何锚定的分层重建，有助于从部分透射遮挡物后的单光子 LiDAR 直方图中恢复隐藏场景，为穿透遮挡物的三维感知提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Partially transmissive screens and protective covers are common in robotic inspection, but they create mixed LiDAR returns from both the foreground material and the scene behind it. Conventional peak-based LiDAR usually discards weak hidden returns, while single-photon LiDAR records time-resolved histograms that preserve attenuated and overlapping echoes. However, existing transient reconstruction methods typically fit a single scene representation to the measured waveform. Under occlusion, weak or nearby foreground--hidden echoes can form a broad peak or subtle shoulder. Because such waveforms can also be explained by a displaced single surface or a thick density distribution, accurate transient fitting does not necessarily imply correct geometry. We propose a state-aware framework for foreground-view and hidden scene reconstruction from occluded single-photon histograms. For each ray, we estimate local echo evidence, identifying no reliable surface evidence, single-return evidence, or two returns. The inferred echo state routes supervision for a two-head neural field: all rays constrain waveform reconstruction, while reliable anchors provide geometry localization. We also introduce a real paired single-photon LiDAR occlusion dataset with occluded and clean captures at fixed poses. Experiments on a real dataset show improved hidden scene depth and point-cloud accuracy over baselines. Our results demonstrate single-photon layered reconstruction as a practical route for 3D perception through partially transmissive occluders.

</details>


### [118] [PhysicsLENS: Diagnosing Physical Property Blindness in Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01162)
*Isaiah Milkey,Som Sagar,Aditya Taparia,Xinyuan Liu,Jiqing Wen,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysicsLENS 是一项面向机器人物理属性评估的视频生成基准；结果显示，视频看起来合理并不代表其遵循了指定物理规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成基准主要评估可从画面观察的物理表现，难以检验重量、黏度、摩擦等隐藏属性是否准确，因此可能高估模型对机器人学习与规划的可靠性。

Method: 构建 PhysicsLENS 数据集与基准，采用条件帧和任务相同、但场景描述中的物理属性不同的配对情景。数据来自公开机器人视频，并覆盖碰撞、重力、动量、摩擦、形变、流体和因果关系七个领域；研究评估了四种视频生成模型，并获得超过 400 条人工标注。

Result: 在 47 个案例中，有 34 个生成视频虽外观合理，却未遵循指定物理属性。明确写出物理属性仅略微降低视频的合理性，且差异不显著。

Conclusion: 仅凭视觉逼真度不足以判断视频世界模型是否准确模拟物理。PhysicsLENS 通过控制条件并改变隐藏物理属性，为评估模型的物理可信度提供了更有针对性的基准。

Abstract: Reliable video world models could provide scalable predictive environments for robot learning, planning, and evaluation. However, generated robot videos can violate physical principles and complete tasks through physically implausible behavior, limiting their reliability for robot learning and planning. Current video-generation benchmarks exclude physics that are inherently hidden by visuals (e.g., weight, viscosity, friction). Due to this, video models are evaluated on the fidelity of physics, not the underlying accuracy of physics. We introduce PhysicsLENS, a dataset and benchmark for evaluating plausibility of physical properties grounded in robotics. PhysicsLENS uses matched scenario pairs that hold the same conditioning frame and task, while varying underlying physics in the scene description. Scenarios are curated from public robot video sources and annotated across seven physical domains: collision, gravity, momentum, friction, deformation, fluid, and causality. We evaluate across four video generation models, producing over 400 human-annotated labels. Results show that plausible-looking videos often ignore the stated property (34 of 47), and that stating the property lowers plausibility only slightly and not significantly.

</details>


### [119] [Ego2Act: Evaluating Goal-Directed Manipulation in Egocentric Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01092)
*Patrick Amadeus Irawan,Iskandar Muda Rizky Parlambang,Rava Maulana,Qinrong Cui,Erland Hilman Fuadi,Zayd M. K. Zuhri,Nanda Ryaas Absar,Ahmed Elshabrawy,Wilfried Ariel Mulyawan,Shoubin Yu,Yue Zhang,Mohit Bansal,Alham Fikri Aji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ego2Act 是用于评估视频生成模型能否通过多步、目标导向的物体操作生成合理第一人称视频的基准，并配套提供无需参考视频的评估流程 Ego2ActJudge。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多关注单个短动作或逐步指令，较少评估模型为实现高层目标而进行多步物理推理、规划及连续状态模拟的能力。

Method: 构建包含 110 项日常真实任务、共 2,640 段视频的 Ego2Act，覆盖不同物体杂乱程度与多步复杂度。输入初始场景图像和高层目标，评估模型生成手部操作物体的第一人称视频。另提出 Ego2ActJudge，以无参考流程评估任务完成度和物理合理性，并与人工判断及相关基线比较。

Result: 实验显示，Ego2ActJudge 在任务完成度和物理合理性评估上与人类共识的吻合度优于相关基线。生成模型常跳过或未完整执行操作步骤，导致后续步骤缺少必要状态，最终无法完成目标；在复杂物体操作和持续世界状态建模方面，对细粒度物理动态的处理也存在明显不足。

Conclusion: Ego2Act 为评估视频生成模型的多步目标导向模拟能力提供了系统测试平台，也揭示了模型在动作完整性、物理动态和状态持续性方面的局限。

Abstract: Video generation models are increasingly being explored as world simulators for embodied planning and learning. To do so effectively, these models must not only generate visually appealing frames, but also predict how environments dynamically evolve when executing goal-directed actions. While evaluating these capabilities is crucial, existing benchmarks focus mainly on single short actions or step-by-step instructions. This leaves multi-step physical reasoning underexplored, especially in egocentric video generation that requires planning to simulate proper execution to accomplish high-level goals by carrying out multiple real-world manipulations. We introduce Ego2Act, a goal-directed benchmark featuring 2,640 videos from 110 real-world tasks across day-to-day settings, varying object clutter and multi-step complexity. Given an initial scene image and a high-level goal, Ego2Act evaluates whether video generation models can produce realistic egocentric videos of a hand manipulating objects to carry out the task. To support scalable evaluation, we also introduce Ego2ActJudge, a reference-free evaluation pipeline that achieves better task completion and physics plausibility evaluation alignment with human consensus compared to relevant baselines. Our findings reveal that models' generated simulations often skip or partially execute steps, leaving later steps missing dependent states, which leads to unfulfilled goal. Furthermore, models consistently fail at fine-grained physical dynamics, particularly during complex object manipulation and persistent world modeling. We hope Ego2Act provides a rigorous testbed for advancing video models toward physically plausible, goal-directed simulation.

</details>


### [120] [FutureWorlds: Learning Robotic World Models from Alternative Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01019)
*Hao Wu,Shengju Qian,Weiyan Wang,Fan Xu,Fan Zhang,Yuanpeng He,Qingsong Wen,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FutureWorlds通过多样化未来候选生成、候选专属记忆和相对质量优化，提升机器人世界模型的长时预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以从替代预测中获得有效学习信号：候选过于相似时难以比较质量，轨迹分化后又难以持续维护各自的历史状态。

Method: FutureWorlds基于多模态离散自回归模型，在强化学习中采用多样化束搜索生成兼顾置信度与差异性的未来候选，并用有界的候选专属记忆保存场景状态，确保生成与策略评分使用一致历史。其优化算法 MemSPO 将视频轨迹奖励转化为组内相对优势，以更新世界模型。

Result: 在 RT-1、BridgeV2 和 RoboCasa 上，32 帧预测的 LPIPS 相较各数据集最强基线分别降低 14.78%、20.84% 和 9.12%。固定评测配置下，仅进行 200 次 MemSPO 更新即可进一步改善生成质量，并支持超出训练时域的持续预测。记忆消融、解码敏感性分析及光流评估也显示，改进涉及运动预测和物体状态一致性，而不只是视觉质量。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将多样化候选搜索、历史记忆与基于相对奖励的策略优化整合起来，可有效提升机器人世界模型的预测质量、运动准确性及长时预测能力。

Abstract: Robotic world models predict action-conditioned future scenes, providing a foundation for understanding action outcomes. However, turning alternative predictions into useful learning signals remains challenging: similar candidates limit informative quality comparisons, while diverging trajectories require persistent maintenance of their individual histories. We introduce FutureWorlds, a framework that unifies candidate construction, history maintenance, and learning from relative quality. Built on a multimodal discrete autoregressive model, FutureWorlds uses diverse beam search during reinforcement learning to construct candidate futures that balance confidence and diversity. Candidate-specific bounded memory preserves scene states and ensures that generation and policy scoring use matching histories. We further propose MemSPO (Memory-Conditioned Search-Guided Policy Optimization), which converts video trajectory rewards into group-relative advantages to optimize the world model. On RT-1, BridgeV2, and RoboCasa, FutureWorlds reduces LPIPS for 32-frame predictions by 14.78%, 20.84%, and 9.12%, respectively, relative to the strongest baseline on each dataset. Under fixed evaluation configurations, only 200 MemSPO updates further improve generation quality and support continued prediction beyond the training horizon. Memory ablations, decoding sensitivity analysis, and optical-flow evaluation show that these gains extend beyond visual quality to more accurate motion prediction and more consistent object states. Project page and code: this https URL .

</details>


### [121] [CtrlWAM: Controllable World Action Models with Aligned Intent and Foresight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00859)
*Chensheng Peng,Wenhao Ding,Ran Tian,Zewei Zhou,Jef Packer,Maximilian Igl,Peter Karkus,Yan Wang,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Yuxiao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CtrlWAM通过在模拟器中执行扰动动作并生成对应视觉结果，提升世界动作模型的动作预测、视频一致性与可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规训练同时扰动记录动作和视频，但扰动动作对应的反事实未来与仍关联真实记录的视频不匹配；低噪声视频还可能泄露场景布局及动态信息，削弱动作与视觉预测的协调性。

Method: 在模拟器中执行扰动动作，并将其产生的视觉结果与动作配对训练；设计视频—动作错位噪声调度，使视觉布局能随动作预测变化；将控制接口扩展为可变数量的智能体动作流。

Result: 驾驶实验显示，模型的动作预测更准确、生成视频与动作更一致，并且更能遵循给定指令；机器人实验显示，运动保真度和可控性更强。对照实验支持非轨迹渲染有助于指令遵循和操作保真度。

Conclusion: 通过训练数据与动作条件相匹配，并协调视频和动作的去噪过程，CtrlWAM提升了世界动作模型对多智能体未来的建模与控制能力。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) jointly predict actions (intent) and visual future (foresight). Standard training adds noise to recorded actions and video simultaneously, but such training paradigms introduce a mismatch: perturbed actions imply counterfactual future visual, while the noised video remains tied to the GT recording. In low-noise regime, the scene geometry and even the dynamic behavior remain clearly visible from the noisy future frames despite the added noise. We present CtrlWAM, which executes perturbed actions in a simulator and pairs them with their noised visual consequences for joint WAM learning. To accommodate the different denoising requirements of video and actions, we introduce warped video--action noise schedules that aim to keep visual layout responsive as action predictions evolve. We further extend the action interface from ego-only control to a variable number of agent streams, allowing a unified model to represent predicted or commanded futures for multiple agents. Driving experiments show more accurate action forecasts, closer agreement between generated video and actions, and better following of supplied commands; robotics experiments show stronger motion fidelity and controllability. Matched controls support the benefit of off-path renders for command following and manipulation fidelity. Together, these findings contribute to a more controllable world action model. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [122] [Lang3DSeg: Annotation-Free Open-Vocabulary 3D Segmentation with Point Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00855)
*Cigdem Kokenoz,Amir Salarpour,Alkim Domeke,Christopher Salas,Pedram MohajerAnsari,Long Cheng,Mert D. Pesé,Bing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lang3DSeg 使用点云 Transformer 实现无需人工标注的户外 LiDAR 开放词汇语义分割，并在 nuScenes 和 SemanticKITTI 上取得同类方法最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督式 LiDAR 分割依赖封闭类别体系和昂贵的逐点标注；开放词汇方法虽可借助二维视觉语言模型降低标注成本，但通常依赖稀疏卷积，且二维到三维的标签投影容易产生深度歧义。

Method: 以点 Transformer 为骨干网络，从头训练且不使用几何预训练。针对投影噪声，采用显式类别优先级合成掩码，并在投影实例的深度分布出现首个间隙处截断，以减少将物体后方点误标为该物体类别的问题。

Result: 在 nuScenes 验证集上达到 52.8% mIoU，在 SemanticKITTI 上达到 41.4%，均为已发表无标注方法中的最高成绩。每次分割仅使用单帧 LiDAR；推理可实时运行，且无需调用视觉语言模型。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，点 Transformer 可有效用于户外 LiDAR 开放词汇分割；直接处理深度歧义有助于提升二维到三维标签投影质量，并支持高效的单帧推理。

Abstract: Accurate 3D semantic perception is critical for safe autonomous navigation. However, supervised LiDAR segmentation remains tied to closed taxonomies and to the cost of point-wise manual annotation. Open-vocabulary methods avoid that cost by projecting the output of 2D vision-language models onto LiDAR and distilling it into a 3D network. These methods rely almost exclusively on voxel-based sparse convolutions, and point transformers have so far been limited to indoor environments, where 3D data is dense and bounded. We present Lang3DSeg, which establishes a point transformer as the backbone for annotation-free open-vocabulary segmentation of outdoor 3D LiDAR, and is trained from scratch without geometric pre-training. This training paradigm necessitates addressing the inherent noise in 2D-to-3D label projections; specifically, naive projection often suffers from depth ambiguity, where points behind an object are erroneously assigned its semantic label. We therefore composite masks using an explicit class-priority rule and truncate each projected instance at the first gap in its depth distribution, correcting the projection error directly rather than averaging it over registered sequences. Lang3DSeg achieves 52.8% mIoU on nuScenes validation and 41.4% on SemanticKITTI, the highest among published annotation-free methods on both benchmarks. Every 3D semantic segmentation is on a single LiDAR sweep, and inference operates in real-time without running vision-language models.

</details>


### [123] [EgoRefine: Ego-Referenced Predictive Alignment and Trajectory-Conditioned Reliability-Aware Fusion for Asynchronous Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00319)
*Lingzhao Kong,Yongsheng Zang,Yu Kang,Kailun Yang,Jie Fu,Yukun Zuo,Zhiyong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoRefine通过自车视角引导异步协作特征对齐，并依据对齐可靠性自适应融合，在两个数据集上优于TraF-Align。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步通信造成协作特征延迟。现有方法主要依据发送端历史进行预测，仍可能与自车当前观测错位；融合时也常忽略不同空间位置的对齐质量差异。

Method: 提出EgoRefine框架，包含两个模块：自车参考预测对齐模块利用当前自车特征引导协作轨迹场预测，并沿自车参考轨迹方向细化采样偏移；轨迹条件可靠性感知融合模块将自车与协作流的轨迹差异及方向细化幅度作为对齐线索，据此调整对齐特征间的关系并自适应重加权，随后进行卷积融合。

Result: 在V2V4Real和DAIR-V2X-Seq上，EgoRefine相较TraF-Align的平均AP@0.5和AP@0.7分别提升1.6和2.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，结合自车参考的预测对齐与可靠性感知融合，有助于提升异步协作感知中的3D目标检测性能。

Abstract: Collaborative perception enables connected agents to share complementary observations for 3D object detection, extending sensing range and mitigating occlusion. Under asynchronous communication, however, cooperative features arrive with temporal delay. Existing prediction-based methods compensate for these features mainly from the transmitting agent's own history, leaving residual misalignment with the ego agent's current observation; subsequent fusion also often overlooks spatial variations in alignment quality. We propose EgoRefine, an ego-referenced predictive alignment and reliability-aware fusion framework for asynchronous collaborative perception. Its Ego-referenced Predictive Alignment module uses the current ego feature to guide cooperative trajectory-field prediction and refines the sampling offsets along an ego-referenced trajectory direction. Its Trajectory-conditioned Reliability-aware Fusion module treats the trajectory discrepancy between the ego and cooperative streams and the directional refinement magnitude as alignment cues, using them to condition the relation between aligned features and adaptively reweight the two streams before convolutional fusion. Experiments on V2V4Real and DAIR-V2X-Seq show that EgoRefine outperforms TraF-Align by 1.6 and 2.9 points on average in AP@0.5 and AP@0.7, respectively. The source code will be made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [124] [Weather-Aware Domain Adaptation for Street-View Weather Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02000)
*Hossein Maghsoumi,George Atia,Yaser P. Fallah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WA-ADDA，将天气识别与领域适配结合，提升模型在真实街景中的跨域天气识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有天气识别训练数据多来自非街景，与自动驾驶中的真实道路图像存在明显域差异，导致模型在实际场景下表现受限。

Method: 提出天气感知对抗判别领域适配方法（WA-ADDA），利用预测天气条件化领域判别器，学习兼具领域不变性和天气敏感性的特征；同时整合多个非街景天气数据集与真实街景目标数据，建立基准及标准化评测协议，以宏平均准确率为主要指标。

Result: 在 ResNet-50、EfficientNet、VGG 和 DenseNet 等骨干网络上，WA-ADDA 均提升了街景天气识别表现；对困难天气类别取得较强召回率，同时保持晴朗天气识别准确性。

Conclusion: 领域适配有助于提升自动驾驶场景中的多类天气识别鲁棒性，所构建的基准也为相关方法的公平评估与后续研究提供了支持。

Abstract: Adverse conditions such as rain, snow, fog, and dust remain challenging for camera-based perception in autonomous driving. We study multi-class weather recognition from street-view images under domain shift, where most available training data come from non-street-view sources that differ markedly from real driving scenes. We propose Weather-Aware Adversarial Discriminative Domain Adaptation (WA-ADDA), which conditions the domain discriminator on predicted weather to promote features that are both domain-invariant and weather-sensitive. We also assemble a multi-dataset benchmark by unifying diverse non-street-view weather collections as sources and real street-view images as targets, and define a standardized evaluation protocol with macro accuracy as the primary metric. Across backbones (ResNet-50, EfficientNet, VGG, DenseNet), WA-ADDA consistently improves street-view performance and yields strong per-class recalls in challenging conditions while preserving clear-weather accuracy. These findings highlight the feasibility of domain-adapted weather recognition and the value of our benchmark for advancing robust, on-board perception.

</details>
